Without properly modeling agent heterogeneity, as in prior MARL work that leverages homogeneous graph networks, communication becomes less helpful and can even deteriorate the team's performance. In the past, we proposed Heterogeneous Policy Networks (HetNet) to learn efficient and diverse communication models for coordinating cooperative heterogeneous teams. In this extended work, we extend Heterogeneous Policy Networks (HetNet) to support scaling heterogeneous robot teams. Building on heterogeneous graph-attention networks, we show that HetNet not only facilitates learning heterogeneous collaborative policies but also enables end-to-end training for learning highly efficient binarized messaging. Our empirical evaluation shows that HetNet sets a new state of the art in learning coordination and communication strategies for heterogeneous multi-agent teams by achieving an 5.84% to 707.65% performance improvement over the next-best baseline across multiple domains while simultaneously achieving a 200x reduction in the required communication bandwidth.

</details>


### [59] [Duet: Dual-Robot Understanding via Efficient Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20990)
*Yiqi Zhao,Ruohai Ge,Celina Shiyu Wang,Junjie Ye,Muchen Xu,Minhao Li,Sergey Zakharov,Basile Van Hoorick,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Leonidas Guibas,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DUET是一个面向双机器人协作移动操作的框架，通过VR远程操作和人类协作演示数据收集，结合基于Action Chunking Transformer的预训练-微调范式，在双机器人协作任务上实现了优于纯机器人数据的性能，并将数据收集效率提升了5.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双机器人协作能完成单机器人无法实现的任务（如协同搬运、交接），但数据获取是训练这类系统的主要瓶颈。现有方法缺乏高效的数据收集手段，尤其是异构双机器人系统的数据采集困难。

Method: 1) 构建统一的双体VR远程操作系统，用于域内异构机器人数据收集；2) 开发互补的人-人协调跟踪流水线，记录人类协作先验知识；3) 基于Action Chunking Transformer的架构，先在人类演示数据上预训练协作策略，再在少量真实机器人轨迹上微调。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人和Dexmate Vega1移动操作器组成的四个协作任务基准测试中，利用人类先验的策略在任务性能上优于仅使用机器人数据训练的基线，同时将数据收集速度平均提升5.4倍，且在所有任务上表现不低于纯机器人数据策略。

Conclusion: 人类先验知识不仅能提升双机器人协作策略的任务性能，还能显著减少数据收集所需的人力投入，验证了预训练-微调范式在机器人协作学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Dual-robot collaboration enables tasks that exceed the reach and payload of a single robot, such as collaboratively transporting objects across environments and executing coordinated handovers. Data acquisition is the primary bottleneck for training these systems. To this end, we introduce DUET, a dual-robot learning framework for mobile manipulation. For efficient data collection, we create a unified dual-embodiment synchronized VR-based teleoperation system for in-domain heterogeneous robot data collection. We further develop a complementary tracking pipeline that records human-human coordination and collaborative mobile manipulation priors. To allow efficient learning, we introduce an Action Chunking Transformer based architecture that first pretrains collaborative policies on efficient human-human demonstrations, before finetuning them on a minimal set of real-robot teleoperation trajectories. We develop a benchmark of four collaborative tasks to evaluate our framework using a Unitree G1 humanoid and a Dexmate Vega1 mobile manipulator. The results demonstrate that harnessing human priors not only yields superior task performance compared to baselines trained only on robot data, but also reduces the total human effort required for data collection. Our human data collection pipeline achieves 5.4x acceleration on average from teleoperation, but we perform equally or better than robot-only data trained policies across all tasks. Our project page is available at https://zhaoy37.github.io/Duet/.

</details>


### [60] [Inductive Generalization for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20999)
*Annabella Macaluso,Haochen Zhang,Ishaan Masilamony,Yingshan Chang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“归纳泛化”（Inductive Generalization）的概念，指策略在逐步变难、分布外任务变体上的推理能力。作者建立了可复用的评估协议，发现现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型及主流策略尽管在传统域偏移（如光照、杂乱）上表现良好，但在归纳泛化测试中失败，揭示了一个与数据/模型扩展正交但亟待解决的学习挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方式仅通过插值测试（光照、杂乱、多样物体等非结构化域偏移）来衡量策略的泛化能力，无法真正反映策略的泛化极限。作者借鉴语言模型中基于轴的评估方法（如序列长度、计数），提出需要测试策略在逐步变难的分布外任务变体上的归纳能力，以暴露其固有局限性。

Method: 1. 提出“归纳泛化”的概念形式化定义；2. 设计可复用的评估协议，用于衡量任何操作策略的归纳泛化能力；3. 对现有主流范式（包括SoTA视觉-语言-动作模型）进行基准测试。

Result: 现有策略（包括VLA模型）在传统域偏移（如干扰物等）上表现出泛化能力，但在归纳泛化测试中全部失败。

Conclusion: 归纳泛化能力所涉及的学习挑战与数据/模型扩展正交，但若要实现通用机器人，这类能力不可或缺。

Abstract: Understanding the generalization capabilities of visuomotor policies is essential in the development of capable robotic agents. Generalizable models learn structures that transfer across domains. However, in practice, visuomotor policies test performance by interpolation on known distributions using unstructured domain shifts (e.g. lighting, clutter, diverse objects). We argue that to measure generalization capabilities we must instead test the inductive capacity of policies on progressively harder, out-of-distribution task variants. We call this inductive generalization, drawing directly on how axis-based evaluation has revealed inherent generalization limitations in language models (e.g. sequence length, counting) arXiv:2502.00197 . We provide a reusable and formal evaluation protocol for measuring inductive generalization in any manipulation policy, and establish baselines showing that existing paradigms fail this test; e.g. SoTA Vision-Language-Action models and find that policies that appear to generalize to prior domain shifts (distractors, etc) fail inductive generalization tests. These results expose a class of learning challenges orthogonal to those addressed by data and model scaling in robot learning, yet are imperative to solve in order to realize general purpose robots.

</details>


### [61] [R2HandoverSim: A Simulation Framework and Benchmark for Robot-to-Human Object Handovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21011)
*Hanxin Zhang,Abdulqader Dhafer,Hongbiao Dong,Zhou Daniel Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了R2HandoverSim，一个用于机器人向人类递送物体的仿真基准测试平台，包含标准化评估协议、四种基线方法比较、30人用户研究，并验证了仿真结果与真实世界评估的相关性，同时提出五个互补指标来更好反映用户感知的交接质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人向人类递送物体（R2H handover）的方法虽快速发展，但缺乏标准化评估协议，导致无法进行客观比较。

Method: 构建了R2HandoverSim仿真基准测试平台，系统比较四种基线方法的共享抓取姿态预测，进行了30名参与者的用户研究，并提出了五个互补评估指标：规划可行性、可达性、抓取稳定性、抓取适切性和安全性。

Result: 仿真结果与真实世界评估结果相关；五个互补指标相比单一成功率的评估方式更能反映用户感知的交接质量。

Conclusion: R2HandoverSim可作为标准化评估平台，促进机器人向人类递送物体方法的客观比较，且多维度指标对于评估用户实际体验至关重要。

Abstract: We present R2HandoverSim, a simulation benchmark for robot-to-human (R2H) object handovers. Although R2H handover methods have advanced rapidly, the lack of standardized evaluation protocols impedes objective comparison. Our benchmark enables reproducible evaluation by systematically comparing four baselines on their predicted shared grasp poses. We conduct a user study with 30 participants, analyze baseline performance, and show that simulation results correlate with real-world evaluation outcomes. Crucially, five complementary metrics (planning feasibility, reachability, grasp stability, grasp affordance, and safety) better reflect user-perceived handover quality than overall success rate alone. Website and code: https://robot-future.github.io/r2handoversim/.

</details>


### [62] [BayesFP: Posterior Estimation for Flow-Based Policies via Feynman-Kac Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21014)
*Sreevardhan Sirigiri,Weiming Zhi,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需重新训练即可在推理时对预训练的扩散模型和流匹配策略进行约束轨迹生成的方法，利用贝叶斯后验采样和Feynman-Kac校正框架，在多种模拟和真实世界的操作任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要生成既忠实于专家行为、又满足推理时指定的安全约束和任务特定目标的轨迹，现有方法往往需要重新训练，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 将约束轨迹生成形式化为贝叶斯后验采样，以学习到的示教分布为先验，以推理时成本推导的似然来倾斜分布；利用Feynman-Kac校正框架（原用于扩散模型）扩展至确定性流匹配策略，实现统一的推理时、无需重训练的采样器。

Result: 在预训练的Diffusion Policy、GR00T-N1.6和π₀.₅检查点上验证，涵盖模拟和真实世界的操作任务（包括推理时引入的非凸障碍物规划），并在零样本任务上优于基础π₀.₅。

Conclusion: 提出的方法无需重新训练即可在推理时对扩散和流策略施加约束，提升了轨迹的可行性和最优性，在零样本任务上表现出改进。

Abstract: Robots must generate trajectories that remain faithful to learned expert behavior while satisfying safety constraints and task-specific objectives specified only at inference time. We formulate constrained trajectory generation for pretrained diffusion and flow-matching policies as Bayesian posterior sampling, with the learned demonstration distribution as a prior and an inference-time, cost-derived likelihood tilting it toward feasible, optimal trajectories. To sample from this posterior without any retraining of the base policy, we leverage the Feynman--Kac corrector framework, originally formulated for diffusion models, and extend it to deterministic flow-matching policies. The result is a unified, inference-time, retraining-free sampler for diffusion and flow policies. We validate the approach on pretrained Diffusion Policy, GR00T-N1.6, and $π_{0.5}$ checkpoints across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, including planning around non-convex obstacles introduced at inference time, and show improvements over the base $π_{0.5}$ on zero-shot tasks.

</details>


### [63] [Membrane-based Acoustic Microrobots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21047)
*Fatih Kocabas,Cemal Polat Avdar,Prithvi Venkatesh,Yunus Alapan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于柔性PDMS膜密封微腔的声学微型机器人，解决了传统气泡型声学微机器人因气体溶解导致寿命短的问题。该设计通过物理阻隔气体扩散，实现了超过24小时的稳定微流推进，并可通过嵌入磁性微粒实现磁场定向控制，器件尺寸可缩小至~100微米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学微机器人依赖被困在微腔内的空气微泡产生声流，但气泡会快速溶解导致共振频率偏移，严重限制了器件的工作寿命。本文旨在解决这一核心瓶颈，提出一种膜基密封方案以延长声学微机器人的操作稳定性与耐久性。

Method: 在微腔上方键合一层薄柔性PDMS膜，物理阻隔气体扩散；通过声场激励PDMS膜振动产生微流；同时将磁性微粒嵌入结构体中，实现低强度（2 mT）外部磁场的定向控制。

Result: 膜基声学执行器在连续工作超过24小时后仍能保持稳定的流场推进和运动性能；成功实现了受控微游泳器运动；设计架构可缩小至~100微米尺度。

Conclusion: 膜基声学微机器人提供了一种高可靠、长寿命的声学微执行器开发框架，为执行长时间任务的靶向药物递送和微创医学应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Acoustic microrobots have emerged as a promising frontier for targeted drug delivery and minimally invasive medicine due to their high-power density and biocompatibility. Despite wide-ranging designs, conventional acoustic microrobots mostly rely on air microbubbles trapped within confined microcavities within the robot body, which suffer from limited operational longevity due to rapid gas dissolution and resultant shifts in resonance frequency. In this paper, we propose a robust, membrane-based acoustic microrobot that overcomes these limitations by employing a thin flexible Polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) membrane bonded over confined microcavities for microstreaming. The introduced design physically prevents gas diffusion, ensuring stable performance over extended periods at high actuation voltages. We systematically characterized the membrane-based acoustic actuator longevity, demonstrating consistent streaming and propulsion for over 24 hours of continuous operation. In addition, by embedding magnetic microparticles into the structural body, these actuators were successfully employed as microswimmers with directional control using low-intensity (2 mT) external magnetic fields. Finally, we demonstrate the scalability of the proposed design architecture down to ~100 um. This membrane-based approach establishes a reliable framework for the development of high-endurance acoustic microactuators and microrobots capable of performing long-term tasks.

</details>


### [64] [ReFPO: Reflow Regularization for Flow Matching Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21086)
*Ge Wang,Yibo Peng,Fan Feng,Shenhao Yan,Chengsi Yao,Jiahao Yang,Honghao Cai,Yiming Zhao,Xi Li,Jinke Ren,Shuguang Cui,Yatong Han,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReFPO 通过在 FPO 中引入显式的 Reflow 正则化，实现了稳定、高效的单步流生成控制，在保持计算开销不变的前提下，显著提升了策略性能与离散化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配策略梯度方法（FPO）在训练中存在 CFM 代理比率尖峰和不稳定的问题，限制了其在复杂物理模拟控制任务中的应用。作者试图通过几何视角揭示 FPO 梯度更新的隐含结构，并设计一种简洁有效的正则化方案来稳定训练、提升性能。

Method: ReFPO 首先理论揭示了 FPO 的梯度更新可解释为隐式的优势加权 Reflow 过程，然后在此基础上引入显式的几何正则化项。该正则化仅需单行代码修改即可实现，不增加额外计算开销或辅助蒸馏阶段，通过优势引导更新与路径校正的协同作用来抑制 CFM 代理比率尖峰、稳定 PPO 训练。

Result: 在 GridWorld、MuJoCo Playground 和高维 Humanoid Control 任务上，ReFPO 相比基线方法提升了平均性能，实现了高保真度的单步推理（通常匹配或超越多步性能），并表现出更好的离散化鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ReFPO 提供了一种可扩展且稳定的生成式策略方法，适用于复杂物理仿真中的控制任务，为流模型在强化学习中的应用提供了新的正则化视角和实用改进。

Abstract: We present Reflow-regularized Flow Matching Policy Gradients (ReFPO), a simple online RL method that adds explicit Reflow regularization to FPO for efficient flow-based control. We uncover a key structural property: the gradient updates in Flow Matching Policy Gradients (FPO) can be interpreted as an implicit advantage-weighted Reflow process, providing a new geometric perspective on flow-based policy gradients. Building on this insight, ReFPO introduces an explicit geometric regularizer that can be implemented with a single line of code change without incurring additional computational overhead or auxiliary distillation stages. By synergizing advantage-guided updates with path rectification, our method reduces CFM proxy-ratio spikes, stabilizes PPO-style training, and enables high-fidelity one-step inference that often matches or exceeds multi-step performance. We experimentally demonstrate that ReFPO improves average performance and discretization robustness across GridWorld, MuJoCo Playground, and high-dimensional Humanoid Control tasks, providing a scalable and stable approach for generative policies in complex physical simulations.

</details>


### [65] [MV-WAM: Manifold-Aware World Action Model with Value Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21088)
*Jintao Chen,Peidong Jia,Qingpo Wuwu,Jiaming Liu,Mengfei Du,Chun-Kai Fan,Xiaowei Chi,Hao Chen,Chengyu Bai,Zezhong Qian,Hao Wang,Jiajun Cao,Weishi Mi,Xiaozhu Ju,Jian Tang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MV-WAM提出了一种跨模态因果掩码、流形感知优化和价值进度调节机制，在无随机动作监督下实现55.7%仿真成功率和77.5%真实世界成功率，分别超越基线29.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在域内表现良好，但域外泛化能力不足，根本原因在于视觉与动作模态之间存在结构异质性，联合优化在分布偏移下会不成比例地损害动作鲁棒性。

Method: 提出MV-WAM端到端框架，联合建模视觉预测、动作生成和价值估计。核心创新包括：(1) 跨模态因果掩码，层次化地将动作锚定到预测视频帧和价值函数令牌；(2) 流形感知优化方案，显式处理模态间的结构异质性；(3) 进度价值调节机制，估计任务完成度并检测预测帧与生成动作间的失配，通过价值引导回滚实现自主恢复。

Result: 在RoboTwin仿真中，无随机动作监督下平均成功率55.7%，超越最强基线29.3%；在双臂机器人四类真实世界任务中平均成功率77.5%。

Conclusion: 流形感知的跨模态对齐是提升策略泛化鲁棒性的关键，为可部署的机器人操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Achieving robust and generalizable manipulation across diverse environments remains a fundamental challenge in embodied robotics. Recent world action models achieve strong in-domain performance, yet their gains do not extend proportionally to out-of-distribution scenarios. We attribute this to a structural mismatch between visual and action modalities, whose intrinsically heterogeneous manifolds cause joint optimization to disproportionately degrade action robustness under distribution shift. To address this, we propose MV-WAM, a novel end-to-end framework that jointly models visual prediction, action generation, and value estimation designed to effectively leverage video priors during both training and inference for enhanced action generalization. Key to this unification is a cross-modality causal mask that hierarchically grounds actions in predicted video frames and value function tokens in both modalities. To further narrow the generalization gap, MV-WAM adopts a manifold-aware optimization scheme that explicitly accounts for the structural heterogeneity across modalities. Finally, MV-WAM introduces a progress-value regulation mechanism that estimates task completion and detects misalignment between predicted frames and generated actions, enabling the policy to autonomously identify execution deviations and recover through value-guided rollback. On the RoboTwin simulation, MV-WAM achieves a 55.7% mean success rate on random scenarios without any randomized action supervision, outperforming the strongest baseline by 29.3%. MV-WAM achieves a 77.5% mean success rate across four real-world tasks of varying difficulty on a dual-arm robot. Our results demonstrate that manifold-aware cross-modal alignment is essential for robust policy generalization, offering a path toward deployable robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [66] [How Should a Robot Configure Its Laser Scanner for Inspection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21093)
*Zhiling Chen,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,Yang Zhang,Jiong Tang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SenseHD通过超维关联记忆将扫描仪配置建模为指令条件化的传感决策，在机器人检测中鲁棒地选择稳定传感参数，显著提升检测可靠性且计算轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人检测工作聚焦于运动与视角规划，但传感参数的配置方法尚未被充分探索，尽管其对测量质量具有决定性影响。

Method: 将传感参数配置形式化为指令条件化的传感决策，把参数视为离散传感动作，通过超维关联记忆选择稳定传感区间。

Result: 在真实机器人检测平台上，SenseHD能鲁棒地选择合适传感配置，显著提升检测可靠性，且相比基线方法更轻量高效。

Conclusion: SenseHD有效填补了机器人检测中传感参数配置的研究空白，通过超维计算实现了鲁棒且高效的传感器配置选择。

Abstract: Robotic inspection relies on accurate sensing to acquire high-fidelity geometric measurements for defect detection and metrology. While prior work has focused on robot motion and viewpoint planning, how to configure sensing parameters remains largely underexplored, despite their decisive impact on measurement quality. We propose SenseHD, a robotic sensing system that formulates scanner configuration as an instruction-conditioned sensing decision. Instead of predicting precise parameter values, SenseHD treats sensing parameters as discrete sensing actions and selects stable sensing regimes through hyperdimensional associative memory. Experiments on a real robotic inspection platform demonstrate that SenseHD robustly selects appropriate sensing configurations and significantly improves inspection reliability, while remaining lightweight and efficient compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [67] [Factor-Aware Mixture-of-Experts with Pretrained Encoder for Combinatorial Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21100)
*Feihong Zhang,Guojian Zhan,Zeyu He,Yinuo Wang,Likun Wang,Tianze Zhu,Yao Lyu,Tao Zhang,Tinghao Yi,Wei You,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAME是一种集成因子感知混合专家（MoE）与预训练编码器的框架，通过三阶段训练（策略预热、因子专用适配器训练、联合微调）实现视觉机器人操作在环境变化（如光照、表面纹理）下的泛化，在Meta-World基准上相比扩散策略基线提升34%，在真实拾放任务中提升35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练编码器与扩散策略的集成在视觉机器人操作中已占主导地位，但在光照、表面纹理等复杂环境变化下泛化能力仍然不足。

Method: 提出FAME框架，采用三阶段训练：（1）策略预热，在标准环境数据上训练扩散策略，冻结编码器；（2）因子专用适配器训练，在冻结编码器与暂时冻结策略之间插入轻量适配器，每个适配器针对特定环境变化训练；（3）联合微调，训练中央路由器和预热后的策略，在混合数据上处理多因子联合变化。中央路由器通过软加权冻结的因子专用适配器形成密集MoE，实现跨因子的组合泛化。

Result: 在Meta-World基准上相比扩散策略基线提升34%；在真实世界拾放任务中，使用新收集数据训练的紧凑模型实现了35%的泛化性能提升。

Conclusion: FAME通过因子感知的MoE结构有效提升了视觉机器人操作策略在环境变化下的泛化能力，验证了在模拟和真实场景中的有效性。

Abstract: The integration of pretrained encoders with diffusion policies has become a dominant paradigm for visual robotic manipulation. However, it still struggles to generalize across complex environments with varying factors such as lighting and surface textures. To address this, we propose FAME, a framework that integrates a factor-aware mixture-of-experts (MoE) with a pretrained encoder to enhance generalization to environmental variations. FAME follows a three-stage training process: (1) policy warmup, where a diffusion policy is trained on standard-environment data with a frozen encoder; (2) factor-specific adapter training, where lightweight adapters inserted between the frozen encoder and the temporarily frozen policy are trained on customized datasets, each targeting a distinct environmental variation; and (3) joint fine-tuning, where a central router and the warmed policy are trained on mixed data to handle multiple factors jointly. FAME is ``factor-aware'' because the central router softly weights frozen factor-specific adapters as a dense MoE, enabling combinatorial generalization across multiple factors. Evaluations on the Meta-World benchmark show that FAME outperforms diffusion policy baselines by 34%. We further validate FAME in a real-world pick-and-place task using a compact model trained on newly collected data, where FAME achieves a 35% improvement in generalization under real-world variations.

</details>


### [68] [PoLAR: Factorizing Extent and Mode in Latent Actions for Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21139)
*Youngjoon Jeong,Jihwan Yu,Minsoo Jo,Junha Chun,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PoLAR方法，通过径向结构对潜在动作空间施加约束，使半径编码变化程度、方向编码变化模式，并在双曲空间中实现。该方法在模拟和真实机器人实验中提升了下游策略性能，表明潜在动作空间的几何结构是视觉预训练迁移的重要设计因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作预训练方法将每个状态转移编码为单一非结构化表示，混淆了转移程度（extent）和转移模式（mode），导致表示能力受限。

Method: 提出PoLAR，在潜在动作空间中引入径向-方向结构：半径编码转移程度，方向编码转移模式；利用时间偏移作为转移程度的弱代理；在双曲空间中实例化该结构，利用其随半径扩大的体积容纳更多样化的转移模式。

Result: 在任务内和大规模预训练场景中，PoLAR在仿真和真实机器人实验中均优于潜在动作基线和强预训练VLA基线，显著提升下游策略性能。

Conclusion: 潜在动作空间的几何结构（径向-方向分解）是视觉预训练迁移到下游机器人策略学习的关键设计选择。

Abstract: Latent action pretraining learns representations of visual change from pairs of observations, but existing methods typically encode each transition as a single unstructured representation that entangles transition extent and transition mode. We introduce Polar Latent Actions with Radial structure (PoLAR), which imposes a radial-direction structure on latent actions, encouraging radius to encode transition extent and direction to retain transition mode. PoLAR uses temporal offset between two observations as a weak proxy for transition extent, encouraging latent action from observation pairs separated by larger temporal gaps to occupy larger radii. We instantiate this structure in hyperbolic space, whose expanding volume with radius offers a natural fit for more diverse transition modes at larger extents. Across in-task and large-scale pretraining settings, PoLAR improves downstream policy performance in simulation and real-world robot experiments, outperforming latent action baselines and strong pretrained VLAs. These results suggest that the geometry of the latent action space is an important design choice for transferring visual pretraining to downstream robot policy learning.

</details>


### [69] [Pose-Agnostic Robotic Functional Grasping via Observation-Action Canonicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21148)
*Le Qiu,Cole Harrison,Jiankai Sun,Yao Liu,Suning Huang,Qianzhong Chen,Yang You,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyMug提出了一种规范化视觉运动强化学习框架，通过观测-动作规范化将深度观测和预测末端执行器动作转换到共享的物体中心坐标系，使单一闭环策略能够零样本部署到真实机器人上完成杯柄抓取任务，在仿真中达到93%以上成功率，真实场景达到80%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能性机器人抓取需要策略在不同物体几何形状和姿态下泛化，同时保持任务特定的接触精度。杯柄抓取中细手柄、实例变化以及直立或倒置放置使得感知和控制对物体配置高度敏感，现有抓取姿态检测方法开环运行且对细手柄结构估计误差敏感，学习型视觉运动策略需隐式处理视觉外观和动作方向耦合变化，限制了泛化能力。

Method: AnyMug框架包含三个关键技术：(1) 观测-动作规范化，将深度观测和预测动作转换到物体中心坐标系，使策略在规范化视角下运行；(2) 手柄感知奖励函数，鼓励精确接近、夹爪对齐和对指放置；(3) 姿态课程学习与域随机化，提升训练稳定性和仿真到真实迁移效果。

Result: 仿真环境中，AnyMug在未见过的直立和倒置杯子上均达到93%以上成功率；零样本迁移到真实Franka Panda机器人上，在5个实物的两种姿态类别中达到80%成功率。

Conclusion: AnyMug通过规范化方法有效解耦了物体姿态变化对视觉观测和动作输出的影响，实现了从仿真到真实的零样本迁移，为功能性抓取中几何与姿态泛化问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Functional robotic grasping requires a policy that generalizes across diverse object geometries and poses while maintaining task-specific contact precision. We study this challenge through mug-handle grasping, where thin handles, instance variation, and upright or inverted placements make both perception and control sensitive to object configuration. Grasp pose detection methods operate open-loop and are sensitive to estimation errors on thin handle structures. Learned visuomotor policies must implicitly learn to handle the coupled variation in visual appearance and action direction induced by different object placements, limiting generalization. We propose AnyMug, a canonicalized visuomotor reinforcement learning framework for functional grasping that trains a single closed-loop policy entirely in simulation and deploys it zero-shot on a real robot. AnyMug introduces observation-action canonicalization, which transforms both the depth observation and the predicted end-effector action into a shared object-centric frame. The policy therefore sees a consistent mug-centered view and emits actions in a canonical direction regardless of mug placement, allowing the same grasping behavior to be reused across configurations. A handle-aware reward further encourages precise approach, gripper alignment, and opposing-finger placement, while a pose curriculum and domain randomization improve training stability and sim-to-real transfer. In simulation, AnyMug achieves over 93% success rate on both unseen upright and inverted mugs and transfers zero-shot to a real Franka Panda, reaching 80% success rate on 5 held-out physical mugs across both pose categories.

</details>


### [70] [OmniV2X: A Generative Foundation Planner for Efficient End-to-End Cooperative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21165)
*Juntong Peng,Juanwu Lu,Yupeng Zhou,Can Cui,Yaobin Chen,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniV2X是一种用于车联网（V2X）协同驾驶的生成式基础模型，它通过直接解释多模态、多智能体的独立上下文序列，结合端到端监督训练和生成式序列规划，在DAIR-V2X-Seq数据集上以不到10%的微调数据和不到1%的通信带宽实现了最先进的协同驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X方法存在三个主要问题：密集3D感知的计算成本高、协同场景下数据稀缺的脆弱性、以及多模态输入融合为标准共享表示时对标准化协议的合规性差。OmniV2X旨在缓解这些问题。

Method: 提出OmniV2X生成式基础模型，直接处理多模态和多智能体的独立上下文序列；使用端到端监督训练流程，以下游轨迹生成损失进行训练；通过交叉注意力注入将多模态输入融入高容量生成式序列规划器；预训练于大规模单智能体规划数据集，通过轻量级、符合标准V2X token适配协同环境。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X-Seq数据集上，OmniV2X超越了现有端到端协同驾驶基线方法，达到最先进性能；仅需不到10%的V2X微调数据集和不到1%的通信带宽。全面评估验证了其在真实约束下的计算效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OmniV2X作为一种生成式基础模型，有效解决了V2X协同驾驶中计算成本、数据稀缺和协议合规性等关键挑战，通过预训练-微调范式实现了高效迁移，为协同自动驾驶提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We present OmniV2X, a generative foundation model for vehicle-to-everything (V2X) cooperative driving. The model directly interprets independent context sequences comprising multi-modal and multi-agent observations. The new design mitigates the computational cost of dense 3D perception, the vulnerability to data scarcity in cooperative scenarios, and the poor compliance with standardized messaging in existing methods that fuse multi-modal inputs into a shared representation. For training, we present an end-to-end supervised pipeline using a downstream trajectory generation loss, in which a high-capacity generative sequence planner implicitly learns to steer the model and leverage multi-modal inputs via cross-attention injection. As a foundation model, we demonstrate that OmniV2X pre-trained on large-scale single-agent planning datasets can efficiently adapt to cooperative environments by integrating the conditioning context with lightweight, standard-compliant V2X tokens. Evaluated on the DAIR-V2X-Seq dataset, OmniV2X outperforms existing end-to-end cooperative driving baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance with less than 10% of the fine-tune V2X dataset and less than 1% of the communication bandwidth. We conduct comprehensive evaluations to demonstrate its computational efficiency and robustness under real-world constraints.

</details>


### [71] [Remember what you did?: Learning Behavioral Memories for Partially Observable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21188)
*Kuancheng Wang,Seungho Yeom,Jinglin Cao,Yuheng Zhi,Nikhil Shinde,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对长时域、接触式操作任务的部分可观测性问题，论文提出压缩动作记忆策略（CAMP），通过自监督学习机器人自身动作历史的压缩表示作为隐式行为记忆，在不依赖额外监督的情况下追踪任务进度并学习失败经验，在四个真实机器人平台和两个新仿真基准上显著超越现有方法，是首个纯粹通过学习记忆在接触式部分可观测操作任务上取得实质性成功的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域、接触式的机器人操作任务本质上是部分可观测的——单一的视觉观测无法捕获完整的动作上下文（如先前的尝试、交互或进度），导致标准视觉运动策略或视觉-语言-动作模型因缺乏记忆而难以胜任。

Method: 训练一个记忆模块来维护过去动作的压缩表示，迫使模型编码所有历史交互的潜在行为记忆，从而更好地为未来动作提供上下文。该方法基于一个关键洞察：机器人自身的动作历史是一种信息丰富且自监督的信号。

Result: 在四个真实机器人平台和两个新仿真基准（Memory-T-Bench、Memory-Manip-Bench）上，CAMP相比现有最先进基线方法取得了显著提升，是首个纯粹通过学习记忆在接触式部分可观测操作任务上成功的策略。

Conclusion: CAMP通过压缩动作记忆隐式地追踪任务进度并从失败尝试中学习，无需额外监督或外部干预，有效解决了长时域接触式操作中的部分可观测性问题。

Abstract: Long horizon, contact-rich manipulation is inherently partially observable. This is as a single visual observation rarely captures a robot's full action context, including prior attempts, interactions, or progress. Consequently, standard visuomotor policies or vision-language-action models are prone to struggle in such tasks due to a lack of memory. To address this, we introduce Compressed Action Memory Policy (CAMP) based on the insight that a robot's own action history serves as a highly informative, self-supervised signal, enabling the policy to learn a robust, compact history representation. In our approach, we train a memory module to maintain a compressed representation of past actions, forcing it to encode a latent behavioral memory of all the robot's past interactions that can then be used to better contextualize future actions. This allows our approach to implicitly track generalized task progress and learn from failed attempts without any additional supervision, or external oversight. We evaluate CAMP across four real-robot setups and two novel simulation benchmarks: Memory-T-Bench and Memory-Manip-Bench. By demonstrating substantial gains over state-of-the-art baselines, CAMP is, to our knowledge, the first policy to demonstrate substantial success on contact-rich partially observable manipulation tasks purely through learned memory.

</details>


### [72] [A scalar per patch from pre-trained ViTs enables fast moving navigation in the real world](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21216)
*Steeven Janny,Leonid Antsfeld,Christian Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过966次、总长24公里的真实世界导航实验，系统评估了视觉编码器在机器人导航中的关键因素，发现预训练视觉任务比机器人下游损失更重要，并提出多教师蒸馏、特征瓶颈化和特权信息微调等改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然认识到视觉编码器对机器人策略至关重要，且预训练视觉任务（而非仅机器人下游损失）是决定性因素，但缺乏大规模真实世界实验来系统评估哪些计算机视觉组件真正对机器人导航任务有效。

Method: 在真实建筑中执行966次静态点目标导航（共24公里），系统评估最先进视觉编码器；探索异构多教师蒸馏生成具有互补技能的多技能编码器；通过原则性空间瓶颈压缩编码器信息量，检验其对导航的必要性；对比RGB数据训练与基于特权信息微调策略的性能差异。

Result: 多教师蒸馏能产生具有多种互补技能的编码器；空间瓶颈压缩可导致与可供性相关的可解释特征涌现；仅使用RGB数据训练策略无法最佳利用视觉特征，基于特权信息微调可显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 本文全面揭示了计算机视觉中哪些方面对真实世界机器人导航真正重要，为视觉编码器设计和训练提供了系统性的指导结论。

Abstract: Trained policies for real-world robotics rely on computer vision components, typically in the form of pre-trained visual encoders. These encoders are an essential component and it has been shown that their power does not emerge from training on robotics downstream losses alone. Pre-training with auxiliary losses in the form of computer-vision pre-text tasks is a defining factor and heavily conditions agent performance in robotics tasks. In this unprecedented large-scale study, we ran 966 navigation episodes of static point goal navigation in a real-world building for 24km and asked which components really matter for the computer vision aspects of robotics: we evaluate state-of-the art visual encoders in realistic conditions. We explore the usefulness of heterogeneous multi-teacher distillation leading to encoders with multiple different and complementary skills. We investigate how much information from these encoders is necessary for robotics by bottlenecking them in a principled and spatially useful way and we show that this leads to the emergence of interpretable features linked to affordances. We also argue that training policies on RGB data alone does not lead to an optimal usage of visual features and show this by finetuning policies pre-trained on privileged information. All in all, we paint a more complete picture of what aspects of computer vision are relevant for real-world navigation.

</details>


### [73] [A UAV-Mounted Sensor Network for Close-Range Inspection of Wind Turbine Rotor Blades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21220)
*Jost Wittmann,Ahmed Abdullahi Hassan,Nils Kömpe,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种搭载于无人机上的多模态传感器网络，集成了工业RGB相机、被动热红外相机和自研3D扫描仪，通过联合标定实现几何、颜色和热数据的空间叠加，用于海上风力涡轮机叶片的高精度近距检测，初步实验室结果验证了多传感器同步采集和点云重建的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风力涡轮机叶片的检测对预测性维护至关重要，但现有方法受限于偏远位置、大结构尺寸以及当前无人机兼容传感器系统的局限性，且缺陷类型分类仍依赖人工且容易出错。

Method: 设计了一种无人机搭载的多模态传感器网络，包括工业RGB相机、被动热红外相机和自研3D扫描仪；所有传感器通过联合标定统一到公共坐标系，实现几何、颜色和热数据的空间叠加；系统设计用于近距操作，解决平台运动、大视场和毫米级测量精度三个基本传感挑战。

Result: 初步实验室结果展示了多传感器同步采集能力和初始点云重建效果，为未来的机载检测试验奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该多模态传感器系统有望提升海上风电叶片检测的自动化水平和缺陷分类可靠性，但仍需通过实际飞行试验进一步验证。

Abstract: Inspection of offshore wind turbine rotor blades is critical for predictive maintenance to maximise efficiency and extend operational lifetime. However, it remains a challenging task due to remote locations, large structural dimensions, and the limitations of current UAV-compatible sensor systems. While existing approaches can detect certain types of surface anomalies, reliable classification of defect types often remains a manual and error-prone process.
  This paper presents the design of a UAV-mounted multimodal sensor network combining an industrial RGB camera, a passive thermal infrared camera, and an in-house developed 3D scanner. All sensors are co-calibrated into a common coordinate frame, enabling spatial superimposition of geometric, colour, and thermal data. The system is designed to operate at close range, addressing three fundamental sensing challenges: platform motion, large field of view, and millimetre-level measurement accuracy. Preliminary laboratory results demonstrate synchronised multi-sensor acquisition and initial point cloud reconstructions, forming the basis for future airborne inspection trials.

</details>


### [74] [FleetAgent: Teleoperation Assistant for Autonomous Fleets via Vectorized V2N Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21222)
*Juntong Peng,Qi Chen,Deyuan Qu,Takayuki Shimizu,Yaobin Chen,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FleetAgent，一种基于多模态大语言模型的云端辅助系统，通过紧凑的向量化V2N消息（而非原始传感器数据）实现自动驾驶车队的高效远程监控。系统包含VecFormer向量嵌入接口和VecEval数据集，可将上行负载降低625倍，KV缓存减少16.54倍，并在Lingo-Judge评分和干预失败率上分别提升16.8%和降低19.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自动驾驶车队依赖远程操作处理罕见故障，但流式传输原始传感器数据成本高昂，且远程操作员同时监控的车辆数量有限，亟需一种高效、可扩展的远程监控方案。

Method: 提出FleetAgent系统，包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用紧凑的向量化V2N消息（地图元素、检测目标、自车规划路径）替代原始图像传输；(2) VecFormer：向量到嵌入的接口，通过可微分的top-K上下文选择机制限制上下文长度和GPU KV缓存增长；(3) VecEval数据集：基于nuScenes构建，包含人工和合成的不完美规划及人工验证的语言标签。

Result: 上行负载比原始图像降低625倍，KV缓存内存比原始文本描述降低16.54倍。在VecEval上，相比使用语言描述的Qwen2.5-VL-7B，Lingo-Judge评分提升16.8%，干预失败率降低19.9%。

Conclusion: FleetAgent证明紧凑的结构化V2N消息能够结合MLLM实现高效、可解释的车队远程监控，在带宽、计算效率和监控质量上均有显著优势。

Abstract: Large-scale autonomous fleets rely on teleoperation to resolve rare failures, yet streaming raw sensor data from many vehicles is costly, and remote operators can only monitor a limited number of vehicles at a time. We introduce FleetAgent, a cloud-hosted multimodal large language model (MLLM) assistant that consumes compact vectorized vehicle-to-network (V2N) messages, such as map elements, detected objects, and the ego planned path. It provides a structured natural-language response (including narration, explanation, and evaluation of the plan and scene), along with an intervention urgency score for operator prioritization. To make structured messages compatible with token-based MLLMs, we propose VecFormer, a vector-to-embedding interface with differentiable top-K context selection that bounds context length and GPU KV-cache growth, enabling more efficient batch processing, which is important under the context of cloud-hosted large-scale fleet management. We also construct VecEval, a nuScenes-derived dataset with paired human and synthetic imperfect plans and human-verified language labels, to facilitate the training and evaluation of our proposed system. Our proposed system can reduce uplink payload by up to 625 times compared with raw images and reduce KV-cache memory by 16.54 times compared with original text descriptions. On VecEval, FleetAgent improves Lingo-Judge score by 16.8% and reduces intervention failure rate by 19.9%, compared with Qwen2.5-VL-7B using language descriptions. These results demonstrate that FleetAgent can utilize compact structured V2N messaging to enable efficient, explainable teleoperation monitoring for autonomous fleets.

</details>


### [75] [Ultra-Fusion: A Resilient Tightly-Coupled Multi-Sensor Fusion SLAM Framework under Sensor Degradation and Spatiotemporal Perturbation for Intelligent Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21223)
*Yihong Tian,Junjie Zhang,Liuyang Li,Deteng Zhang,Yunfei Zuo,Jie Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Ultra-Fusion，一个基于统一滑窗估计器的紧耦合多传感器定位框架，支持WIO/VIO/LIO/LVIO等多种配置，通过可观测性感知初始化、因子级可靠性调度和在线标定，在多种退化条件下实现了高精度鲁棒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有紧耦合多传感器融合系统在理想条件下虽精度高，但在光照不足、LiDAR退化、车轮打滑、GNSS失效等传感器退化场景下，以及在时空标定误差存在时仍表现脆弱，导致城市峡谷、隧道、高速走廊等场景中定位漂移，影响路线跟踪、隧道通行和地图对齐。

Method: (1) 基于统一滑窗估计器的紧耦合多传感器融合框架，将异步测量按时间戳排序并转换为优化窗口内的可选因子，支持WIO/VIO/LIO/LVIO及可选轮速和GNSS增强；(2) 可观测性感知初始化选择引导模式；(3) 因子级可靠性调度门控退化测量；(4) 在线LiDAR-IMU时空标定，优化时间偏移和旋转外参。

Result: 在M3DGR、M2DGR-Plus、KAIST、GrandTour、MARS-LVIG等基准上对60余个开源SLAM系统进行评估，结果表明Ultra-Fusion在轮式、足式、空中平台上，在长时间高速运行、传感器退化和标定扰动条件下均具有竞争性精度，提升了道路级自主、校园/仓库移动和低空巡检的定位可用性。

Conclusion: Ultra-Fusion通过统一框架、可观测性感知初始化、退化感知因子调度和在线标定，显著提升了多传感器定位在退化环境下的鲁棒性和可用性，论文接收后将开源代码和数据集。

Abstract: Reliable localization is essential for intelligent transportation systems (ITS), including autonomous vehicles, quadruped last-mile carriers, and infrastructure-inspection unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). Although tightly-coupled multi-sensor fusion improves accuracy in favorable conditions, deployed systems remain vulnerable to sensor degradation -- poor illumination, LiDAR degeneracy, wheel slippage, and GNSS outage -- and to spatiotemporal calibration errors. These failures are common in urban canyons, tunnels, and high-speed corridors, where localization drift can degrade route tracking, tunnel passage continuity, and local map alignment. This paper presents Ultra-Fusion, a tightly-coupled multi-sensor localization framework based on a unified sliding-window estimator. Asynchronous measurements are timestamp-ordered and converted into optional factors within one optimization window, supporting WIO, VIO, LIO, and LVIO with optional wheel and GNSS augmentation. Observability-aware initialization selects the bootstrap mode, factor-wise reliability scheduling gates degraded measurements, and online LiDAR--IMU spatiotemporal calibration refines temporal offsets and rotational extrinsics during operation. We extend the M3DGR benchmark with simulation trajectories and evaluate more than 60 open-source SLAM systems on M3DGR, M2DGR-Plus, KAIST, GrandTour, and MARS-LVIG. The results show competitive accuracy across wheeled, legged, and aerial platforms under long-duration and high-speed operation, degradation, and calibration perturbation, improving localization availability for road-level autonomy, campus and warehouse mobility, and low-altitude aerial inspection. To benefit the industrial and academic community, we will release source code and datasets upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [76] [A Human-Inspired Thumb-Index Robotic Hand with Strain Gauges Embedded in Soft Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21245)
*Jonas Papenbrock,Shubhan Patni,Tomaso Lisini Baldi,Michele Guerri,Elia Landi,Ada Fort,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受人类手部抓取适应机制启发的STIR（安全拇指-食指机器人）手，这是一种低成本、轻量化的两指欠驱动原型。该手通过将腱驱动机械设计与嵌入柔性应变片的硅胶软关节相结合，实现了被动抓取适应，并建立了本体感知与外部感知。在20种物体的抓取数据集上进行无监督分析、物体分类和消融研究，结果表明其能有效区分物体尺寸、形状和材料刚度，且无需额外指尖触觉或外部视觉系统。与类似设备的对比验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类手部抓取适应中物理结构与生物反馈协同作用的启发，旨在开发一种低成本、轻量化且无需外部视觉或额外触觉传感器即可实现安全、自适应抓取的机器人手。

Method: 设计了一种不对称拇指-食指配置的两指欠驱动原型STIR手；采用腱驱动机电设计与嵌入柔性应变片的硅胶软关节；通过无监督分析、物体分类任务和消融研究对20种物体的抓取数据进行评估。

Result: 物体分类任务在区分物体尺寸、形状和材料刚度方面取得了高分类精度；消融研究验证了软关节传感器的贡献；与传统工业夹爪和机器人手相比，传感器化柔性关节显著提升了灵敏度并确保了抓取安全性。

Conclusion: STIR手证明了传感器化顺从关节能够在无需额外指尖触觉元件或外部视觉系统的情况下，显著提升整体灵敏度并实现安全抓取，与类似设备抓取相同物体的对比验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: Human hand grasp adaptation depends mainly on the synergy between physical structure and biological feedback. Inspired by this biomechanical principle, the Safe Thumb-Index Robotic (STIR) Hand was developed as a minimal, lightweight, and low-cost two-digit prototype featuring an asymmetric thumb-index configuration. By pairing an underactuated, tendon-driven mechanical design with flexible strain gauges embedded into silicone-encapsulated soft joints, the system achieves passive grasp adaptation while establishing both internal proprioception and external perception. Unsupervised analysis was carried out on a dataset of the STIR hand grasping 20 different objects, along with an object classification task and an ablation study to highlight the contribution of the soft joint sensors. The object classification task discriminated object size, shape, and material stiffness with a high classification accuracy. In contrast to traditional industrial grippers and robotic hands, the STIR Hand demonstrates that sensorized compliant joints significantly improve overall sensitivity and ensure safe grasping, while remaining independent of additional fingertip tactile elements or external vision systems. Finally, a comparison to similar devices grasping identical objects validates the utility of the STIR Hand.

</details>


### [77] [Spectral GS-SLAM: Observability-Aware, Degeneracy-Robust Tracking for Real-Time 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21258)
*Edward Beng Wai Tan,Siew-Kei Lam,Dongshuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Spectral GS-SLAM，通过频谱分解自适应融合ICP与特征约束，利用高斯协方差结构进行平面性加权，在退化场景中实现鲁棒且实时的跟踪（40.14 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-SLAM系统中，特征匹配在无纹理环境中易失效，ICP跟踪在无结构或几何退化场景中因病态优化而表现不佳，缺乏一种能同时应对两类退化场景的高效鲁棒跟踪框架。

Method: 1) 通过频谱分解识别ICP优化中的欠约束方向，自适应补偿退化场景中的数值不稳定性；2) 利用3D高斯协方差结构的内在几何特性，设计高斯感知的平面性加权机制，引导特征与ICP约束的信息融合。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D序列上实现40.14 FPS的实时性能，在无结构和无纹理的退化环境中保持一致的跟踪，轨迹完整性得到保留，非退化场景下性能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 频谱融合策略有效解决了ICP在退化场景中的病态优化问题，高斯协方差结构为场景几何表征提供了新视角，为3DGS-SLAM在复杂环境下的鲁棒跟踪提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent 3DGS-SLAM systems enable real-time operation by leveraging conventional feature matching or ICP-based tracking, thereby avoiding the heavy dense photometric optimization used in earlier approaches. However, feature matching remains prone to failure in textureless environments, while ICP-based tracking struggles in structureless or geometrically degenerate scenes due to ill-conditioned optimization. To address this issue, we propose Spectral GS-SLAM, an efficient yet robust tracking framework that integrates ICP with complementary feature-based constraints. Our method mitigates numerical instability by adaptively compensating under-constrained directions in degenerate scenarios, without interfering with the shared Gaussian representation used for mapping. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware planarity weighting mechanism that exploits the intrinsic covariance structure of 3D Gaussians to characterize scene geometry and guide information fusion. Extensive evaluations on challenging TUM RGB-D sequences demonstrate that Spectral GS-SLAM achieves real-time performance (40.14 FPS) while maintaining consistent tracking in both structureless and featureless environments. The proposed method preserves trajectory integrity in degenerate scenes while maintaining competitive performance in non-adverse conditions.

</details>


### [78] [RogueRover: Autonomous Rogue Device Localization for Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21264)
*Priyanka Prakash Surve,Asaf Shabtai,Yuval Elovici*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RogueRover系统，利用单台四足机器人自主巡逻，通过标准WiFi接口采集带空间标签的RSSI测量值，在无需RF指纹、预装传感器或场地标定的零配置约束下，实现对非法无线设备的物理定位。11次巡逻、62次AP-巡逻会话中，单次巡逻中位定位误差1.62米，多次聚合后5/6设备误差<1米，盲测中从73个BSSID中正确识别非法设备并定位至0.34米和1.84米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理定位非法无线设备（如恶意接入点）是网络物理安全的关键瓶颈。现有方法依赖密集传感基础设施、站点特定RF指纹或人工巡检，限制了及时的事件响应能力。研究探索单一通用机器人能否在零配置约束下自主完成定位任务，无需RF指纹、预装传感器或场地标定。

Method: RogueRover系统部署四足机器人按预设路径自主巡逻，通过标准802.11接口采集带空间标签的RSSI测量值，离线估计设备位置。采用加权质心估计器（WCE）和参数化路径损耗模型（PLM）两种定位算法，在真实室内环境（11次巡逻、6个非法设备、73个BSSID）进行多轮评估和盲测验证。

Result: 62次AP-巡逻会话中，单次巡逻中位定位误差1.62米（无先验RF知识）；多轮聚合后5/6设备误差<1米；盲测中从73个BSSID中正确识别非法设备，定位误差分别为0.34米和1.84米。简单加权质心估计器表现与参数化路径损耗模型相当或更优。

Conclusion: 在零配置约束下，利用单一机器人自主巡逻实现基础设施无关的非法设备物理定位是切实可行的。自主巡逻带来的测量覆盖率是定位精度的主要决定因素，而非传播模型的复杂度。该方案可实现无需额外传感基础设施的快速物理事件响应。

Abstract: Physically localizing unauthorized wireless devices remains a critical bottleneck in cyber-physical security operations, where rogue access points can provide entry points for lateral movement and persistent compromise. While such devices can often be detected through network-side mechanisms, determining their physical location typically requires dense sensing infrastructure, site-specific RF fingerprinting, or manual inspection, limiting timely incident response. We investigate whether a single commodity robot can autonomously detect and localize rogue wireless devices under zero-configuration constraints, without RF fingerprinting, pre-installed sensors, or site calibration. We present RogueRover, an end-to-end system in which a quadruped robot autonomously patrols, collects spatially labeled RSSI measurements via a standard 802.11 interface, and estimates device locations offline. We evaluate the system across 11 patrol runs in a real indoor environment, with 6 rogue devices deployed under heterogeneous propagation conditions. Across 62 AP-patrol sessions, RogueRover achieves a median single-patrol localization error of 1.62 m without prior RF knowledge. Under multi-run aggregation, five of six devices are localized within 1 m. A blind trial validates the full pipeline, correctly identifying rogue devices among 73 observed BSSIDs and localizing them with errors of 0.34 m and 1.84 m. Across environments, simple weighted-centroid estimators perform comparably to, or better than, parametric path-loss models, indicating that measurement coverage from autonomous patrols is the primary determinant of localization accuracy under zero-prior constraints. Our results demonstrate that infrastructure-free, autonomous localization is feasible in practice, enabling rapid physical incident response in cyber-physical environments without additional sensing infrastructure.

</details>


### [79] [NAC: Neural Action Codec for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21372)
*Ahad Jawaid,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出神经动作编解码器（NAC），借鉴神经音频编解码器架构，将机器人动作轨迹作为多通道一维信号进行多尺度RVQGAN压缩，通过替换音频特有的梅尔频谱损失为时域和非梅尔频谱重建损失，实现了高保真动作标记化，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中的离散动作分词器在压缩率、延迟和下游性能之间存在权衡，亟需一种更优的动作标记化设计。

Method: 借鉴神经音频编解码器架构，采用卷积编码器-解码器结构结合残差向量量化（RVQGAN），将短机器人动作轨迹视为多通道一维信号进行多尺度压缩，用时域和非梅尔频谱重建损失替代音频梅尔频谱损失，并通过偏移码本和Vocos风格解码器（含ISTFT头和对抗鉴别器）恢复轨迹。

Result: 在LIBERO-10、RoboMimic及真实操作任务上，NAC在同等或更优压缩率下，重建误差更低、任务成功率高于分箱、FAST及先前VQ方法。

Conclusion: 改造后的神经音频编解码器为现代VLA模型中的学习型动作标记化提供了强大且实用的基础架构。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models rely on discrete action tokenizers to bridge continuous robot control and autoregressive sequence modeling, yet existing tokenizers often trade off between compression, latency, and downstream performance. We revisit this design through the lens of neural audio codecs-convolutional encoder-decoder architectures with residual vector quantization that serve as the standard front end for audio foundation models. Motivated by their success, we introduce the Neural Action Codec (NAC), which treats short robot action trajectories as multi-channel 1D signals and compresses them using a multi-scale RVQGAN architecture. We observe that audio-specific mel-spectrogram objectives are ill-suited for kinematic signals; however, by replacing them with simple time-domain and non-mel spectral reconstruction losses, audio-codec-style models can autoencode actions with high fidelity without substantial architectural changes. NAC provides a compact, ordered token space via offset codebooks, enabling standard autoregressive policies to operate over short, structured sequences. Meanwhile, a Vocos-style decoder with an ISTFT head and adversarial discriminators recovers smooth, detailed trajectories. Across LIBERO-10, RoboMimic, and a suite of real-world manipulation tasks, NAC achieves lower reconstruction error and higher success rates than binning, FAST, and prior VQ-based tokenizers at comparable or better compression rates. These results demonstrate that repurposed neural audio codecs offer a strong, practical backbone for learned action tokenization in modern VLAs.

</details>


### [80] [Long-Distance Real-World Navigation of the Legged-Wheeled Robot Go2-W Using Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21387)
*Takaaki Matsuzawa,Kiyoshi Irie,Tomoaki Yoshida,Taro Suzuki,Yoshitaka Hara,Masahiro Tomono*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对四轮腿机器人Go2-W，构建了基于深度强化学习的运动控制器和自主导航系统，解决了轮式运动导致髋关节过热的问题，并在筑波挑战赛2025中实现了约2.8公里无中断自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮腿机器人兼具轮式高效平地和腿部越障能力，但长距离自主导航的实际应用案例有限，且轮式运动会导致髋关节过热，阻碍持续行进。

Method: 将此前四足机器人的本体感觉策略扩展至16自由度轮腿机器人Go2-W，并通过负载分配策略抑制髋关节过热；同时构建自主导航系统。

Result: 在筑波挑战赛2025中，系统自主穿越约2.8公里路线（含人行道、公园、楼梯），未因过热而中断。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于DRL的运动控制器和导航系统有效解决了轮式运动过热问题，实现了轮腿机器人在真实环境中的长距离自主导航。

Abstract: Legged-wheeled robots have long been studied for their potential to combine the efficient flat-ground mobility of wheels with the rough-terrain capability of legs. However, examples of their application to long-range autonomous navigation in real environments remain limited. This paper reports our effort to build a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) based locomotion controller and an autonomous navigation system for the commercially available legged-wheeled robot Go2-W, and to apply them to long-range autonomous navigation in a real environment. For locomotion control, we extended a proprioception-only policy, which we had previously developed for quadruped robots, to the 16-DoF legged-wheeled robot. We also found that wheeled locomotion concentrates the load on the hip joints and causes heat concentration that hinders sustained travel, and obtained a policy that suppresses it by distributing the load. We evaluated the system at the Tsukuba Challenge 2025, demonstrating that it can autonomously traverse an approximately 2.8 km route including sidewalks, a park, and stairs without stopping due to overheating.

</details>


### [81] [Durability-Aware Multi-Objective Optimization of the Jansen Linkage: Trading Gait Quality Against Joint Wear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22129)
*Jichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对Jansen平面腿机构，在原有仅优化足端轨迹的基础上引入耐久性目标，通过耦合正运动学、逆动力学和Archard磨损模型，建立了双目标优化框架（步态质量 vs 关节总磨损），利用NSGA-II求解。实验表明，在经典Jansen设计已被帕累托支配，通过调整连杆长度（±29%内）可同时改善步态（支撑相平坦度-28%，速度平滑度-58%）并减少总关节磨损约56%，且该优势对曲柄转速-负载包络具有鲁棒性（48%-56%）。全局灵敏度分析识别出两个主导磨损方差的关键连杆长度，蒙特卡洛制造公差仿真显示磨损优势在合理加工误差下性能退化平缓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jansen连杆机构的"11个神圣数字"仅通过进化算法优化了足端轨迹，未考虑转动副的磨损问题。针对这一空白，本文希望在保持良好步态质量的同时，引入耐久性（关节磨损）目标，实现Jansen腿的多目标设计优化。

Method: 1) 建立参数化正运动学模型（两圆交点求解器）；2) 建立逆动力学模型（约束雅可比/拉格朗日乘子法求解七体十铰链系统，并用降维能量法独立交叉验证）；3) 耦合Archard磨损模型计算每个销轴每周期滑动磨损；4) 构建双目标优化问题（复合步态误差 vs 总关节磨损，约束包括步长、离地间隙、占空比和装配条件），采用NSGA-II求解；5) 进行灵敏度分析（Sobol全局方差分析）和蒙特卡洛制造公差分析。

Result: 经典Jansen设计在双目标下被帕累托支配；代表性优化设计在±29%连杆长度调整内，支撑相平坦度降低28%，速度平滑度提高58%，总关节磨损减少约56%；磨损优势在曲柄转速-负载包络内保持48%-56%；两个关键连杆长度主导磨损方差；磨损优势在合理加工误差下性能退化平缓。

Conclusion: 本文建立的优化框架为延长步行连杆机构寿命提供了实用路径，并为未来考虑磨损-间隙-冲击耦合的研究奠定了基线。

Abstract: The Jansen linkage is a single-degree-of-freedom planar leg mechanism whose eleven "holy numbers" were evolved by Theo Jansen to optimize the foot-path gait alone, with no regard for the wear of its revolute joints. This paper introduces a durability objective into the design of the Jansen leg. A parametric forward-kinematic model (two-circle-intersection solver), an inverse-dynamic model (constraint-Jacobian / Lagrange-multiplier formulation of a seven-body, ten-joint system, independently cross-verified by a reduced-DOF energy method), and an Archard wear model are coupled to evaluate, for any set of link lengths, both gait quality and the per-cycle sliding wear at every pin. Because the wear is computed on ideal, clearance-free revolute joints, the resulting wear figures are a relative comparative ranking rather than an absolute life prediction. A bi-objective problem -- composite gait error versus total joint wear, subject to step-length, ground-clearance, duty-factor and assembly constraints -- is solved with NSGA-II. Under the adopted gait metric the classical Jansen design is Pareto-dominated: for a representative design, link-length adjustments within +/-29% simultaneously flatten the stance (-28%), smooth the stance velocity (-58%) and reduce total joint wear by ~56%. A sensitivity study shows the wear advantage is robust across a crank-speed x payload envelope (48%-56%) and identifies the link lengths that most strongly govern wear. A variance-based global (Sobol) analysis confirms that two link lengths dominate the wear variance, and a Monte-Carlo manufacturing-tolerance study shows the wear advantage degrades gracefully under realistic fabrication error. The framework provides a practical route to longer-lived walking linkages and a baseline for future wear-clearance-impact coupled studies.

</details>


### [82] [Overcoming Imperfect Kinematics in Surgical Robotics Through Sim-to-Real Visuomotor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21396)
*Zhaoxuan Yan,Kaizhong Deng,Zhaoyang Jacopo Hu,George P. Mylonas,Daniel S. Elson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于教师-学生学习框架的视觉闭环控制策略，通过融合不可靠的内部传感器与外部视觉数据，实时补偿手术机器人的运动学误差，并在达芬奇研究套件上验证了该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人固有的运动学不精确性导致控制误差，传统的基于模型的标定方法成本高且效果有限，亟需一种低成本、有效的实时误差补偿方法以推动手术自动化发展。

Method: 采用教师-学生学习框架训练闭环视觉反馈策略，融合不可靠的内部编码器读数与精确的外部视觉数据，无需精确物理模型即可实时修正运动学误差。

Result: 在达芬奇研究套件(dVRK)上成功部署该学习策略，实验验证了利用外部视觉克服内部传感器缺陷的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种基础且可靠的控制方法，为更先进、鲁棒的手术自动化铺平了道路。

Abstract: Robot-Assisted Surgery is integral to modern minimally invasive procedures, with automation emerging as the next frontier to enhance precision and reduce surgeon fatigue. This evolution is largely impeded by the inherent kinematic inaccuracies of surgical robots, where unreliable internal sensors lead to significant control errors. While previous methods attempted to mitigate these issues through complex model-based calibration, they often suffer from high cost and limited effectiveness. This work utilises a learning-policy to actively compensate for hardware inaccuracies using closed-loop visual feedback that was trained from a teacher-student learning framework. The policy can fuse unreliable internal readings with precise external visual data, allowing it to correct for kinematic errors in real time without needing a perfect physical model. The learned policy was successfully deployed on the da Vinci Research Kit, where experiments validated the fundamental feasibility of using external vision to overcome internal sensor deficits. This research provides a foundational and reliable control methodology, paving the way for more advanced and robust surgical automation.

</details>


### [83] [Zero-shot Transfer of Reinforcement Learning Control Policies for the Swing-Up and Stabilization of a Cart-Pole System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22145)
*Nikki Xu,Hien Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习策略从仿真到实际硬件的零样本迁移，用于推车-摆杆的起摆和稳定控制。两个策略独立训练，通过Simulink切换逻辑实现交接。采用一阶动作平滑滤波器防止硬件损坏，结合带宽感知滤波、灵敏度引导的域随机化和线性课程学习，成功实现了策略的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用强化学习实现控制器设计的现代化，并解决RL策略从仿真到真实硬件零样本迁移的跨域问题。

Method: 1) 独立训练起摆和稳定两个策略；2) 通过Simulink切换逻辑实现策略交接；3) 应用一阶动作平滑滤波器抑制高频振荡；4) 结合带宽感知滤波、灵敏度引导的域随机化和线性课程学习进行训练。

Result: 起摆策略能够注入足够能量使系统进入稳定器的吸引域；稳定策略能有效抵抗测试范围内的扰动；起摆策略在较大扰动后可重新介入并恢复摆杆倒立位置。

Conclusion: 结合动作平滑滤波、域随机化和课程学习的强化学习方法能够实现从仿真到硬件的零样本策略迁移，且在实际推车-摆杆系统上表现良好。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful and convenient tool to modernize controller design. In this work, we study the zero-shot transfer of RL-based control policies from simulation to hardware for cart-pole swing-up and stabilization. The two policies are trained independently, and the handoff is implemented in Simulink via switching logic. We apply a first-order action smoothing filter to prevent hardware damage from high-frequency oscillatory actuation. Pairing this bandwidth-aware filtering with sensitivity-guided domain randomization (DR) and a simple linear curriculum learning (CL) schedule, we obtain a swing-up policy that in all of our experiments injects sufficient energy for handoff into the stabilizer's region of attraction. The stabilization policy rejects disturbances within the tested range, and the swing-up policy can re-engage after larger perturbations and restores the pendulum to the inverted position.

</details>


### [84] [BIT-Nav: Brain-Inspired Trajectory Memory for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21398)
*Rithvik Jonna,Aakash Gurram,Man Namgung,Wyatt Mackey,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIT-Nav提出了一种受海马体路径整合启发的紧凑轨迹记忆框架，通过Bi-GRU编码动作和相对位姿序列，将结构化运动历史压缩为单个记忆token注入VLM导航管道，以固定token成本实现长程导航中的行为信号捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM导航方法从增长轨迹中稀疏选择固定帧数，但增加帧数（8→64）无精度提升，表明瓶颈在于表征本身而非数量。稀疏帧选择无法捕获长程推理所需的结构化行为信号（转向模式、累积位移、路径拓扑）。

Method: 1) 训练Bi-GRU编码器处理动作与相对位姿序列；2) 使用多正例InfoNCE对比学习目标在共享行为意图的轨迹前缀上进行训练；3) 通过轻量MLP将嵌入投影到VLM token空间；4) 在每个决策步骤作为单一记忆token注入，保持恒定token成本。

Result: 在长程导航场景中，BIT-Nav以固定token成本实现了对结构化运动历史的有效编码，克服了稀疏帧选择的表征瓶颈。

Conclusion: 受海马体路径整合机制启发，将空间经验压缩为结构化 episodic 痕迹而非原始感官重放，为VLM导航提供了一种高效、可扩展的轨迹记忆方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for embodied navigation rely on selecting a fixed number of frames from a growing trajectory history. As episodes extend, this selection grows increasingly sparse, yet prior work shows no accuracy gain when scaling from 8 to 64 frames, suggesting the bottleneck is not frame quantity but the representation itself. Sparse frame selection cannot capture the structured behavioral signal that long-horizon reasoning requires: turning patterns, cumulative displacement, and path topology.
  We introduce BIT-Nav (Brain-Inspired Trajectory Memory for Navigation), a framework that augments frozen VLM navigation pipelines with a compact learned trajectory memory. Motivated by hippocampal path integration, where spatial experience is compressed into structured episodic traces rather than stored as raw sensory replay, BIT-Nav trains a Bi-GRU encoder over action and relative pose sequences via a multi-positive InfoNCE contrastive objective on trajectory prefixes sharing the same behavioral intent. The resulting embedding is projected into the VLM token space via a lightweight MLP and injected as a single memory token at each decision step, conditioning the model on structured motion history at constant token cost regardless of episode length

</details>


### [85] [A Relaxed Quadratic-Program-based Framework for Trajectory Tracking of Unicycle Robots with Singularity Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23355)
*Hamza Tariq,Usman Ali,Adeel Akhtar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对轮式移动机器人轨迹跟踪中动态反馈线性化（DFL）控制器在速度为零时的奇异性问题，提出了一种基于二次规划（QP）的最优控制框架，通过引入松弛变量将DFL约束转化为等式约束QP，确保所有状态和参考信号下的可行性，并在TurtleBot3机器人上通过仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DFL控制器在机器人线速度为零时出现奇异，无法支持停-倒车等操作，需要一种避免奇异性的控制方法。

Method: 将DFL约束重构为带松弛变量的等式约束QP问题，引入可调参数避免奇异配置，建立反馈律的局部Lipschitz连续性。

Result: 在ROS 2-Gazebo仿真环境下使用TurtleBot3 Waffle机器人验证了轨迹跟踪的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提QP框架有效避免了DFL奇异性，对于包含零速度的大类参考轨迹均能保证可行性，且反馈律具有局部Lipschitz连续性。

Abstract: Dynamic feedback linearization (DFL) is a classical technique for trajectory tracking of unicycle-type mobile robots, but the resulting DFL-based controller becomes singular when the linear velocity vanishes, rendering standard DFL-based controllers unsuitable for stop-and-reverse maneuvers. This paper proposes a quadratic-program (QP)-based optimal control framework that avoids this singularity, while establishing local Lipschitz continuity of the resulting feedback law. Our approach reformulates the DFL constraints as an equality-constrained QP with a slack variable, ensuring feasibility for all states and reference signals, including at points where the robot's velocity vanishes. By introducing slack variables and tunable parameters, we demonstrate that the singular configuration can be avoided for a large class of reference trajectories. The effectiveness of the proposed approach for trajectory tracking is demonstrated through ROS 2-Gazebo simulations on a TurtleBot3 Waffle robot. The code is available at https://gradslab.github.io/DFL_QP_Unicycle/

</details>


### [86] [Robot Self-Improvement via Human-Video Dynamics Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21406)
*Hanzhi Chen,Anran Zhang,Simon Schaefer,Kejia Chen,Shi Chen,Daniel Cremers,Oier Mees,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DGAC方法，利用人类视频学习不受本体限制的动作、动力学和价值表征，使机器人能够从自身的失败中自主改进。在7个真实操作任务中，成功率从40%提升至81%，实现了跨本体的机器人自我提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频能否支持机器人不仅初始化策略，还能评估、纠正并改进自身尝试（即支持第二和第三种学习方式），这是一个尚未明确的问题。

Method: 提出DGAC（动力学引导的动作修正），一种无需训练的方法。从人类视频中学习跨本体的动作、动力学和价值表征，用于对失败状态提出并排序纠正动作，将失败转化为下一策略更新的监督信号。

Result: 在7个真实操作任务中（涵盖移动机械臂和静态机械臂），跨多个策略骨干将成功率从40%提升至81%。

Conclusion: 人类先验与机器人失败可以结合，实现可扩展的自主策略改进，证明了跨本体机器人自我提升的可行性。

Abstract: A central question in robot learning is how to acquire skills from the kinds of data that humans learn from: passive observation, embodied practice, and the experience of failure. Human videos provide the first of these in abundance, and prior work has shown they can initialize useful policies. Far less clear is whether they can support the second and third: whether priors extracted from human videos can ground a robot's own attempts well enough to evaluate them, correct them, and improve from them. In this work, we show that human videos can be used to learn embodiment-agnostic action, dynamics, and value representations that transfer across robot embodiments, providing the predictive foundation required for robots to autonomously improve from their own rollouts and failures. We introduce Dynamics-Guided Action Correction (DGAC), a training-free approach that leverages these adapted models to repair failed states: each failure becomes a query for which the learned models propose and rank corrective actions, turning failures into supervision for the next policy update. Across seven real-world manipulation tasks spanning both a mobile manipulator and a static manipulator arm, our approach improves success rates from 40% to 81% across multiple policy backbones, demonstrating cross-embodiment robot self-improvement from human-video priors. These results show that human priors and robot failures can be combined to enable scalable autonomous policy improvement. Project page: https://ethz-mrl.github.io/robot-self-improvement-website/.

</details>


### [87] [Any-Body Guard: Universal Safeguarding for Manipulation Policies via Action Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22278)
*Alex Beaudin,Hanna Krasowski,Kartik Nagpal,Sanjit A. Seshia,Murat Arcak,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-Safe是一种通用安全保障方法，通过在机器人配置空间中直接推理，提供碰撞避免的形式化概率保证，无需额外数据或工程调整即可跨实体和任务迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保障方法在跨实体和任务时存在保守性强、计算开销大、缺乏形式化保证或工程迁移成本高等问题，亟需一种通用且高效的安全保障方案。

Method: 在机器人配置空间中直接推理，利用基于物体的准静态场景表示和机械臂正向运动学模型，提供碰撞避免的形式化概率保证。

Result: 在多种实体和策略的仿真及硬件实验中，X-Safe相比现有最优保障方法任务性能退化更小，硬件实验零碰撞，形式化保证得到实证验证。

Conclusion: X-Safe提供了一种跨实体、无需额外工程投入的通用安全保障框架，兼具形式化保证与实际有效性。

Abstract: Ensuring safety of learning-enabled robotic manipulation across diverse embodiments and tasks still requires significant manual engineering. Existing approaches typically rely on heuristically designed fallback controllers or complex forward invariance assessments. These methods are often too conservative for task success, too computationally expensive for real-time execution, too heuristic to provide useful safety guarantees, or too engineering-heavy to transfer between setups. In this paper, we propose a universal safeguarding approach, X-Safe, which reasons directly in the robot's configuration space to provide formal probabilistic guarantees for collision avoidance. By operating in the configuration space, our method transfers across embodiments while relying solely on an object-based, quasi-static scene representation and a forward kinematics model of the robotic manipulator. Thus, X-Safe provides useful formal safety guarantees without requiring additional data, or engineering effort for different embodiments or scenes. We demonstrate X-Safe for diverse embodiments and policies, both in simulation and on hardware. We observe less degradation in task performance compared to state-of-the-art safeguarding, no collisions on hardware experiments, and empirically corroborate our formal guarantees.

</details>


### [88] [Temporal logics and formal synthesis for robot planning and control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21438)
*Jana Tumova,Joris Verhagen,Matti Vahs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于时态逻辑的形式化合成方法在机器人行为规范与保证中的应用，涵盖从离散图/博弈方法到采样运动规划、轨迹优化及控制证书合成等连续方法，并讨论了在真实机器人部署中面临的建模保真度、计算可行性与保证类型之间的权衡挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人从受控环境进入真实世界，确保机器人按预期执行变得至关重要，因此需要对期望的机器人行为进行严谨的规范描述，并开发能够满足这些规范且具有可证明保证的规划与控制合成方法。

Method: 本文首先介绍线性时态逻辑(LTL)和信号时态逻辑(STL)作为表达机器人随时间行为的规范语言，然后讨论形式化合成原理，包括离散的图/博弈方法、基于采样的运动规划、轨迹优化以及基于控制证书的合成方法。

Result: 系统梳理了时态逻辑在机器人行为规范中的应用框架，以及从离散到连续各类形式化合成方法的技术路线。

Conclusion: 指出在真实机器人系统中部署形式化合成面临挑战，核心在于建模保真度、计算可处理性与可实现严格保证类型之间的复杂平衡。

Abstract: As robots move from controlled environments into real-world settings, it becomes increasingly crucial to ensure that they perform as expected. A key step toward that goal is a rigorous specification of the desired robot behavior, capturing intricate temporal, spatial, and logical requirements. Complementing this, plan and control synthesis methods are needed to fulfill these specifications with provable guarantees. This manuscript presents temporal logics - particularly linear and signal temporal logic - as expressive specification languages for robot behavior over time. We then discuss principles of formal synthesis, from discrete graph- and game-based approaches to sampling-based motion planning, trajectory optimization, and control-certificate-based synthesis. Finally, we outline challenges in deploying formal synthesis in real-world robotics, emphasizing the interplay between modeling fidelity, computational tractability, and the types of rigorous guarantees that can be achieved.

</details>


### [89] [Manipulider: A Multi-Engine Buoyancy-Controlled Robot for Thrusterless Underwater Gliding and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21461)
*Yitao Jiang,Yewei Huang,Weizhi Cao,Mingi Jeong,Alberto Quattrini Li,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为Manipulider的浮力驱动水下机器人，采用无推进器的滑翔式运动和姿态操作，配备磁力模块化接口用于快速更换载荷（如夹爪或传感器）。四个分布式注射器式浮力引擎共同调节净浮力和浮心位置，使机器人能够在无需持续推力的情况下通过静态力平衡维持大倾斜角度，并避免螺旋桨缠绕风险。实验表明，每个引擎的可控浮力位移范围为40 mL（总浮力约160 g），单引擎和双引擎最大静态稳定倾斜角分别为64.6°和61.8°，并展示了倾斜调节、受控上升/下降原语以及无推进器的夹爪载荷运输序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水下机器人依赖推进器进行运动和操控，存在螺旋桨缠绕风险、能耗高、噪声大等问题。本研究旨在开发一种无推进器的水下机器人，通过浮力驱动实现高效、安全的 locomotion 和姿态操控。

Method: 采用四个基于注射器的浮力引擎对称分布于机器人主体周围，通过调节每个引擎的浮力位移来联合控制净浮力和浮心位置，实现机器人的倾斜姿态控制和上下运动。同时设计了磁力模块化接口，便于快速更换不同载荷。

Result: 每个浮力引擎的可控浮力位移范围为40 mL，总浮力约160 g；单引擎最大静态稳定倾斜角64.6°，双引擎61.8°；展示了倾斜调节、受控上升/下降运动以及无推进器的夹爪载荷运输能力。

Conclusion: Manipulider成功验证了纯浮力驱动水下机器人在姿态操控和载荷运输方面的可行性，为水下作业提供了一种无推进器风险、低能耗的新方案。

Abstract: The Manipulider is a buoyancy-actuated underwater robot that enables thrusterless, glide-like locomotion and attitude-based manipulation, while providing a magnetic modular interface for rapid payload swapping (e.g., a gripper or sensors). Four syringe-based buoyancy engines distributed around the body jointly regulate net buoyancy and the center of buoyancy, allowing the vehicle to maintain large tilt angles through static force balance without continuous thrust and to avoid propeller entanglement risks. We present the mechanical and electrical design, calibration procedure, and control architecture. Experiments with a gripper attached (no external payload) show a controllable buoyancy-displacement range of 40 mL per engine ({\approx}160 g total buoyancy authority), maximum statically stable tilts of 64.6° (single-engine) and 61.8° (dual-engine), and representative vertical and tilt-transition dynamics. We further demonstrate tilt regulation, controlled ascent/descent primitives, and a proof-of-concept gripper-based payload-transport sequence without thrusters.

</details>


### [90] [HERCULES: An Open-Source Simulation Framework for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot SLAM, Collaborative Perception, and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22756)
*Sandilya Sai Garimella,Daniel Chase Butterfield,Sean Wilson,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HERCULES是一个基于UE5的开源异构多机器人仿真与数据采集平台，支持大规模、高保真的UAV-UGV协同操作，提供统一导航栈、增强传感器套件、ROS 2接口及严格时间同步，可在被动数据回放和主动闭环规划两种模式下运行，并通过实验验证了其在异构多机器人SLAM、协同感知与探索中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于UE5的仿真框架（如AirSim、Cosys-AirSim）在支持异构多机器人（UAV-UGV）大规模协同操作时存在架构局限性，缺乏统一控制接口、跨平台导航栈及高保真退化视觉环境仿真能力，亟需一个开源、可重复、支持闭环验证的异构多机器人仿真平台。

Method: 基于UE5的AirSim和Cosys-AirSim进行架构扩展：设计新UGV航点跟踪控制器以统一UAV控制接口；构建共享导航栈（建图、可通行性分析、规划与控制）；新增物理长波红外相机和可配置夜视模式；提供轻量API和ROS 2封装，实现跨传感器和平台严格时间同步；集成智能体（行人、交通、野生动物）和高保真动态现象（火灾、洪水、作物病害）；支持被动离线轨迹回放和主动闭环在线规划两种模式。

Result: 在异构多机器人SLAM、协同感知与探索任务中，使用HERCULES生成的数据和主动闭环执行均验证了其有效性；发布了包括两架UAV和两辆UGV在沙漠、森林、城市公里级场景下的异构多机器人SLAM基准数据集。

Conclusion: HERCULES有效克服了现有仿真框架在异构多机器人协同方面的局限，提供了高保真、可扩展、支持闭环验证的仿真平台，并开源全栈代码与数据集，有助于推动异构多机器人自主性研究。

Abstract: We present HERCULES, an open-source simulator and data-collection pipeline for heterogeneous multi-robot autonomy. Built upon the Unreal Engine 5 (UE5)-based simulators AirSim and Cosys-AirSim, HERCULES resolves key architectural limitations of prior frameworks to enable concurrent unmanned aerial and ground vehicle (UAV-UGV) operation in large-scale, photorealistic, dynamic environments. It introduces a new waypoint-tracking UGV controller that mirrors existing UAV control interfaces, and provides a shared navigation stack for mapping, traversability analysis, planning, and control across heterogeneous platforms. Expanding inherited sensor suites, it adds physics-based long-wave infrared (LWIR) cameras and configurable night-vision modes for degraded visual environments. HERCULES provides lightweight APIs, ROS 2 wrappers, and rigorous time synchronization across sensors and platforms, and brings state-of-the-art game-engine capabilities into robotics simulation, integrating intelligent agents such as pedestrians, traffic, and wildlife with high-fidelity dynamic phenomena, including fire, flooding, and crop disease spread. HERCULES runs in two modes: passively, replaying offline-designed trajectories to generate reproducible multi-modal datasets, and actively, running an online planner in closed loop from live observations. Our experiments in heterogeneous multi-robot SLAM, collaborative perception, and exploration, using both HERCULES-generated data and active closed-loop execution, demonstrate its utility for advancing heterogeneous multi-robot autonomy. We publicly release our source code, experiment code, documentation, and datasets, including a heterogeneous multi-robot SLAM benchmark collected with two UAVs and two UGVs across kilometer-scale desert, forest, and city environments, at https://lunarlab-gatech.github.io/HERCULES-website.

</details>


### [91] [ASCII Art Turns LLMs into VLA Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21470)
*Yitao Jiang,Roy Xing,Luyang Zhao,Brian Plancher,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过ASCII渲染将视觉观测转换为文本表示，从而将纯文本LLM改造为VLA控制器，无需依赖多模态视觉-语言模型。实验表明该方法在2D操作任务中（仿真与实物）能够有效识别实体并规划动作序列，为轻量级、可解释的VLA研究提供新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA控制器通常基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）构建，需要大规模多模态数据和计算资源。本文试图探索是否可以通过纯文本LLM实现VLA控制，降低对多模态骨干网络的依赖。

Method: 将视觉观测通过ASCII表示渲染为文本输入，使纯文本LLM能够条件于视觉状态、遵循自然语言指令并生成可执行动作。采用多种LLM和VLM进行微调比较，使用基于规划专家的演示和DAgger迭代改进训练。

Result: 在2D操作基准测试中（仿真与实物），基于ASCII渲染的LLM控制器能够识别任务相关实体并规划可行动作序列，性能与基于VLM的控制器可比。

Conclusion: ASCII渲染可作为从图像到文本的轻量级、可解释的模态桥梁，补充传统VLA流水线，为使用纯文本骨干网络进行VLA研究开辟新方向。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) controllers are often built by extending vision--language models (VLMs) with action supervision, relying on multimodal backbones with large data and compute requirements. We demonstrate that a text-only large language model (LLM) can be adapted into a VLA-style controller when visual observations are rendered into a text input using an ASCII representation. This ASCII-as-vision interface enables existing training and deployment stacks for LLMs to efficiently condition on visual state, follow natural-language instructions, and produce constrained, executable actions. We fine-tune and compare multiple LLMs and VLMs across model families and scales, using both expert demonstrations from a planning-based teacher, as well as DAgger for iterative improvement. In a 2D manipulation benchmark, in both simulation and on a physical manipulator, the resulting controllers can identify task-relevant entities and plan feasible action sequences. Our results suggest that ASCII rendering can serve as a lightweight, interpretable modality bridge from images to text, complementing conventional VLA pipelines, and opening directions for VLA research with text-only backbones.

</details>


### [92] [Decoupling the Declarative from the Procedural in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21496)
*Nikolaos Tsagkas,Andreas Sochopoulos,Chris Xiaoxuan Lu,Oisin Mac Aodha,Alexandros Kouris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出w²VLA，一种通过重构信息流来解耦声明性知识与程序性知识的视觉-语言-动作模型，实现了对未见物体的零样本技能迁移，解决了当前VLA模型在分布外泛化能力弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将视觉-语言编码器输出的多模态token直接输入到大型黑盒transformer动作专家中，导致声明性知识（概念和实体语义）与程序性知识（操作方式）在参数中纠缠不清，模型在面对新的物体时无法实现零样本技能迁移，泛化能力差。

Method: 提出w²VLA模型，重构信息流：不再将所有多模态token直接输入动作专家，而是以组合式和可解释的方式，用视觉、空间和技能信息来调制机器人状态序列，实现知识表示的显式解耦。

Result: 实验表明，w²VLA成功解耦了知识表示，在分布内保持性能的同时，在未见过的、不相似的物体上展现出前所未有的零样本技能迁移能力。

Conclusion: 模块化、可解释的信息流设计是解决VLA模型泛化瓶颈的有效路径，w²VLA通过解耦声明性与程序性知识，实现了鲁棒的行为克隆和零样本迁移。

Abstract: Deploying generalist robotic agents in the real world requires transferable skills. Specifically, a policy trained to clone a behavior from object-specific demonstrations must generalize beyond that object, otherwise data collection requirements become intractable. Recently, fine-tuning of pre-trained billion-parameter Vision-Language Models (VLMs), initially on large-scale robot datasets and then on fewer scenario-specific demonstrations, has emerged as the predominant paradigm for designing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. While these policies achieve state-of-the-art manipulation performance in-distribution, they remain brittle to minor spatial, semantic, and task variations. In this work, we address the inability of current models to decouple the declarative (i.e., concepts and entity semantics) from the procedural knowledge (i.e., how to do something) encoded in their parameters, which is a fundamental bottleneck for zero-shot skill transfer to novel objects. To address this, we propose w$^{2}$VLA, a new VLA model with restructured information flow. Rather than feeding all multimodal tokens from the VLM encoder into a large, opaque transformer-based action expert, our approach modulates the robot state sequence with visual, spatial, and skill information in a compositional and interpretable manner. Unlike popular, state-of-the-art VLAs, we show that our modular approach successfully decouples knowledge representations, enabling robust behavior cloning and unprecedented zero-shot skill transfer capabilities across dissimilar, unseen objects.

</details>


### [93] [UniviewVLA: A Unified Multiview Vision-Language-Action Model with World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21501)
*Tao Xu,Runhao Zhang,Zhijian Huang,Jiayi Guan,Jiaxin Wang,Yifan Ding,Yong-Lu Li,Long Chen,Guang Chen,Jinghui Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniviewVLA提出了一种统一的多视图视觉-语言-动作模型，结合世界建模，仅从标准双摄像头观测中生成多视图未来场景演化，揭示遮挡信息并预测动作，同时通过动作信息令牌压缩（625→16令牌，延迟从6-7秒降至0.2-0.3秒）和训练无关的动作-熵视图选择实现高效推理。在LIBERO上达95.8%，CALVIN ABCD→D为4.60，定制遮挡任务成功率从40.0%提升至73.3%，真实机器人成功率平均提升33.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作在遮挡场景下存在瓶颈：额外物理相机要求训练-推理相机一致，显式3D重建计算成本高，且标准观察视角无法捕捉遮挡信息和未来场景演化。

Method: 提出UniviewVLA，包含三个核心模块：(1) 多视图世界模型，从标准双视图生成未来多视图场景演化；(2) 运动信息令牌压缩（Motion-Informative Token Compression），将每视图从625令牌压缩至16令牌，延迟从6-7秒降至0.2-0.3秒；(3) 训练无关的动作-熵视图选择（Action-Entropy View Selection），动态识别最具有动作信息量的视图。

Result: 标准无遮挡基准：LIBERO达95.8%，CALVIN ABCD→D达4.60。定制遮挡任务成功率从40.0%提升至73.3%。真实机器人平均成功率提升33.4个百分点。

Conclusion: UniviewVLA通过生成式多视图未来场景建模，在不牺牲标准无遮挡性能的前提下，显著提升了遮挡场景下的机器人操作能力，同时避免了额外硬件和显式3D重建的需求。

Abstract: Occluded tasks remain a bottleneck in robot manipulation. Existing solutions either deploy additional physical cameras requiring training-inference camera parity, or rely on explicit 3D reconstruction with high computational cost. Moreover, both approaches rely on standard agent-view and wrist-view observations, while failing to capture occlusion information and future scene evolution. To this end, we propose UniviewVLA, a unified multiview Vision-Language-Action model with world modeling, which infers multiview scene evolution for action prediction from only standard two-camera observations. We demonstrate that by leveraging generated multiview future views from the world model, UniviewVLA reveals occluded cues and models future scene evolution, improving action prediction and removing the need for extra hardware or explicit reconstruction. Besides, to accelerate inference while preserving prediction accuracy, UniviewVLA develops Motion-Informative Token Compression, which compresses each generated view from 625 to 16 tokens and reduces per-view latency from 6-7s to 0.2-0.3s. UniviewVLA also proposes training-free Action-Entropy View Selection, which dynamically identifies the most action-informative view at different inference stages. Extensive experiments show that UniviewVLA achieves 95.8% on LIBERO and 4.60 on CALVIN ABCD to D, both standard occlusion-free benchmarks. On customized occlusion-focused tasks, it improves success rate from 40.0% to 73.3%, and average real-robot success rate by 33.4 points, demonstrating stronger occlusion-focused performance without sacrificing standard occlusion-free benchmarks.

</details>


### [94] [A Stitch in Time Saves Nine: Preserving Policy Compatibility Under Perception Updates in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21509)
*Yueyuan Li,Yifei Xiao,Mingyang Jiang,Xiang Zuo,Songan Zhang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对端到端自动驾驶系统中感知模型更新导致下游策略性能下降的问题，提出通过轻量级潜在空间对齐（模型拼接）来保持策略兼容性，实验表明线性拼接和卷积拼接能有效恢复驾驶行为，在跨域场景下卷积拼接保留91%以上的驾驶得分，并将适应时间从22.18小时大幅降至0.91小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶系统通过潜在表示紧密耦合感知和决策模块，更新感知模型会改变潜在表示分布，导致固定的下游策略性能下降。现有策略重训练或架构解耦方案计算和验证成本高昂，亟需一种轻量级、高效的替代方案来维护系统兼容性。

Method: 本文形式化定义了端到端自动驾驶的模型拼接问题，提出使用低复杂度的线性拼接器（linear stitcher）和卷积拼接器（convolutional stitcher）对更新后的感知模块与冻结的下游策略模块之间的潜在表示进行对齐，以恢复策略兼容性。

Result: 在多种感知更新场景（随机初始化变化、传感器配置变化、训练域变化）下，模型拼接有效保留了驾驶行为。最难的跨域场景（nuScenes→CARLA）中，卷积拼接保留了91%以上的无偏移驾驶得分，适应时间从22.18小时降至0.91小时。

Conclusion: 模型拼接为维护端到端自动驾驶系统提供了一种高效且计算成本低的替代方案，显著优于重训练或微调方法。代码将在论文被接收后开源。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems tightly couple perception and decision-making through latent representations. Consequently, updates to perception models can alter these representations and degrade the performance of downstream policies that remain fixed. Existing solutions typically rely on policy retraining or architectural decoupling, both of which incur substantial computation and validation costs. In this paper, we formulate the model stitching problem for end-to-end autonomous driving and test the hypothesis that policy compatibility can be preserved through lightweight latent-space alignment. We study low-complexity model stitching methods, including linear and convolutional stitchers, for restoring compatibility between updated perception modules and frozen downstream policy modules. Experiments demonstrate that stitching effectively preserves downstream driving behavior under diverse perception updates, including changes in random initialization, sensor configuration, and training domain. In the most challenging cross-domain setting from nuScenes to CARLA, convolutional stitching retains over 91\% of the no-shift driving score while reducing adaptation time from \SI{22.18}{h} to \SI{0.91}{h}.
