These results suggest that model stitching provides an effective and computationally efficient alternative to retraining or fine-tuning for maintaining end-to-end autonomous driving systems. The model will be open-sourced upon paper acceptance at https://github.com/SCP-CN-001/model-stitching to support further research and development in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [95] [LOGOS: LiDAR-Only Gaussian Elevation Splatting for Unified Tiny Obstacle Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21527)
*Nan Ming,Yeqiang Qian,Chunxiang Wang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOGOS提出了一种基于2D高斯原语连续建模的纯激光雷达微小障碍物分割系统，通过免反向传播的高程估计方法实现道路与非道路区域的高精度区分，在道路和越野场景中均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的方法在日常障碍物上表现良好，但对路缘、砾石、坑洼等微小障碍物分割效果不佳，主要原因是微小障碍物与道路固有起伏之间特征高度相似；同时，在越野场景下LiDAR扫描退化导致分割精度急剧下降。

Method: 将道路表面建模为2D高斯原语的连续混合，通过自由空间感知的初始化直接估计高斯参数，无需反向传播；利用平滑性约束增量剪除非道路原语；提出法线感知的高程溅射函数计算逐点符号距离，同时对平坦和倾斜地形保持鲁棒性。

Result: 在包含城市交通场景和矿山越野环境的高度异构点云基准上评估，使用了不同LiDAR传感器采集的数据，在点密度、地形粗糙度和障碍物类型上具有大差异。实验表明LOGOS在退化点云区域和挑战性越野场景中显著优于其他最先进方法，且保持实时效率。

Conclusion: LOGOS通过纯LiDAR方式实现了鲁棒的微小障碍物分割，克服了现有方法在退化点云和越野场景中的瓶颈，验证了无需RGB训练的高斯原语建模在高精度高程估计中的有效性。

Abstract: Robust obstacle segmentation is essential for the safety of intelligent robots, where LiDAR-based perception systems play a fundamental role in the robot-environment interaction. While extensive LiDAR-based approaches have demonstrated high performance on common obstacles in urban scenarios, their results on tiny obstacles such as curbs, gravel, and potholes remain unsatisfactory due to the significant similarity between tiny obstacles and inherent road undulations. Moreover, their segmentation accuracy even deteriorates sharply when the LiDAR scans suffer from degradation in challenging off-road scenes. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose LOGOS, a LiDAR-only unified tiny obstacle segmentation system, which models the road surface as a continuous mixture of 2D Gaussian primitives and distinguishes tiny obstacles via high-presicion elevation estimation. Unlike existing Gaussian splatting methods that rely on iterative RGB training, LOGOS is a backpropagation-free LiDAR-only approach. It directly estimates Gaussian parameters via a freespace-aware initialization by incrementally pruning non-road primitives using smoothness constraints. Subsequently, pointwise signed distances are computed via a novel normal-aware elevation splatting function, ensuring robustness to both flat and sloped terrains. We evaluate LOGOS on a highly heterogeneous benchmark of point cloud frames collected from urban mobility scenarios and mining haulage off-road environments. These data are practically acquired using different LiDAR sensors and exhibit large variations in point density, terrain roughness, and obstacle types. Experiments on the road and off-road scenes demonstrate that LOGOS significantly outperforms other state-of-the-art methods, particularly in degraded point cloud regions and challenging off-road scenarios, while maintaining real-time efficiency.

</details>


### [96] [Autonomous Subsea Cable Search and Tracking with Graph-Optimised Priors and Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23606)
*Ibrahim Fadhil Djauhari,Adrian Bodenmann,Samuel Simmons,Cailei Liang,David White,Susan Gourvenec,Tom Bennetts,Darryl Newborough,Blair Thornton*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用不确定先验路由图的水下电缆搜索与跟踪方法，通过图优化、物理约束的悬链线模型和半监督分类器，使AUV能够在路由图不准确的情况下实现电缆检测与视觉跟踪，并在海试中成功定位电缆并跟踪120米测试电缆的59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海底电缆基础设施易受自然危害和人类活动损坏，现有AUV检测面临电缆路由图不确定、电缆直径小、部分掩埋等挑战，亟需一种能够在不确定先验信息下实现可靠电缆搜索与跟踪的方法。

Method: 采用图优化持续更新电缆路由以保持与视觉观测一致；利用基于悬链线模型的物理约束限制路由不确定性为观测距离的函数；使用运行在AUV机载的半监督分类器进行实时电缆检测，检测结果同时反馈至图优化和视觉跟踪；当跟踪丢失时，利用约束后的搜索空间实现高效恢复。

Result: 在南安普顿大学Smarty200 AUV上进行的海试中，尽管初始路由图存在有意误差，系统仍成功定位电缆；更新后路由图与观测一致；视觉跟踪覆盖120米测试电缆的59%，并在跟踪丢失后成功恢复。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效应对先验路由图的不确定性，结合物理模型约束和在线学习分类器，实现了鲁棒的AUV海底电缆搜索与跟踪，并在实际海试中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Global communications rely on subsea cable infrastructure that remains vulnerable to damage from natural hazards and human activity. Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) offer an efficient means to inspect long sections of exposed cable, but uncertainty in cable route maps, small cable diameters and partial burial makes continuous tracking a challenge. This paper presents a novel cable search and tracking method that leverages uncertain prior cable route maps. Graph-based optimisation continuously update the cable route to remain consistent with visual observations. Route uncertainty is constrained as a function of distance from observations using physics-based catenary models that account for cable parameters (i.e., lay depth, diameter, and density), bounding the search space to physically feasible regions and improving search efficiency. Cable detection is performed using a semi-supervised classifier running in real-time on-board a camera-equipped AUV. These detections both update the graph-based optimisation and enable visual cable tracking. When tracking is lost due to misclassification, burial or imperfect control, the bounded search space enables efficient recovery. The approach was demonstrated in field trials using the University of Southampton's Smarty200 AUV. The system successfully located the cable despite deliberate errors in it initial cable route map, updating this to be consistent with observations and using visual tracking to inspect up to 59% of a 120m test cable, with successful recovered after tracking loss.

</details>


### [97] [Robot Critics that Sweat the Small Stuff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21572)
*Sruthi Sudhakar,Junbang Liang,Sreehari Rammohan,Pavel Tokmakov,Richard Zemel,Carl Vondrick*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过成对进度监督微调VLM评论器的方法，使其能够精细判断机器人操作的成功与失败，并结合动作条件视频模型选择最佳动作，在真实和仿真任务中分别提升11%和5.9%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉语言模型蕴含丰富的世界先验知识，可作为推理时的评论器引导机器人策略走向成功，但现有VLM难以判断成功与失败之间的细微视觉差异，限制了其在闭环机器人操作中的应用。

Method: 利用策略收集的成功/失败轨迹构建成对进度监督信号来微调VLM评论器；同时使用动作条件视频模型预测候选动作的视觉效果，由微调后的评论器识别最佳候选动作执行。

Result: 微调后的评论器在细粒度进度推理和细微失败检测上优于现有基线；在真实任务中提升平均策略成功率11%，仿真任务中提升5.9%。

Conclusion: 通过成对进度监督微调VLM评论器，并结合动作条件视频模型进行动作筛选，能有效提升机器人操作策略的成功率。

Abstract: Large vision-language models contain several priors about the world and object interactions, making them useful critics during inference to steer robot policies towards success. However, closed-loop robot manipulation requires judging small visual differences between success and failure, which remains a challenge for current VLMs. We introduce a method to fine-tune critics by constructing pairwise progress supervision using success and failure rollouts obtained from a policy. Our fine-tuned critic excels at fine-grained progress reasoning and subtle failure detection, outperforming prior progress reasoning baselines. Additionally, we use an action-conditioned video model to predict the visual effect of several candidate actions sampled from a policy, and show that our critic can correctly identify successful candidates to execute, improving the average policy success rate by 11% across real-world tasks and 5.9% across simulation tasks.

</details>


### [98] [Conflict-Aware Switching for CBF-CLF-Based Multi-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21577)
*Rohan Walia,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对CBF-CLF-QP控制中安全约束与标称控制目标之间的冲突问题，本文提出了一种冲突感知切换策略，通过检测高冲突条件并在可用标称控制目标之间进行切换来减少约束冲突。在多智能体多目标到达-避障场景中，该方法相比基线策略降低了完成时间和超时率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBF和CLF约束在本质上是冲突的，会导致性能下降（如减速和死锁），尤其在多目标场景下问题加剧。现有方法要么计算成本高且过于保守，要么不适合顺序目标导航。

Method: 提出一种冲突感知切换策略，检测高冲突条件，在可用标称控制目标之间切换以减少CBF与CLF约束冲突，并应用于多智能体多目标到达-避障场景的CBF-CLF-QP控制框架中。

Result: 与基线顺序目标遍历策略相比，所提方法在完成时间和超时率方面均有降低，在满足所有标称控制目标的同时保障了安全性。

Conclusion: 冲突感知切换策略有效缓解了CBF-CLF约束冲突问题，在保持安全性的前提下提升了多目标导航任务的性能。

Abstract: Quadratic programs (QPs) using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) and Control Lyapunov Functions (CLFs) are widely used for safe control in reach-and-avoid navigation. However, the inherently conflicting nature of CBF and CLF constraints can lead to performance degradation, including slowdowns and deadlocks. This issue is exacerbated in multi-goal scenarios, where multiple nominal control objectives must be satisfied under shared safety constraints. Existing approaches for preemptive safety are often computationally expensive or overly conservative, while methods that relax or switch between nominal objectives are not well-suited for sequential goal-to-goal navigation. To address these limitations, we propose a conflict-aware switching strategy that detects high-conflict conditions and switches between available nominal control objectives to reduce constraint conflict. We apply this approach to multi-agent, multi-goal reach-and-avoid scenarios under CBF-CLF-QP control. Compared to a baseline sequential goal traversal strategy, our method reduces both completion time and timeout rates, demonstrating improved performance in satisfying all nominal control objectives while respecting safety constraints.

</details>


### [99] [VQActFlow: Vector-Quantized Action Mode Steering for Multi-Task Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21600)
*Zhigen Zhao,Mark Leggiero,Yipu Chen,Haoran Liu,Yifan Wu,Huishu Xue,Sirui Zhan,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VQActFlow提出了一种将连续动作标记化为离散代码序列，并通过变分流匹配生成的多任务操作策略，利用推理时引导机制（基于语言的无分类器引导和代码书批评器）来明确偏好动作模式，在LIBERO、Unitree G1人形机器人和ALOHA双臂平台三个平台上均优于连续和离散基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务机器人操作策略从示范中学习困难，因为传统单一网络需要根据语言和视觉上下文从多模态示范分布中选择性质不同的动作模式，模式选择错误会导致执行错误任务或场景中不可行的动作。

Method: 将连续动作标记化为学习到的离散代码书以分离动作模式，通过变分流匹配生成代码序列；在推理时使用无分类器引导来调控语言条件以引导策略走向指令动作模式，并学习代码书批评器提供可行性信号。

Result: 在LIBERO模拟基准、Unitree G1人形机器人全身抓取放置任务和ALOHA双臂接触密集型任务三个平台上，VQActFlow均优于连续和离散基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过离散代码书将动作模式在表征层面分离，并引入推理时引导机制来调控模式选择，可以有效提升多任务操作策略的性能和可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-task robot manipulation policies are challenging to learn from demonstration because traditionally a single network must select among qualitatively different action modes from a multimodal demonstration distribution, conditioned on language and visual context. A wrong mode selection means executing the wrong task or an action infeasible in the scene. Tokenizing continuous actions into a learned discrete codebook separates these modes at the representation level, offering structural advantages for multi-task learning. We propose VQActFlow, a multi-task manipulation policy that tokenizes action chunks and generates code sequences via Variational Flow Matching. VQActFlow maintains an explicit preference over action modes throughout generation. Inference-time guidance acts on this preference to steer mode commitment. We instantiate this with classifier-free guidance over language conditioning, which steers the policy toward the instructed action mode, and a learned codebook critic that supplies a complementary feasibility signal. We evaluate VQActFlow on three platforms: the LIBERO simulation benchmarks, a Unitree G1 humanoid performing whole-body pick-and-place, and an ALOHA-style bimanual platform performing contact-rich tasks. Across these benchmarks, VQActFlow outperforms both continuous and discrete baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Energy-based Compositional Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21646)
*Tao Sun,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Jiaxin Lu,Danfei Xu,Iro Armeni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出能量基组合扩散规划器（ECD），通过将全局轨迹建模为局部桥势能之和的最小化器，解决了组合扩散规划器中启发式拼接导致的非保守场问题。该方法引入基于马尔可夫的分数近似，通过单次块三对角求解计算边界反应项，在保持线性时间复杂度的同时实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合扩散规划器在处理长程机器人任务时，依赖对短训练轨迹的启发式拼接（heuristic stitching），导致拼接后的更新场是非保守的（non-conservative field），在数学上不对应任何有效的全局轨迹对数密度函数，从而影响规划质量。

Method: 1) 将全局轨迹建模为局部桥势能（local bridge potentials）之和的最小化器，从能量视角定义保守校正场（conservative correction field）；2) 引入启发式拼接忽略的边界反应项（boundary reaction term）；3) 提出基于马尔可夫的分数近似（Markov-based score approximation），通过单次块三对角求解（block-tridiagonal solve）高效计算反应项，保持时间复杂度与规划步长成线性关系。

Result: 在OGBench拼接任务上达到SOTA成功率，同时推理速度接近启发式拼接方法。

Conclusion: ECD通过能量视角和高效计算框架，在理论正确性与实际效率之间取得了良好平衡，为长程机器人任务的组合扩散规划提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Compositional diffusion planners aim to solve long-horizon robotic tasks using short training trajectories. Yet, current approaches often rely on the heuristic stitching of local predictions. We show that the resulting stitched update is generally a non-conservative field} that does not mathematically correspond to any valid global trajectory log-density function. We propose Energy-based Compositional Diffuser (ECD), a framework that formulates the global trajectory as the minimizer of the sum of local bridge potentials. This energy-based perspective defines a conservative correction field and contains a boundary reaction term that heuristic stitching omits. To enable efficient inference, we further introduce a Markov-based score approximation that computes the reaction term via a single block-tridiagonal solve, maintaining time complexity linear in the planning horizon. Empirically, ECD achieves state-of-the-art success rates on a range of OGBench stitching tasks, while nearly matching the inference speed of heuristic stitching methods. Code is available at https://github.com/GradientSpaces/ECD.

</details>


### [101] [Online Learning of Robust Legged Odometry with Minimal Exteroceptive Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21669)
*Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Yuze Du,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种即插即用的鲁棒腿式机器人里程计系统，通过利用现成的外感受运动管线作为持续监督信号，训练一个基于本体感受数据的在线学习速度神经网络，并结合不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器（InEKF）融合IMU数据。该系统无需显式的外感受-本体感受标定或运动学建模，在外感受失效时可无缝切换至学习到的本体感受模型，从而在不同四足平台上实现鲁棒的运动估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多传感器融合的腿式机器人里程计需要精密的传感器标定和平台特定的运动学建模，部署复杂；而工业级外感受传感器虽能提供精确运动跟踪，但在感知退化条件下易受影响。因此，需要一种无需标定和运动学建模、且能在感知退化时保持鲁棒性的即插即用里程计系统。

Method: 利用现成的外感受运动管线（如视觉里程计）作为持续监督信号，训练一个在线学习的速度神经网络，该网络仅依赖本体感受数据（如关节编码器、电机电流等）；然后使用不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器（InEKF）融合学习到的本体感受速度（或外感受速度，如果有）和IMU数据，实现鲁棒的状态估计。

Result: 在不同四足平台上展示了该方法与平台无关、易于部署的特性，在多种挑战性场景下保持了鲁棒的运动估计性能。

Conclusion: 该方法消除了对外感受-本体感受标定和运动学建模的需求，通过监督学习实现本体感受速度估计，并利用InEKF融合实现鲁棒切换，为腿式机器人的鲁棒里程计提供了一种即插即用、平台无关的解决方案。

Abstract: Robust locomotion and navigation for legged robots relies heavily on dependable odometry. Traditional multi-sensor fusion for such state estimation requires meticulous sensor calibration and platform-specific kinematic modeling, which complicates deployment. Industrially packaged exteroceptive sensors can provide accurate motion tracking but remain vulnerable to perceptually degraded conditions. We thus develop a plug-and-play, robust legged odometry system that eliminates the need for explicit exteroceptive-to-proprioceptive calibration or system kinematic modeling. Our approach leverages established exteroceptive motion pipelines as a continuous supervisory signal to train an online learned velocity neural network directly from proprioceptive data. An Invariant EKF (InEKF) is then used to fuse the learned proprioceptive or exteroceptive velocity (if any) and IMU data. When exteroception fails due to environmental degradation, the system seamlessly falls back to using the learned proprioceptive model, yielding a resilient legged odometry that readily adapts to new hardware. We demonstrate the platform-agnostic, easily deployable nature of our approach on different quadruped platforms, showcasing promising results in maintaining robust motion estimation across challenging scenarios.

</details>


### [102] [Imitation from Heterogeneous Demonstrations using Grounded Latent-Action World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21672)
*Tianyou Wang,Anson Lei,Joe Watson,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GLAM（接地潜在动作世界模型），通过共享潜在动作空间从异构数据源学习模仿策略，在数据稀缺设置下平均成功率提升48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习受限于演示数据的规模和多样性，现有异构数据协同训练方法依赖启发式人工对齐，缺乏统一的动作表示学习框架。

Method: 构建一对生成模型组成的世界模型，共享潜在动作空间，通过跨数据源一致地预测未来观察来接地该空间；训练BC策略将观察映射到潜在动作并解码为机器人动作。

Result: 在5个操作任务（仿真+真实世界）中，GLAM对齐策略显著优于BC基线和先验潜在动作方法，数据稀缺场景下平均成功率提升48%。

Conclusion: 将动作表示接地于环境效果预测是统一异构数据源的有效原则，GLAM成功实现了跨数据源的动作转移和行为克隆。

Abstract: Imitation learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for learning visuomotor policies, but its generalisation and stability are limited by the scale and quality of demonstration data needed. A promising direction is to leverage more abundant but heterogeneous data sources, which differ in action space and often lack action labels altogether. Existing co-training approaches that combine heterogeneous data sources rely on heuristic and hand-engineered alignment techniques. In contrast, we argue that action representations should be grounded in prediction: actions that produce the same effect on the environment should share the same representation, regardless of their sources. To this end, we instantiate this principle by using a grounded latent-action world model (GLAM), a pair of generative models with a shared latent action space across data sources that is grounded by predicting future observations consistently across sources. This latent action space is used to train downstream behavioural cloning (BC) policies which map observations to latent actions and decode them back to robot actions, providing a paradigm for learning from heterogeneous data. Empirically, we demonstrate that GLAM successfully learns an aligned latent action space that facilitates action transfer across data sources with and without action labels. Across five manipulation tasks in simulation and in the real world, GLAM-aligned policies significantly outperform BC baselines and prior latent-action methods, achieving an average of +48% improvement in task success rate with the same data-scarce setting. Videos and code are available at https://viccccciv.github.io/glam/.

</details>


### [103] [Shape-programmable Magnetic Soft Membranes for Mechanically Active Microchannels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21716)
*Direnc Akyildiz,Fatih Kocabas,Xiangyi Tan,Yunus Alapan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形状可编程的磁性软膜驱动器，可作为微通道的主动组件，在外部磁场作用下动态改变形状，从而实现对层流条件下微流体的主动操控和混合增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微流控平台中通道边界动态控制能力有限，层流条件下流体传输和相互作用受限，亟需一种能远程控制、可变形界面的主动微通道组件。

Method: 采用模板法对磁性软膜进行磁编程，使其在外部磁场下可控制地变形为正弦波形，并将该膜集成到微通道中，将被动通道壁转化为动态可调界面。

Result: 实现了磁性软膜在磁场下的正弦形变控制，成功将被动通道壁转变为可动态变化的界面，实现了主动流体操控和微混合增强。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个无线控制的可变形界面通用平台，为下一代微流控和芯片实验室系统提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: The capability to encode spatially distinct magnetization patterns within soft materials enables remote control over complex deformations. This characteristic is especially important for microfluidic platforms, where limited dynamic control of channel boundaries and laminar flow conditions usually restrict fluid transport and interactions. The present study introduces a shape-programmable magnetic soft membrane actuator as an active microchannel component that can dynamically modulate its shape under magnetic fields and therefore the microfluidic environment. The membrane is magnetically programmed using a template-based approach, which allows it to be controllably deformed in the form of a sinusoid under the influence of an external magnetic field. The membrane's integration into a microchannel converts a passive channel wall into a dynamically changeable interface, allowing active fluid manipulation and enhancing micromixing in laminar flow conditions. The proposed approach establishes a versatile platform for wirelessly controlled deformable interfaces in next-generation microfluidic and lab-on-chip systems.

</details>


### [104] [Programmable magnetic soft robots with controlled locomotion and directional liquid cargo release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21737)
*Youyi Zhou,Zoe Evelyn Gureno,Meghna Majumder,Yunus Alapan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种磁性可编程软体机器人新设计策略，通过优化磁化分布实现定向液体药物释放，同时保持形状变形和运动能力，为胃肠道靶向给药奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁性软体机器人在胃肠道靶向给药中，难以在保持形状变形和运动能力的同时，实现受控的定向液体药物向目标组织界面释放。

Method: 采用优化的空间分布磁化分布曲线使释放界面与外磁场方向对齐；嵌入互连微孔弹性体储存药液；微晶蜡层密封释放孔防止运输泄漏；通过高强磁场机械破裂蜡层实现触发释放。

Result: 实现了定向翻转、运动与触发释放在外磁场方向和强度上的解耦控制，在保持形状变形和运动能力的前提下完成了定向液体药物释放。

Conclusion: 该策略将定向靶向液体释放、形状变形与运动能力集于一体，为胃肠道靶向给药应用建立了基础框架。

Abstract: Magnetically programmable soft elastomers enable complex shape morphing and locomotion dynamics in small scale soft robots under external magnetic fields. Benefiting from their programmed deformation and wireless actuation capabilities, magnetic soft robots have emerged as promising platforms for targeted drug delivery, especially in human gastrointestinal tract. However, achieving controlled directional liquid cargo release toward desired tissue interface while preserving the encoded shape morphing and locomotion capabilities remain a significant challenge. Here, we report a new design strategy that employs an optimized magnetization profile to enable controlled directional release of aqueous cargo without compromising shape morphing and locomotion capabilities. Magnetic soft robots with a specific spatially distributed magnetization profile allow directional alignment of the release interface with the orientation of the external magnetic field. This orientation control ensures active alignment of the release interface toward the intestinal wall prior to drug release. An interconnected microporous elastomer is embedded within the robot for aqueous cargo storage, while a thin microcrystalline wax layer seals the release opening hole to isolate the stored liquid cargo from external environment during transport. Triggered release is achieved by mechanically rupturing the wax sealing layer under a higher magnitude external magnetic field. Controlled directional flipping, locomotion, and triggered release are decoupled through external magnetic field's direction and strength. The controlled directional release strategy reported here integrates directional targeted liquid cargo release, shape morphing, and locomotion, which establishes the groundwork for target drug delivery in gastrointestinal tract applications.

</details>


### [105] [RoverDevKit: An open, physics-grounded tradespace toolkit for conceptual design of lunar micro-rovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21755)
*Jon Reifschneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoverDevKit，一个面向50kg以下月球微型巡视器的开源快速分析评估工具，可在30ms内完成一次任务评估，并作为多目标优化器的适应度函数。通过NSGA-II帕累托前沿分析揭示了不同任务场景下的关键设计权衡，并挑战了六轮构型在重质量下更优的传统预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型月球巡视器（<50kg）的Phase-A前概念设计受制于紧密耦合的机动性、功耗、热控和质量权衡，而现有概念设计工具要么是专有的、要么缺乏基准验证、要么优化速度太慢，无法驱动设计优化。

Method: 开发了RoverDevKit开源分析评估器，集成了地面力学、质量、功耗、热控生存和遍历任务建模，单次运行仅需30ms。使用NSGA-II多目标优化算法，在月海、极地、高地和陨石坑边缘四种场景下生成帕累托前沿。通过单轮牵引力实验数据验证地面力学内核、自底向上质量模型与已公布巡视器数据对比、以及真实巡视器的重新发现检验进行基准测试。

Result: 不同任务场景下的关键设计权衡不同：高纬度以储能为约束、高地松散风化层以斜坡牵引力为约束、月海和陨石坑边缘以遍历距离为约束。刚性四轮构型在整个质量范围内帕累托支配六轮构型，六轮摇臂式悬架仅在增加障碍导航需求时才进入帕累托集。质量模型中位绝对误差13.3%。将地面力学误差传播至优化器后，定性设计规则不变。

Conclusion: RoverDevKit是一种快速、开源、经过验证的微型巡视器概念设计工具，可驱动多目标优化。挑战了六轮构型在重质量下更优的传统认知，揭示了任务环境对设计权衡的主导作用。工具、数据、验证结果和可视化脚本均已开源。

Abstract: Pre-Phase-A design of lunar micro-rovers is dominated by tightly coupled mobility, power, thermal, and mass trades, yet conceptual-design tooling for the rapidly growing sub-50 kg class is typically proprietary, weakly benchmarked, or too slow to drive optimization. We contribute RoverDevKit, an open analytical evaluator coupling terramechanics, mass, power, thermal survival, and traverse that runs in 30ms per mission, fast enough to serve directly as a multi-objective optimizer's fitness function. Across mare, polar, highland, and crater-rim scenarios, NSGA-II Pareto fronts show that the binding design trade changes with mission profile within a single mass class: energy storage dominates at high latitude, slope traction on loose highland regolith, and traverse range on mare and crater-rim missions. Notably, rigid four-wheel layouts Pareto-dominate the full modeled mass range under smooth-regolith range-mass-slope objectives, contrary to the expectation that six-wheel architectures become optimal at heavier masses; six-wheel rocker-bogie layouts enter the Pareto set only once missions impose an obstacle-navigation requirement. The evaluator performance is benchmarked using both component and system checks: the terramechanics kernel matches measured single-wheel drawbar pull within the literature model-form band on two independent datasets, the bottom-up mass model predicts published in-class (5-50 kg) rover masses to 13.3% median absolute error, and a rediscovery check places real micro-rovers near the optimizer's fronts. Propagating the measured terramechanics error through the optimizer leaves the qualitative design rules unchanged. The tool, data, validation artifacts, and figure-generation scripts are released openly.

</details>


### [106] [A Novel Bio-Inspired Fish Robot with Tunable Stiffness via Particle Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21771)
*Jack Stonecipher,Allen Gao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于粒子堵塞技术的可快速调刚度的仿生鱼机器人，实现了在0至-40 kPa真空压力下54%的弯曲刚度变化。实验表明，低频（1-1.5 Hz）时软体（0 kPa）速度最大、运输成本最低；高频（2.5-3 Hz）时硬体（-40 kPa）性能更优。主动刚度调节对实现高效高速游泳至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼类通过主动调节身体柔韧性实现不同速度下的高效游泳，受此启发，探索可调刚度对游泳性能的影响机制。

Method: 设计了一种基于粒子堵塞技术的可快速调刚度仿生鱼机器人，在形状和体积几乎不变的情况下实现刚度快速调整；通过运动追踪系统测量速度和运输成本（CoT），并可视化不同刚度下摆动体的中线曲率。

Result: 在0至-40 kPa真空压力下实现了54%的弯曲刚度变化；低频（1-1.5 Hz）时软体（0 kPa）速度最大且CoT最小，高频（2.5-3 Hz）时硬体（-40 kPa）速度更优且CoT更低；主动刚度调节在1-3 Hz拍频范围内对维持高效高速游泳至关重要。

Conclusion: 刚度调节是仿生机器人实现自适应高效推进的关键策略。

Abstract: Fish achieve efficient swimming across varied speeds through active modulation of their body flexibility. To explore the effects of tunable stiffness on swimming performance, we present a bio-inspired freely swimming fish robot with a rapidly tunable particle-jamming body. This design enables rapid stiffness adjustments with negligible changes in shape or volume, achieving a 54% variation in flexural rigidity across vacuum pressures of 0 to -40 kPa. We visualize the midline of the oscillating body under both low- and high-stiffness conditions, and the comparison confirms that the body curvature varies with stiffness. We further experimentally evaluate the tunable stiffness body's effects on swimming performance using velocity and cost of transport (CoT) measurements obtained via a motion tracking system. Results show that active stiffness tuning is essential for sustaining efficient and high-speed swimming across beating frequencies of 1-3 Hz. At low frequencies (1-1.5 Hz), a softer body (0 kPa) maximizes velocity and minimizes CoT, whereas at high frequencies (2.5-3 Hz), a stiffer body (-40 kPa) delivers superior velocity and reduced transport cost. These findings highlight stiffness modulation as a key strategy for adaptive and efficient propulsion in bio-inspired robotic swimmers.

</details>


### [107] [Rotation-Aware Point-Cloud Embeddings for Vision-Based In-Hand Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21788)
*Yashom Dighe,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种旋转感知的点云嵌入方法，将点云间欧氏距离与SO(3)测地线误差对齐，使得无模型强化学习策略能够直接从当前和目标点云嵌入、本体感知和质心元数据中学习灵巧手内重定向，而无需显式姿态、相对姿态、稠密光流或教师动作监督，在实验中达到了与特权状态和蒸馏基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始点云目标条件因点云无序性、重采样和可见性依赖等问题，导致策略学习条件不良；现有方法需引入额外结构（如显式姿态、稠密光流或教师蒸馏）来弥补这一缺陷。本文旨在消除这些外部模块，通过表示学习本身来解决点云目标条件的问题。

Method: 学习一个旋转感知的点云嵌入空间，使得该空间中的欧氏潜距离与物体方向间的SO(3)测地线误差对齐。模型-free RL策略以当前点云嵌入、目标点云嵌入、本体感知和质心元数据作为输入，输出动作，无需物体姿态、相对姿态、稠密光流或教师动作监督。

Result: 在手内重定向实验中，所提方法与使用特权状态和蒸馏的基线方法性能相当，同时避免了测试时计算结构化的姿态或光流输入。此外，实验表明通用视觉点云预训练不足以支持当前-目标点云比较，因为它丢弃了任务相关的状态信息而仅保留了形状特征。

Conclusion: 点云目标条件在灵巧手内重定向任务中变得可行，关键在于表示本身编码了任务相关的旋转几何信息，而非依赖外部模块。这为简化灵巧操作系统的输入接口提供了新思路。

Abstract: Point-cloud goals provide a direct way to specify dexterous in-hand reorientation: instead of defining an object-specific pose frame or estimating 6D pose at test time, the policy is given the desired 3D geometry of the object. Yet raw point-cloud goal conditioning is poorly conditioned for policy learning. Current and goal clouds are unordered, independently sampled, and often visibility-dependent, so their discrepancy entangles object rotation with permutation, resampling, and unstable correspondence structure. For this reason, prior point-cloud manipulation methods typically add structure outside the representation itself, such as explicit pose or relative-pose inputs, dense flow features, or distillation from privileged teachers. We close this gap by learning a rotation-aware point-cloud embedding whose Euclidean latent distance is calibrated to the SO(3) geodesic error between object orientations. The resulting representation turns current-goal comparison into a smooth control signal, allowing a model-free RL policy to act from current and goal point-cloud embeddings, proprioception, and centroid metadata, without object pose, relative pose, dense flow, or teacher-action supervision. In in-hand reorientation experiments, this interface matches privileged-state and distillation-based baselines while avoiding brittle test-time computation of structured pose or flow inputs. These results suggest that point-cloud goals become practical for this task when the representation, rather than an external module, encodes the task-relevant geometry of rotation. We also show evidence that generic visual point-cloud pretraining is insufficient for such a current-goal comparison because it discards the task-relevant state and preserves only shape features.

</details>


### [108] [THREAD: Trajectory Planning for Hybrid Rigid-Soft Manipulators with Environment-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21792)
*Shivani Kamtikar,Pranav Asthana,Naveen Kumar Uppalapati,Girish Krishnan,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出THREAD，首个基于扩散模型的混合刚柔机械臂轨迹规划器，通过学习生成先验并结合物理损失，在窄孔穿线任务中实现92.4%成功率，碰撞减少5倍，并支持跨实体迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合刚柔机械臂面临两个规划挑战：自由空间可行的背骨形状在接触环境下不可行，以及独立规划刚柔段忽略其运动学耦合。现有方法难以解决这些问题。

Method: 采用扩散模型学习基于局部环境几何条件的物理可实现背骨轨迹生成先验，设计包含曲率、平滑度和碰撞约束的物理启发损失函数，联合优化刚柔两段轨迹。

Result: 仿真中任务成功率92.4%，碰撞次数比最强基线减少5倍；通过最小在线更新实现跨实体真实世界迁移，成功穿过仅1.3倍软段直径的窄孔。

Conclusion: THREAD有效解决了混合刚柔机械臂在受限环境中的轨迹规划难题，扩散模型结合物理损失的方法具有高成功率和强泛化能力。

Abstract: Manipulation in confined environments, such as threading a manipulator through narrow apertures, remains a fundamental challenge, especially for conventional rigid robots. Hybrid rigid-soft manipulators offer promise but face two compounding planning challenges: backbone shapes feasible in free space become infeasible under environmental contact, and planning rigid and soft segments independently ignores their kinematic coupling. We present THREAD, the first diffusion-based trajectory planner for hybrid manipulation, learning a generative prior over physically realizable backbone trajectories conditioned on local environment geometry, with physics-inspired losses encoding curvature, smoothness, and collision constraints jointly across both segments. Trained in simulation, THREAD achieves 92.4% task success with 5x fewer collisions than the strongest baseline. We show cross-embodiment real-world transfer with minimal online updates, successfully threading through apertures as small as 1.3x the soft segment diameter.

</details>


### [109] [SurGE: Surrogate Gradient-guided Evolution for Co-design of Legged Robots with Parallel Elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21866)
*Yulun Zhuang,Yue Qin,Justin Lu,Zelin Shen,Yichen Wang,Sicheng He,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SurGE框架，通过可微管道（Kino-SRB模型+设计感知控制策略）计算代理梯度，并将其注入CMA-ES（通过均值平移+余弦退火步长衰减），解决了含弹性元件的腿式机器人协同设计中接触动力学不可微的问题。在跳跃机器人4-DOF设计空间中，SurGE相比原始CMA-ES将跨种子标准差降低6倍、种群集中度提升18%，同时匹配或改善最优目标值。硬件实验在2D设计子空间上，从手工调优初始设计出发，将设计目标降低37.65%，且仿真中识别的改进趋势成功迁移至物理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含弹性元件的腿式机器人协同设计面临接触动力学和机构啮合不可微的挑战，导致传统基于梯度的优化方法难以应用，而非可微的进化算法（如CMA-ES）收敛速度慢且不稳定。

Method: SurGE框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 可微管道：由运动动力学单刚体（Kino-SRB）模型和设计感知控制策略组成，可计算设计目标的代理梯度；(2) 代理梯度注入：通过均值平移（mean shift）将代理梯度信息注入CMA-ES，指导种群更新方向；(3) 余弦退火步长衰减：对梯度注入步长采用余弦退火调度，平衡优化初期探索与后期收敛。

Result: 仿真实验：在4-DOF设计空间中，SurGE相比原始CMA-ES跨种子标准差降低6倍，种群集中度提升18%，最优目标值匹配或更优。硬件实验：在2D设计子空间上，从手工调优初始设计出发，设计目标降低37.65%，且仿真中识别的改进趋势成功迁移到物理系统。

Conclusion: SurGE通过代理梯度有效加速了腿式机器人中不可微的协同设计问题，在仿真和硬件实验中均验证了其优于原始CMA-ES的收敛稳定性和优化效果，展示了从仿真到物理系统的成功迁移能力。

Abstract: Co-design of legged robots with elastic elements is challenging due to the non-differentiability of contact dynamics and mechanism engagement. This paper presents SurGE, a framework that computes surrogate gradients of the design objective through a differentiable pipeline consisting of a kinodynamic single-rigid-body (Kino-SRB) model and a design-aware control policy, and injects them into CMA-ES via mean shift with cosine-annealed step decay. On a 4-DOF design space of a hopping robot with unidirectional parallel spring, SurGE achieves 6 times lower cross-seed standard deviation and 18% tighter population concentration compared to vanilla CMA-ES, while matching or improving the best objective. Hardware experiments on a 2D design subspace show that, starting from a hand-tuned initial design, SurGE reduces the design objective by 37.65% on hardware, with the improvement trend identified in simulation transferring consistently to the physical system. SurGE provides the potential to accelerate non-differentiable co-design problems in legged robots via surrogate model gradients.

</details>


### [110] [Predictive Gaze Is Preserved but Reorganized toward Monitoring during Robot-Mediated Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21920)
*Manuela Uliano,Silvia Fattorini,Marco Controzzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究通过远程操作实验发现，目标导向的眼动在机器人操控中仍保持预测性，但视觉注意力会重新分布，增加了对机械末端和操作对象的在线监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在通过机器人而非自身身体执行动作时，人类视觉运动系统中的预期性和任务驱动性眼动行为是否得以保留，以及视觉运动控制如何适应改变的具身状态。

Method: 通过远程操作实验，对比人类在自身操控与机器人中介操控下的眼动行为模式，分析注视行为与任务目标的对齐程度及注意力分布变化。

Result: 远程操作中，注视仍与任务目标高度对齐，保持预测性角色；但视觉注意力系统性重新分布，增强了对机器人末端执行器和操作对象的在线监控。

Conclusion: 预测性注视在改变的具身状态下并未丧失，而是根据感觉反馈和控制需求的变化进行重组；揭示了人类视觉运动系统在自然感觉运动耦合被破坏时的灵活性，并指出眼动可作为人机交互中推断动作意图的信息信号。

Abstract: Goal-directed eye movements are a fundamental component of visuomotor control, enabling humans to anticipate and guide their actions. Whether this anticipatory and task-driven behavior is preserved when actions are executed through a robot rather than through one's own body remains unclear. Here we address this question by investigating gaze behavior during goal-directed telemanipulation to determine how visuomotor control adapts to altered embodiment. Our findings show that gaze remains strongly aligned with task goals, preserving its predictive role even during robot-mediated manipulation. At the same time, teleoperation systematically redistributes visual attention toward the robotic end-effector and manipulated objects, increasing online monitoring. These findings show that predictive gaze is not lost under altered embodiment, but reorganized in response to changes in sensory feedback and control demands. More broadly, they reveal the flexibility of the human visuomotor system when the natural sensorimotor coupling is disrupted and identify gaze as an informative signal for inferring action intentions in human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [111] [CoRDE: Concept-Prior Routed Diffusion Experts for Structural Generalization in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21935)
*Haidong Huang,Xixin Zhao,Yaohua Zhou,Jiayu Song,Jiayi Zhang,Jun Ma,Haiyue Zhu,Xiaocong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRDE提出了一种概念先验引导的变分蒸馏框架，通过冻结的概念编码器提取语义分布，指导混合专家路由，并利用LoRA参数高效专家池避免参数爆炸，在机器人模仿学习的多任务和长程场景中实现了语义对齐的路由分配和优越的动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在机器人模仿学习中缺乏结构泛化能力，多任务和长程场景下存在梯度冲突；纯数据驱动的MoE方法存在路由崩溃和参数爆炸问题。

Method: 提出CoRDE框架：(1) 从冻结的概念编码器中提取语义分布，通过可学习的软映射矩阵指导变分后验责任分配；(2) 引入熵控制的责任推断机制，在可靠语义预测下鼓励自信路由，同时保留随机扩散项以维持行为多样性；(3) 在共享冻结主干上使用LoRA构建参数高效专家池。

Result: 理论分析证明了混合分数差异受责任加权局部专家误差约束；实验验证CoRDE系统性地减少了路由崩溃，形成了鲁棒的语义对齐专家分配，实现了更优的动作质量和增量学习效率。

Conclusion: CoRDE通过结构引导的变分蒸馏框架有效解决了多任务和长程机器人模仿学习中的路由崩溃和参数膨胀问题，实现了语义对齐的路由分配。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at capturing multi-modal action distributions in robot imitation learning. However, in multi-task and long-horizon scenarios, monolithic architectures lack structural generalization capabilities, suffering from gradient conflicts between distinct semantic sub-stages. While pure data-driven Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) methods introduce labor division, they frequently trigger routing collapse, and instantiating full-scale experts causes parameter explosion and high expansion costs. To address these issues, we propose Concept-prior Routed Diffusion Experts (CoRDE), a structure-guided variational distillation framework. CoRDE extracts semantic distributions from a frozen concept encoder to guide the variational posterior responsibility via a learnable soft mapping matrix. This mechanism introduces an entropy-controlled responsibility inference process that encourages confident routing under reliable semantic predictions while preserving the stochastic diffusion term for behavioral diversity. To overcome parameter inflation, CoRDE employs a parameter-efficient expert pool using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on a shared frozen backbone. Theoretical analysis shows that the mixture score discrepancy is bounded by responsibility-weighted local expert errors, supporting high-fidelity generation under low-rank expert adaptation. Empirical evaluations confirm that, compared to existing baselines, CoRDE systematically reduces routing collapse, forming robust, semantically aligned expert allocations while achieving superior action quality and incremental learning efficiency.

</details>


### [112] [RARM: Confidence-Gated Progress Reward Modeling for RL in Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22027)
*Pengzhi Yang,Xinyu Wang,Pengyu Jing,Kehan Wen,Yiduo Qu,Zhenhao Huang,Minghao Fu,Xin Liu,Yaheng Shen,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出参考锚定奖励模型(RARM)，一种轻量级视觉比较器，通过将单个成功演示转化为密集的进度感知奖励。RARM仅需在通用视频上训练一次，使用对比时间目标，无需机器人特定数据或任务特定奖励标签。在9个模拟任务和4个真实世界任务中，RARM在后续RL训练中取得了最佳成功率，尤其在长时域任务如布料折叠中表现显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作强化学习常受奖励设计瓶颈制约，稀疏成功奖励监督弱，手工密集奖励泛化差且设计繁琐。现有基于进度的奖励模型需要任务特定演示或进度标签，且可能对视觉上合理但物理上错误的状态给予高奖励。

Method: 提出RARM，一种轻量级视觉比较器，将单个成功演示转化为密集进度感知奖励。训练阶段在通用视频上使用对比时间目标进行单次训练；部署阶段将rollout片段与参考片段匹配，仅奖励置信的前向进度，抑制可能导致假阳性奖励的不确定匹配。

Result: 在LIBERO和MetaWorld的9个模拟任务以及4个真实世界任务中，RARM在后续RL训练中取得最佳成功率，尤其在对不可靠进度估计敏感的长时域任务（如布料折叠）中表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: RARM通过轻量级视觉比较器将单个演示转化为密集奖励，有效解决了奖励设计瓶颈，无需任务特定数据或手工奖励工程，在长时域任务中尤其有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for robot manipulation is often bottlenecked by reward design, especially in long-horizon tasks: sparse success rewards provide weak supervision, while hand-crafted dense rewards are tedious to design and generalize poorly across tasks. Progress-based reward models offer a promising alternative by estimating how far an observation has advanced toward task completion, but existing approaches often require task-specific demonstrations or progress labels, and can assign high rewards to visually plausible but physically incorrect states. We introduce the Reference-Anchored Reward Model (RARM), a lightweight visual comparator that converts a single successful demonstration into a dense, progress-aware reward. RARM is trained once on general-purpose videos with a contrastive temporal objective, requiring no robot-specific data, task-specific reward labels, or per-task reward engineering. At deployment, RARM matches rollout clips to reference clips and rewards only confident forward progress, suppressing uncertain matches that may otherwise produce false-positive rewards. Across 9 simulated manipulation tasks from LIBERO and MetaWorld and 4 real-world tasks, RARM achieves the best overall success rates in subsequent RL training, with particularly large gains on long-horizon tasks such as cloth folding, where unreliable progress estimates are especially harmful.

</details>


### [113] [Deep RL- Tuned Mo del-Free Adaptive Control for Lower-Limb Exoskeletons During Sit-to-Stand Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22040)
*Ranjeet Kumbhar,Appaso M. Gadade,Rajmeet Singh,Ashish Singla,Ravinder Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对双侧下肢外骨骼在坐-站（STS）运动中的智能无模型自适应反步控制策略，结合超局部二阶模型、高斯RBF神经网络和TD3强化学习增益调度器，在MATLAB/Simulink与Simscape Multibody联合仿真中取得了最低平均RMSE 0.078度，相比PID、MFAC、LQR、SMC分别提升60.2%、54.4%、48.7%、42.6%，TD3集成进一步将髋、膝、踝关节跟踪误差分别降低35%、33%、79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STS转换对老年人下肢关节负荷大，是外骨骼辅助的关键目标；但STS过程中人-外骨骼交互动力学复杂、时变且受个体差异影响，基于模型的控制方法难以实际应用。

Method: 采用超局部二阶模型避免显式系统辨识；高斯RBF神经网络在线估计未知集总动力学；TD3强化学习智能体作为监督增益调度器，根据STS不同阶段自适应调整控制器增益。

Result: 所有关节平均RMSE为0.078度，优于PID（提升60.2%）、MFAC（54.4%）、LQR（48.7%）、SMC（42.6%）；TD3集成使髋、膝、踝跟踪误差分别降低35%、33%、79%。

Conclusion: 所提控制器设计在辅助外骨骼STS控制中有效且鲁棒。

Abstract: Sit-to-stand (STS) transitions impose significant joint-loading demands on elderly individuals, making them a primary target for lower-limb exoskeleton assistance. However, accurate trajectory tracking during STS is challenging due to complex, time-varying human exoskeleton interaction dynamics and inter-subject variability that render model-based control approaches difficult to apply in practice. This paper presents an intelligent model free adaptive backstepping control strategy for a bilateral lower-limb exoskeleton during STS motion. The proposed controller design uses an ultra-local second-order model to avoid explicit system identification, while a Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) neural network estimates the unknown lumped dynamics online. To further improve phase-aware tracking performance, a Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) reinforcement learning agent is integrated as a supervisory gain scheduler that adaptively adjusts controller gains across the distinct phases of STS motion. The proposed controller is evaluated through co-simulation in MATLAB/Simulink and Simscape Multibody using OpenSim-derived reference trajectories and benchmarked against state-of-the-art controllers. Results demonstrate that the proposed controller achieves the lowest average RMSE of 0.078 degree across all joints, representing improvements of 60.2%, 54.4%, 48.7%, and 42.6% over proportional integral derivative (PID), model-free adaptive control (MFAC), linear quadratic regulator (LQR), and sliding-mode control (SMC), respectively. TD3 integration further reduces tracking error by 35%, 33%, and 79% at the hip, knee, and ankle joints compared to the standalone RBF-MFAC baseline. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed controller design for assistive exoskeleton control during STS transitions.

</details>


### [114] [A Multimodal Tiltwing Framework for Bioinspired Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22046)
*Krispin C. V. Broers,Sophie F. Armanini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适应性倾转机翼框架，使仿蜂鸟扑翼微型飞行器能够在悬停、高速定向飞行和高效滑翔之间切换，通过独立双翼推力矢量控制、混合式苏格兰轭扑动机构、被动翼旋转机构和非接触式前缘追踪传感器，实现了宽扑动角度、有效控制和增强机动性，实验表明单翼在188°振幅下可产生21.1 gf平均升力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扑翼微型飞行器受限于专用设计，飞行包络狭窄且续航能力差，缺乏能够灵活切换多种飞行模式（悬停、高速飞行、高效滑翔）的通用平台。

Method: 提出适应性倾转机翼框架，采用两个独立推进单元实现推力矢量控制；开发混合式苏格兰轭扑动机构以提供对称运动轮廓和宽扑动角度；实现被动翼旋转机构与双翼推力矢量结合；设计非接触式前缘追踪传感器用于翼方位反馈和滑翔模式下二面角控制。

Result: 单翼在188°扑动幅度、全油门条件下，使用3.1 g 1S无刷直流电机产生21.1 gf平均升力；倾转能力覆盖大范围角度，推力矢量控制架构具有线性和对称的力矩响应曲线。

Conclusion: 该倾转机翼框架有效扩展了扑翼飞行器的飞行模式切换能力，提高了机动性和能效，为未来微型飞行器的精确优化和多功能飞行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tailless flapping-wing micro-aerial vehicles (FWMAVs) mimic the impressive flight performance of hummingbirds, utilising unsteady aerodynamic effects. However, existing designs are still limited and purpose-built with a restricted flight envelope and poor endurance. We therefore propose an adaptable tiltwing framework enabling bioinspired aerial robots to switch between hovering flight, high-speed directional flight, and energy-efficient gliding flight. The proposed framework utilises thrust vectoring with a wide actuation range via two fully independent propulsion units, each flapping a single wing, for effective control and enhanced manoeuvrability. For this, we developed a hybrid Scotch-yoke-based flapping mechanism that ensures a symmetric motion profile with a modular design guaranteeing an arbitrarily wide flapping angle to exploit the lift-enhancing clap-and-fling effect. Additionally, we implemented a passive wing-rotation mechanism, which, in combination with our dual-wing thrust-vectoring approach, allows unprecedented wing-design freedom, unlocking potential for precise optimisation. A contactless leading-edge tracking sensor provides accurate feedback on the wing's orientation and, in the gliding mode, enables dihedral-angle control, augmenting the active wing-pitch control. Extensive testing of a propulsion unit was conducted with a six-axis force/torque sensor, demonstrating the flapping mechanism's performance while optimising transmission efficiency and the passive wing-pitch mechanism. At full throttle, the average lift force generated by a single wing, flapping with a 188° amplitude, was 21.1 gf for a small 3.1 g 1S BLDC motor. Additional tests covering the full range of the wide-angle tilting capability showed an effective thrust-vectoring control architecture with a linear and symmetric response curve of the moments generated.

</details>


### [115] [GeoFlow-SLAM++: A Robust Multi-Camera Visual-Inertial SLAM System with Relocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22051)
*Wei Fen,Tingyang Xiao,Liu Liu,Xiaolin Zhou,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoFlow-SLAM++ 是一个紧耦合的多相机视觉惯性SLAM系统，支持ORB和基于SuperPoint/LightGlue的神经网络特征前端，通过多相机重投影约束、IMU预积分、跨视角地点识别和双流跟踪实现鲁棒定位，并可选集成伪深度约束，在多个数据集上达到接近LiDAR的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目和RGB-D视觉惯性SLAM系统受限于视野有限、传感器特定失效模式以及跨会话重定位不可靠等问题。

Method: 将GeoFlow-SLAM从单RGB-D传感器扩展为紧耦合的多相机视觉惯性SLAM系统，采用统一机体中心公式，支持ORB和SuperPoint+LightGlue神经网络特征两种前端，融合多相机重投影约束、IMU预积分、跨视角地点识别和双流光流/NN特征跟踪，可选引入跨视图一致的伪深度预测作为辅助几何约束。

Result: 在EuRoC、OpenLORIS、TUM、Hilti及自采集手持多相机数据集上评估，NN-Feature前端改善了外观挑战场景的鲁棒性，多相机公式在Hilti上取得有竞争力的定位精度，统一跨视角重定位设计在手持数据集上达到与LiDAR相当的性能。

Conclusion: 多相机视觉惯性SLAM系统能有效克服单目/RGB-D系统的视野限制和传感器失效问题，在鲁棒性和精度上取得显著提升，跨视角重定位可达LiDAR级别性能。

Abstract: Monocular and RGB-D visual-inertial SLAM systems remain susceptible to limited field of view, sensor-specific failure modes, and unreliable cross-session relocalization. To address these issues, we present GeoFlow-SLAM++, a tightly coupled multi-camera visual-inertial SLAM system that extends GeoFlow-SLAM from a single RGB-D sensor to a calibrated multi-camera rig with a unified body-centric formulation. Within this multi-camera framework, GeoFlow-SLAM++ supports two interchangeable visual front-ends: a conventional ORB front-end and a neural network feature (NN-Feature) front-end built on SuperPoint and LightGlue. The system unifies tracking, mapping, and relocalization on a shared body state, and combines multi-camera reprojection constraints, IMU pre-integration, cross-view place recognition, and dual-stream optical flow/NN-Feature tracking for robust localization. As an optional extension, the system can further incorporate cross-view-consistent pseudo-depth predictions from RGB images as auxiliary geometric constraints. We evaluate GeoFlow-SLAM++ on EuRoC, OpenLORIS, TUM, Hilti, and a self-collected handheld multi-camera dataset. Results show that the NN-Feature front-end improves robustness in appearance-challenging scenarios, the multi-camera formulation achieves competitive localization accuracy on Hilti, and the unified cross-view relocalization design reaches LiDAR-comparable performance on the handheld dataset.

</details>


### [116] [How Should a Simulation-to-Reality Transfer Budget Be Spent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22062)
*Syed Hamzah Rizvi,Yash Vardhan Tomar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仿真到现实迁移中，系统辨识（系统测量）与域随机化（训练广度）之间的权衡。通过在隐藏参数的摆锤模拟实验中，发现少量真实数据即可显著缩小迁移差距，且一旦有真实数据，在估计参数处训练比扩大随机化范围效果更好。建议先测量可辨识的参数，仅对剩余不确定性进行随机化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在机器人学习的 sim-to-real 迁移中，测量真实系统（系统辨识）与扩展现模拟动态范围（域随机化）之间的资源分配权衡。

Method: 在受控的 sim-to-sim 摆锤环境中进行实验，使用隐藏参数模型模拟物理机器人，通过系统辨识 rollout 数量与随机化分布宽度的系统扫描来研究权衡。

Result: 测量预算起着主要作用：少量辨识 rollout 即可缩小大部分迁移差距；一旦有真实数据，在估计参数处训练优于扩宽随机化范围；包含真实系统的广泛随机化无法替代测量。

Conclusion: Sim-to-real 流程应优先测量可辨识的参数，仅对剩余不确定性进行随机化，而非盲目扩大随机化范围。

Abstract: Simulation-to-reality transfer, often called sim-to-real transfer, is a central challenge in robot learning. Yet, the tradeoff between measuring a system more accurately and training over a broader range of simulated dynamics is still poorly understood. In this work, we focused on the allocation of real-robot measurement time between system identification and domain randomization. We studied this tradeoff in a controlled sim-to-sim pendulum setting, where a hidden-parameter model stands in for the physical robot, and the experiment sweeps identification rollouts against the width of the randomization distribution. Across the reality gaps and noise levels we tested, the measurement budget did most of the work. A small number of identification rollouts closed most of the transfer gap, and once any real data was available, policies performed best when trained at the estimated parameters rather than over a widened randomization band. Broad randomization that contained the true system still did not substitute for measurement. These results hold in a benign regime where the dynamics are identifiable and only two parameters are unknown, so structural model mismatch remains the setting where randomization breadth may become more valuable. Overall, our results suggest that sim-to-real pipelines should first measure the parameters they can and reserve randomization for the uncertainty that remains.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamics, stability, and energy efficiency of an energy-recycling rimless wheel with spring-clutch legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22073)
*Tongchen Lin,Yanqiu Zheng,Chuhan Zhang,Ruigang Chen,Yizhar Or,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带弹簧离合腿的能量回收无辐轮，通过锁定离合器存储足地碰撞后的弹性势能并在下一步态周期释放，仿真显示能效提升16.13%，原型样机在1°斜坡上实现被动行走，CoT约0.02。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无辐轮在行走过程中足地碰撞会造成能量损失，降低行走效率。本文旨在通过弹簧离合机构回收碰撞中的弹性势能并重新利用，从而提高步行机器人的能效。

Method: 提出带可锁止离合器的弹簧腿能量回收无辐轮机构；建立混合动力学模型；通过数值仿真分析周期步态的局部稳定性和运输成本（CoT）；制作原型样机在倾斜平面上进行被动行走实验。

Result: 仿真表明，与粘弹性腿无辐轮相比，能量回收模型CoT降低16.13%；与刚性无辐轮相比，两种弹性腿模型CoT均降低50%以上；能量回收模型在测试的坡度和刚度范围内维持局部稳定周期步态。实验验证了该机构在1°斜坡上实现被动行走，CoT约0.02。

Conclusion: 所提出的弹簧离合机构能够提高能量回收无辐轮的模拟行走效率，原型实验证实了该机构实现被动行走的可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes an energy-recycling rimless wheel with spring-clutch legs. The proposed mechanism uses a lockable clutch to store part of the impact-induced elastic energy after foot contact and reinject it in the next gait cycle. First, we develop a hybrid dynamic model of the energy-recycling rimless wheel. Second, numerical simulations are used to examine the dynamics, local stability of periodic gaits, and the Cost of Transport (CoT) of the proposed mechanism. The simulation results show that the proposed mechanism reduces the CoT by up to 16.13% compared with a benchmark viscoelastic-legged rimless wheel with telescopic spring-damper legs. Compared with the rigid rimless wheel, the viscoelastic-legged and energy-recycling models reduce the CoT by more than 50%. The energy-recycling model also maintains locally stable periodic gaits over the tested slope and stiffness ranges. Finally, prototype experiments on an inclined plane are conducted to examine the feasibility of the proposed mechanism. The experimental results show that the proposed rimless wheel achieves passive walking on a shallow 1° slope, corresponding to a CoT of approximately 0.02. These results suggest that the proposed spring-clutch mechanism can improve the simulated walking efficiency of the energy-recycling rimless wheel, while the prototype experiments support the feasibility of passive walking with the mechanism.

</details>


### [118] [ACEsplat: Accelerated 3D Gaussian Scene Regression via RGB and Poses Only](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22091)
*Mingkai Liu,Haohua Que,Dikai Fan,Haojia Gao,Tianle Zhu,Handong Yao,Qian Zhang,Ruopeng Zhang,Xianliang Huang,Fei Qiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACEsplat 是一种无需外部3D先验（如SfM点云或深度图），仅依赖RGB图像和相机位姿，即可快速完成每场景3D高斯重建的优化框架。它通过自监督场景坐标回归模块构建内部几何先验，再结合轻量级高斯初始化头进行3DGS优化，在15-25分钟内完成场景重建，在静态渲染和稀疏视角新视图合成任务上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的每场景3D高斯重建方法（如3DGS）通常依赖外部几何初始化（如SfM点云或深度估计），这些外部先验在机器人或AR场景的现场部署中速度慢且脆弱，限制了其实用性。本文旨在设计一种仅需RGB图像和相机位姿、无需外部3D先验的快速场景重建方案。

Method: ACEsplat 采用两阶段流水线：(1) 自监督场景坐标回归（SCR）模块，在4-5分钟内构建内部几何先验；(2) 将SCR特征与坐标先验通过轻量级高斯初始化头融合，随后进行每场景3DGS优化。整个流程无需预计算的SfM模型或有监督深度图。

Result: 在Wayspots数据集上，使用实时SLAM位姿达到29.11 dB PSNR；在Cambridge Landmarks数据集上，使用SfM精炼位姿达到33.20 dB PSNR。在RealEstate10K稀疏视角（2视图）新视图合成任务上取得了有竞争力的图像保真度。整个场景重建在单GPU上15-25分钟内完成。

Conclusion: ACEsplat 提供了一种仅需RGB+位姿的实用场景重建方案，无需外部3D先验，速度快且效果有竞争力，适合机器人和混合现实应用中的快速场景部署。

Abstract: Per-scene 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity rendering, but practical robotic and AR scene capture pipelines often depend on external geometric initialization (e.g., SfM point clouds or depth estimates), which can be slow and brittle in on-site deployment. We present ACEsplat, a fast per-scene optimization framework that reconstructs 3D Gaussian representations from RGB images and camera poses only, without requiring external 3D priors (e.g., precomputed SfM models or supervised depth maps). ACEsplat uses a two-stage pipeline: (1) a self-supervised scene coordinate regression (SCR) module builds an internal geometry prior within 4--5 minutes; (2) SCR features and coordinate priors are fused by a lightweight Gaussian initialization head, followed by per-scene 3DGS optimization. On static-view rendering, ACEsplat achieves 29.11 dB PSNR on Wayspots with real-time SLAM poses and 33.20 dB on Cambridge Landmarks with SfM-refined poses. On RealEstate10K sparse-view novel view synthesis, it achieves competitive image fidelity under a challenging 2-view setting. ACEsplat completes scene-specific SCR mapping and 3DGS reconstruction within 15--25 minutes on a single GPU, making it a practical RGB+pose-only solution for rapid scene setup in robotics and mixed-reality applications.

</details>


### [119] [KITE: Decoupling Kinematics and Interaction for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22113)
*Qianxu Wang,Kuan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KITE提出了一种将操作解耦为与具体形态无关的任务推理和与具体形态相关的运动控制的方法，通过接触模式学习到的交互意图潜表征连接两者，实现了零样本的跨形态操作策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机器人形态的操作策略泛化困难，因为标准策略将任务推理与特定形态的运动控制纠缠在一起，导致在新形态上需要重新收集大量演示数据。

Method: KITE通过解耦框架实现跨形态迁移：1) 一个共享策略从源形态的演示中预测潜在交互意图（基于接触模式的隐表征）；2) 意图条件化的动作解码器利用每个形态的运动学模型生成具体动作。适应新形态时只需训练新的动作解码器，无需重新收集演示数据。

Result: 在三类操作任务（平行夹爪、灵巧手、复合形态）的迁移实验中，KITE一致地实现了对结构不同的目标形态的零样本迁移，在迁移成功率和任务-形态覆盖范围上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: KITE通过解耦任务推理与形态特定运动控制，有效地实现了零样本跨形态操作策略迁移，显著提升了策略在不同机器人形态间的泛化能力。

Abstract: Generalizing manipulation policies across robot embodiments remains difficult because standard policies entangle task reasoning with embodiment-specific motor control. We study zero-shot cross-embodiment manipulation, where a policy trained on source embodiments must be deployed on a structurally different target embodiment without additional task demonstrations. We introduce Kinematic Interaction Transfer across Embodiments (KITE), which decouples manipulation into embodiment-agnostic task reasoning and embodiment-specific motor control, connected through a learned latent representation of interaction intent based on contact patterns. Task reasoning is performed by a shared policy that predicts latent intents from source demonstrations, while motor control is performed by an intent-conditioned action decoder learned from each embodiment's kinematic model. With KITE, adaptation to a new embodiment requires only training a new action decoder using its kinematic model, without recollecting demonstration data. We evaluate KITE on three manipulation tasks spanning transfer between parallel grippers, dexterous hands, and composite embodiments. KITE consistently achieves zero-shot transfer to structurally different target embodiments, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines in transfer success and task-embodiment scope.

</details>


### [120] [DeformX: A Versatile Co-Simulation Framework for Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22116)
*Yi Yang,Xiang Fei,Lehong Wang,Chenhao Li,Zilin Dai,Henry Kou,Lu Li,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeformX是一个将Cosserat杆物理引擎与NVIDIA Isaac Sim集成的协同仿真框架，实现了可变形线性物体的高保真物理仿真和视觉真实感渲染，支持机器人学习管线，并在实际线缆分割和绳索摆动任务中展现了出色的sim-to-real迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DLO仿真方法存在视觉与物理的割裂：基于视觉的方法缺乏物理真实的形变行为，而基于物理的机器人学习方法通常将DLO近似为刚性链或通用软体，无法准确捕捉细长弹性结构的弯曲、扭转和剪切力学特性。

Method: 提出DeformX协同仿真框架，集成了专用的Cosserat杆物理引擎（模拟DLO动力学、自碰撞及与任意自由曲面网格的接触交互）与NVIDIA Isaac Sim；采用网格蒙皮技术将离散杆变形映射到导入的CAD模型上，实现高保真可视化。

Result: 在DeformX生成数据上微调SAM3，真实图像线缆分割mAP@75提升10.2%；完全在DeformX中训练的绳索摆动策略在UR5e机械臂上真实世界试验的平均目标命中误差为6.6 cm。

Conclusion: DeformX是首批统一真实感可视化、严格物理原理和机器人学习管线兼容性的DLO仿真框架之一，在合成数据生成和策略学习方面具有广泛适用性，并展现了强大的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Deformable linear objects (DLOs) such as wires, cables, and ropes are common in robotic manipulation tasks, yet simulating them with both visual realism and physical accuracy remains challenging. Existing visual simulation methods typically rely on procedural geometric primitives that lack physically grounded deformation behavior, while physics-based approaches with robot learning support often approximate DLOs as rigid-link chains or generic soft bodies, failing to accurately capture the bending, twisting, and shear mechanics of slender elastic structures. In this work, we introduce DeformX, a co-simulation framework that integrates a dedicated Cosserat rod physics engine with NVIDIA Isaac Sim, enabling DLO simulations that are both physically faithful and visually realistic. Our Cosserat rod engine simulates the dynamics and self-collisions of DLOs, and contact interactions with arbitrary free-form meshes. To achieve high-fidelity visualization, we employ mesh skinning to map discrete rod deformations onto imported CAD models. To the best of our knowledge, DeformX is the one of the first frameworks for DLO simulation that unifies realistic visualization, principled physics, and compatibility with robot learning pipelines. We demonstrate its versatility across synthetic data generation and policy learning for DLO manipulation, and validate visual and physical fidelity through comparisons against real-world experiments. Notably, fine-tuning Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3) on DeformX-generated data yields a 10.2% mAP@75 improvement in real-image wire segmentation, and a rope-swinging policy trained entirely in DeformX achieves a mean target-hitting error of 6.6 cm on a UR5e manipulator in real-world trials, highlighting its strong sim-to-real transfer capability.

</details>


### [121] [Wh0: Generative World Models as Scalable Sources of Egocentric Human Hand Manipulation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22136)
*Yangtao Chen,Zixuan Chen,Peiyang Wang,Yong-Lu Li,Jing Huo,Jieqi Shi,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Wh0提出利用生成式视频世界模型作为可扩展且可控的源，生成人手交互的egocentric视频数据，通过手部运动重建和视觉编辑转化为机器人可训练监督信号，与少量真实机器人数据联合训练，显著提升预训练灵巧VLA模型在18个真实灵巧操作任务上的零样本泛化能力（从8.3%到38.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作数据来源存在规模与场景/本体对齐之间的权衡：遥操作数据对齐性好但成本高，仿真数据可扩展但受sim-to-real差距限制，真实egocentric视频可扩展但与机器人部署不对齐。

Method: Wh0框架包含：(1) 利用生成式视频世界模型，以语言、物体和场景为条件，生成50K条egocentric人-物交互视频数据集WM-H；(2) 通过手部运动重建和视觉编辑将生成视频转化为机器人可训练监督信号；(3) 与少量真实机器人数据联合训练，使预训练VLA模型适应灵巧操作部署。

Result: 在18个真实灵巧操作任务上，仅使用机器人数据训练的模型零样本成功率为8.3%，Wh0提升至38.9%。消融实验表明可扩展生成和场景/本体对齐是性能提升的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 生成式视频世界模型可以作为可扩展且可控的源来弥合人类数据和机器人部署之间的对齐差距，为灵巧操作提供有效的训练数据。

Abstract: Scaling dexterous manipulation requires generalization across objects, scenes, and tasks, yet existing data sources face a trade-off between scale and scene/embodiment alignment: teleoperation data is well aligned with robot deployment but expensive to collect; simulation is scalable but limited by the sim-to-real gap; and real egocentric videos scale effectively but remain misaligned with robot deployment. We propose Wh0, a framework that uses generative video world models as scalable and controllable sources of egocentric human-hand manipulation data to unlock the manipulation capabilities of pretrained dexterous VLA models. Conditioned on language, objects, and scenes, Wh0 uses a generative world model to produce WM-H, a 50k-episode dataset of egocentric human-object interaction videos. Wh0 then converts the generated videos into robot-trainable supervision through hand motion reconstruction and visual editing. Co-trained with a limited amount of real robot data, WM-H adapts pretrained VLA models to dexterous manipulation deployment. Across 18 real-world dexterous manipulation tasks, compared with a model post-trained only on robot data, Wh0 improves zero-shot success on unseen tasks from 8.3% to 38.9%. Ablation studies further show that scalable generation and scene/embodiment alignment are key drivers of performance gains. Videos and open-source code can be found on our project website: https://chenyt31.github.io/wh0.github.io/.

</details>


### [122] [RoboLineage: Agent-Native Data Lifecycle Governance Across Robot Policy Iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22142)
*Qian Luo,Wentao Guo,Zhennan Qin,Nanchun Guo,Yunhan Zhao,Yi Ma,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboLineage 是一个面向机器人策略迭代的智能体原生数据生命周期治理系统，通过将数据收集、审查、训练、评估等环节建模为类型化血缘工件，使策略迭代流程更快速、可审计，并开源供不同机器人平台使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人策略通过反复的数据收集、审查、重新训练、评估和发布决策来改进，但连接这些步骤的证据通常分散在本地工具、脚本和专家记忆中，缺乏系统化的生命周期管理。

Method: 将 rollout、审查、数据集决策、训练运行、策略元数据、评估、部署建议以及下一轮收集计划等环节表示为类型化的血缘工件（lineage artifacts），由智能体解释具身 rollout 证据、适配已有训练栈、维护数据健康，并在显式的工件边界下汇总跨迭代状态。

Result: 在实际机器人操作流程中，RoboLineage 使常规策略迭代更快、更可审计，同时保持下游策略性能不受影响。

Conclusion: RoboLineage 作为轻量级生命周期层开源，可适用于不同的机器人形态和训练框架，为机器人策略的持续迭代提供了系统化的数据治理方案。

Abstract: We present RoboLineage, an agent-native data lifecycle governance system for robot policy iteration. Modern robot policies improve through repeated data collection, review, retraining, evaluation, and release decisions, but the evidence connecting these steps is often scattered across local tools, scripts, and expert memory. RoboLineage makes this lifecycle explicit by representing rollouts, reviews, dataset decisions, training runs, policy metadata, evaluations, deployment recommendations, and next-collection plans as typed lineage artifacts. Agents interpret embodied rollout evidence, adapt accepted data to existing training stacks, maintain data health, and summarize cross-iteration state under explicit artifact boundaries. In real-robot manipulation workflows, RoboLineage makes routine policy iteration faster and more auditable while maintaining downstream policy performance. We open source RoboLineage as a lightweight lifecycle layer for different robot embodiments and training families. Project page: https://robolineage.github.io/

</details>


### [123] [Physics-Informed Eikonal Caging for Whole-Arm Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22143)
*Yan Zhang,Yiming Li,Yifei Dong,Florian T. Pokorny,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将抓取笼（caging）重新定义为最小时间逃逸问题，利用物理信息神经网络求解eikonal方程得到连续的逃逸时间场，并将其嵌入到全身操作规划中，以提升对接触模型不匹配的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身接触操作规划因复杂接触动力学难以精确建模而充满挑战，现有抓取笼方法难以融入基于连续优化的操作规划，亟需一种不依赖精确接触模型的几何鲁棒性规划原则。

Method: 将caging重新表述为最小时间逃逸问题，定义连续逃逸时间场度量机器人对物体的包围质量，证明该场满足eikonal方程；采用物理信息神经网络（PINN）近似该场，获得光滑可微表示并直接嵌入操作规划目标函数，以偏好能抵抗物体逃逸的机器人构型。

Result: 在仿真和实物实验中，相比基线方法，所提方法在对抗扰动和接触模型不匹配方面展现出更强的鲁棒性，支持使用准动态近似和简化几何模型的简化接触模型进行规划。

Conclusion: 几何包围可作为全身操作规划中一种实用的鲁棒性原语，有效提升对接触模型误差的容忍度。

Abstract: Planning contact-rich whole-arm manipulation is challenging because interactions that involve extended robot geometry give rise to complex contact dynamics that are difficult to model accurately. This creates a need for planning principles that do not rely heavily on precise contact models. Caging offers one such geometric notion of robustness to modeling inaccuracy by restricting object escape through geometrically enclosing the object. However, existing caging formulations are difficult to incorporate into continuous optimization-based manipulation planning. We reformulate caging as a minimum-time escape problem in which the object seeks to leave an enclosing robot geometry in the shortest time. This yields a continuous escape-time field that measures the robot's enclosure quality and we show it satisfies an eikonal equation. We therefore can approximate this field using a physics-informed neural network, producing a smooth differentiable representation that can be embedded directly into manipulation planning. The resulting objective supports whole-arm manipulation planning to favor robot configurations resisting object escape. This improves the manipulation robustness to contact model mismatch, thus enabling planning with simplified contact models, including quasi-dynamic approximations and simplified object geometry. Across simulation and real-world experiments, we show improved robustness to disturbances and contact-model mismatch relative to baselines. These results suggest that geometric enclosure can serve as a practical robustness primitive for whole-arm manipulation. A supplementary video, which includes an intuitive overview of our method and experiment video results, is available on our project webpage.

</details>


### [124] [Full Nonlinear Nonholonomic Dynamics and Motion Analysis of a 3-DoF Underactuated Spherical Rolling Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22169)
*Lakshmesha Krishnapa,Ahnaf Sharaar Mazahar,Seyed Amir Tafrishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了MonoRollBot——一种由单电机、丝杠传动和弹簧耦合内部移动质量驱动的3自由度球形滚动机器人的完整非线性约束动力学模型，这是首个针对此类单驱动、超欠驱动球形滚动机器人的完整非线性非完整模型之一。模型使用拉格朗日-达朗贝尔方法推导，仿真结果与原型行为定性吻合，揭示了重力、柔顺性和惯性如何共同塑造该强欠驱动机器人的运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏针对单驱动、超欠驱动球形滚动机器人的完整非线性非完整动力学模型，现有研究未能充分揭示重力、柔顺性和惯性对这种强欠驱动机器人运动行为的联合影响。

Method: 采用拉格朗日-达朗贝尔方法建立动力学模型：将丝杠关系作为完整约束施加，将滚动条件以Pfaffian形式作为非完整约束处理；完整保留了壳体平移、壳体姿态、丝杠行程、螺母旋转和径向质量运动的广义坐标。

Result: 仿真和代表性运动研究表明该模型与原型行为定性一致，揭示了重力、柔顺性和惯性如何共同塑造该强欠驱动机器人的运动能力。

Conclusion: 该模型为未来针对此类非完整单驱动滚动机器人的状态估计和混合控制器设计提供了力学一致的基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a full nonlinear constrained dynamic model of MonoRollBot, a novel 3-DoF spherical rolling robot driven by a single motor, a lead-screw transmission, and a spring-coupled internal moving mass, together with motion analysis of its behavior. To the best of our knowledge, this is one of the first full nonlinear nonholonomic models reported for a mono-actuated, super-underactuated spherical rolling robot of this kind. Because rolling without slipping is nonholonomic, the dynamics are derived using the Lagrange--d'Alembert formulation, with the lead-screw relation imposed as a holonomic constraint and the rolling condition imposed in Pfaffian form. The formulation retains the complete generalized coordinates of shell translation, shell attitude, screw travel, nut rotation, and radial mass motion. Simulations and representative motion studies show qualitative agreement with prototype behavior and reveal how gravity, compliance, and inertia jointly shape the locomotion and motion capabilities of this strongly underactuated robot. The resulting model also provides a mechanically consistent basis for future state estimation and hybrid controller design for this nonholonomic mono-actuated rolling robot.

</details>


### [125] [OpenHLM: An Empirical Recipe for Whole-Body Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22174)
*Yingdong Hu,Haodong Zhu,Boyuan Zheng,Yihang Hu,Tong Zhang,Zunhao Chen,Junming Zhao,Ruiqian Nai,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenHLM，一套面向全身人形机器人操作-移动的完整方案，包含全身遥操作、视觉-语言-动作模型设计及异构协同训练三阶段，在长时域任务上以不到一半的演示时间超越GR00T N1.6和Ψ₀两个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身人形机器人操作-移动系统通常将上下半身解耦为独立控制器，限制了全身协调性，导致行为类似于轮式双臂平台。本文旨在探索如何构建原生全身视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，直接将语言和像素映射到人形机器人所有自由度。

Method: 采用系统性实证研究，按"一次改变一个变量"的实验路线图分三个阶段进行：全身遥操作、VLA模型设计、异构协同训练。具体包括：基于关节的全身遥操作接口；在静态和轮式双臂平台上预训练的VLA迁移到人形机器人全动作空间；使用HuMI（人形机器人版UMI）进行协同训练扩展策略泛化能力。

Result: 基于关节的全身遥操作接口优于仅暴露部分自由度的方案；在静态/轮式双臂平台预训练的VLA能惊人地迁移到人形机器人全动作空间；HuMI协同训练可在无需额外全身遥操作数据的情况下泛化到新物体和指令。OpenHLM在极具挑战的长时域任务上以不到基线一半的演示时间超越GR00T N1.6和Ψ₀。

Conclusion: OpenHLM提供了一套开源的人形机器人全身操作-移动方案核心技术路线，证明了全身原生VLA模型的可行性和有效性，为未来人形机器人全身协调控制提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid loco-manipulation requires coordinating the robot's entire kinematic chain. However, most existing systems typically decouple the upper and lower bodies into separate controllers, limiting such coordination and yielding behaviors similar to those of a wheeled dual-arm platform. In this paper, we ask what it takes to build a whole-body native vision-language-action (VLA) model that maps language and pixels directly to all of the humanoid's degrees of freedom. We conduct a systematic empirical study organized as a roadmap of one-variable-at-a-time experiments across three phases: whole-body teleoperation, VLA model design, and heterogeneous co-training. Our study yields several intriguing findings: a joint-based whole-body teleoperation interface outperforms alternatives that only partially expose the humanoid's degrees of freedom; a VLA pretrained on static and wheeled dual-arm platforms transfers surprisingly well to a humanoid's full action space; and co-training with HuMI, the humanoid analog of UMI, extends the policy to new objects and instructions without additional whole-body teleoperation on those targets. Following this roadmap yields OpenHLM, an open-source recipe for whole-body humanoid loco-manipulation. In a challenging long-horizon task that spans a wide vertical range of the humanoid, OpenHLM outperforms two state-of-the-art humanoid VLA baselines (GR00T N1.6 and $Ψ_0$) using less than half the total demonstration time. Our code, training data, and model checkpoints are available at [https://openhlm-project.github.io/].

</details>


### [126] [Geometric Reconstruction of Extrinsic Contact Trajectories using Tactile Sensing and Proprioception for Tool Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22251)
*Seojung Min,Yoonjin Kim,Jeong-Jung Kim,Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用触觉传感器和机器人本体感知，在单点接触假设下，通过几何推理方法重建工具末端在操作过程中的外部接触轨迹，实现了平均轨迹RMSE为8.59 mm、形状RMSE为5.96 mm的在线估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多工具介导的操控任务中，决定任务成功与否的交互发生在远离触觉传感器的工具尖端，直接感知工具与环境之间的接触信息十分困难，尤其是当接触点在交互过程中移动时。

Method: 将工具尖端轨迹重建建模为单点接触假设下的几何推理问题。首先通过校准段估计全局工具尖端接触位置，然后利用连续接触下触觉标记观测估计的相对工具运动，合成完整轨迹。

Result: 在51次试验中，考虑了多种轨迹、工具、腕部姿态和抓取配置，该方法在世界坐标系下达到轨迹RMSE 8.59±2.41 mm，形状RMSE 5.96±1.16 mm，在线运行频率14.00±4.11 Hz。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，可以从抓取层面的触觉传感中稳定恢复工具尖端的外部轨迹几何形状，且轨迹形状在不同工具、腕部姿态和抓取配置下保持稳定。

Abstract: Tactile sensing enables robots to perceive rich contact information at the grasp, supporting tasks such as object recognition, in-hand pose estimation, and slip detection. However, in many tool-mediated manipulation tasks, the interaction that determines task success occurs at the tool tip, away from the tactile sensor, making direct sensing of tool-environment contact difficult, particularly when the contact moves during interaction. In this work, we reconstruct the trajectory of extrinsic tool-tip contact using tactile sensing and robot proprioception. We formulate tool-tip trajectory reconstruction as a geometric inference problem under a single-point contact assumption. Our method first estimates the global tool-tip contact location from a calibration segment designed to approximate fixed-point behavior, and then reconstructs the full trajectory by composing relative tool motion estimated from tactile marker observations under continuous contact. Across n=51 trials with multiple trajectories, tools, wrist poses, and grasp configurations, the proposed pipeline achieves a trajectory RMSE of 8.59 +/- 2.41 mm in the world frame and a shape RMSE of 5.96 +/- 1.16 mm, while operating online at 14.00 +/- 4.11 Hz. Overall, the results show that extrinsic tool-tip trajectory geometry can be recovered consistently from grasp-level tactile sensing, with trajectory shape remaining stable across variations in tools, wrist poses, and grasp configurations.

</details>


### [127] [FlowDPG: Deterministic Policy Gradient on Flow Matching Policies for Real-World Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22303)
*Kexin Shi,Junyao Shi,Poorvi Hebbar,Zhuolun Zhao,Tarun Amarnath,Yifan Su,Shikhar Bahl,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowDPG提出了一种免BPTT的DDPG风格算法，通过将评论家梯度蒸馏到速度场中，实现了流匹配策略的稳定策略改进，在双臂AirPods装配任务上达到92%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将策略梯度方法应用于流匹配策略时，需要沿多步ODE进行反向传播（BPTT），这在计算上代价高昂且数值上不稳定，严重限制了流匹配策略在真实世界机器人操作任务中的应用。

Method: 提出FlowDPG，一种专门为流匹配策略设计的DDPG风格方法。核心思想是在训练时将评论家梯度蒸馏到速度场中，从而完全绕过BPTT。该方法结合了两种互补的向量：由演示驱动的速度场（保持动作可行性）和由评论家驱动的修正（引导动作向更高价值方向移动）。

Result: 在长时域、多阶段、双臂AirPods装配任务上，FlowDPG达到了92%的端到端成功率，显著优于最近的价值条件化、辅助模块自适应和伴随法评论家梯度等方法。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：(1) 免BPTT的蒸馏框架，实现了流匹配策略上稳定的DDPG风格策略改进；(2) 通过三个显式近似建立了FlowDPG更新方向与标准确定性策略梯度之间的形式化联系；(3) 在真实世界任务上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Real-world reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation remains challenging, and this difficulty is amplified for flow matching policies: applying policy gradient methods to these policies is fundamentally limited by the need to backpropagate through time(BPTT) along the multi-step ODE that maps noise to actions, which is computationally prohibitive and numerically fragile. We propose FlowDPG, a DDPG-style method specifically designed for flow matching policies that distills the critic gradient into the velocity field at training time, bypassing BPTT entirely. Intuitively, FlowDPG combines two complementary vectors: the demonstration-driven velocity that keeps the action feasible, and the critic-driven correction that steers it toward higher value. Our contributions are threefold: (1) a BPTT-free distillation framework that enables stable DDPG-style policy improvement on flow matching policies, (2) a formal connection between the FlowDPG update direction and vanilla Deterministic Policy Gradient via three explicit approximations, and (3) real-world validation on a long-horizon, multi-stage, dual-arm AirPods assembly task, where FlowDPG attains a 92% end-to-end success rate, substantially outperforming recent RL methods spanning value-conditioning, auxiliary-module adaptation, and adjoint-based critic-gradient approaches. Videos and more results are provided on the project page https://flowdpg.github.io.

</details>


### [128] [EmbodiedUS-FS: Fast Slow Intelligence for Ultrasound Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22319)
*Fangzhuo Zhang,Xinyu Wang,Xiao Yang,Jinchang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种快慢分层具身超声系统（Fast and Slow Hierarchical Embodied Ultrasound System），通过慢脑进行意图解析和阶段式任务规划，快脑融合多模态反馈执行局部动作，并集成安全防护与分层升级策略，在真实临床动态环境中实现了安全、可解释的机器人超声辅助操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器人超声扫描需要同时具备高层临床工作流推理和低层闭环执行能力，而自然语言指令往往隐含解剖目标、手术逻辑、图像质量要求及安全约束，且执行过程受患者运动、接触变化和目标漂移等动态因素影响，现有系统难以兼顾规划与执行的安全性与鲁棒性。

Method: 采用快慢分层架构：慢脑（Slow Brain）通过API和手册语料进行知识增强，完成意图解析和阶段式任务规划，构建任务图并执行结构化计划验证生成可执行计划；快脑（Fast Brain）融合超声图像、机器人位姿与力状态、患者运动等多模态反馈，细化局部动作并执行基于图像质量的恢复行为；系统额外集成安全防护（Safety Shield）和分层升级策略，在持续失败或安全边界违反时限制风险动作并触发重规划或人工确认。

Result: 在规划评估、动态扰动下的闭环执行以及安全机制验证实验中，该分层设计有效提升了任务成功率，同时减少了安全违规事件。

Conclusion: 快慢分层具身架构结合安全防护机制，能够在真实临床动态环境中实现安全、可解释且鲁棒的机器人超声辅助操作，为临床机器人超声系统的实用化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound scanning in real clinical environments requires both high-level clinical workflow reasoning and low-level closed-loop execution. Physicians natural-language instructions often contain implicit anatomical targets, procedural logic, image-quality requirements, and safety constraints, while execution is affected by patient motion, contact variations, and target drift. We propose a fast and slow hierarchical embodied ultrasound system for safe and interpretable robotic ultrasound assistance. The Slow Brain performs intent parsing and stage-wise task planning with knowledge augmentation from an API and handbook corpus, and generates executable plans through task-graph construction and structured plan verification. The Fast Brain fuses multimodal feedback, including ultrasound images, robot pose and force states, and patient-motion information, to refine local actions and perform image-quality-guided recovery behaviors. The system further integrates a Safety Shield and a hierarchical escalation policy to constrain risky actions and trigger replanning or human confirmation under persistent failures or safety-bound violations. Experiments on planning evaluation, closed-loop execution under dynamic perturbations, and safety-mechanism validation demonstrate that the proposed hierarchical design improves task success rates while reducing safety violations.

</details>


### [129] [Tactile Genesis: Exploring Tactile Sensors at Scale for Learning Dexterous Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22332)
*Trinity Chung,Kashu Yamazaki,Dhruv Patel,Alexis Duburcq,Yiling Qiao,Katerina Fragkiadaki,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tactile Genesis，一个GPU并行触觉传感器仿真平台，支持多种触觉模态、可配置参数和真实噪声模型，通过在大规模去手操作任务上的消融实验发现：传感器覆盖范围比类型和分辨率更重要，每触点力/力矩是最有效的传感类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感对于灵巧操作至关重要，但不同触觉抽象对策略的影响以及更丰富的触觉场是否值得其硬件成本尚不明确。由于每种传感器实际上定义了一个新机器人，没有实验室能在所有传感器上复现相同的学习实验，因此需要统一的仿真平台进行系统研究。

Method: 基于GPU并行的触觉传感器仿真平台Tactile Genesis，统一接口下支持二值接触、接触深度、每触点运动学力/力矩、弹性体标记位移、几何感知接近度、接触音频和体素化温度场（首创），具备可配置的放置位置、分辨率和真实噪声模型（漂移、迟滞、死触元、串扰）。在单个GPU上支持超过20,000个并行环境和1,000个触元，吞吐量比先前仿真器提升3-20倍。

Result: 在三个灵巧操作任务上训练师生策略，消融传感器类型、放置位置、分辨率和噪声。结果发现：仅靠本体感觉不足以完成所有任务；传感器放置位置主导传感器类型——仅指尖覆盖远落后于全手覆盖，而添加手掌和近端指骨可缩小与特权教师的差距；分辨率远不如覆盖范围重要——在全手放置200个触元即可满足任务需求；每触点力/力矩最有用。

Conclusion: 为未来灵巧操作中的触觉硬件设计和策略端观测选择提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is critical for contact-rich dexterous manipulation, yet it remains unclear which tactile abstractions a policy needs and when richer tactile fields justify their hardware cost. This is hard to study empirically: each sensor effectively defines a new robot, and no lab can replicate the same learning experiment across all of them. We present Tactile Genesis, a GPU-parallel tactile sensor simulation platform that exposes binary contact, contact depth, per-taxel kinematic force/torque, elastomer marker displacement, geometry-aware proximity, contact audio, and a voxelized temperature field (the first of its kind in robot learning physics simulation platforms) under a common interface, with configurable placement, resolution, and a realistic noise model (drift, hysteresis, dead taxels, crosstalk). It scales past 20,000 parallel environments and 1,000 taxels on a single GPU, improving throughput by 3 to 20 times over previous tactile simulators. We train teacher-student policies on three dexterous tasks, ablating sensor type, placement, resolution, and noise, and verify transfer to the real XHand1. Proprioception alone is insufficient on every task. Sensor placement dominates sensor type: fingertip-only coverage trails whole-hand coverage by a wide margin, while adding the palm and proximal phalanges closes most of the gap to the privileged teacher. Resolution matters far less than coverage: placing 200 taxels across the whole hand suffices across tasks. We find that force/torque per taxel is consistently the most useful sensor type. These results give concrete guidance for both future tactile hardware design for improving robot hands and policy-side observation choice in dexterous manipulation. https://neuroagents-lab.github.io/2026-tactile-genesis/

</details>


### [130] [Multi-AUV Marine Life Tracking with Single Hydrophone Payloads via a Hidden Markov Model Equipped Particle Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22335)
*Christopher Herrera,Kehlani Fay,Christopher Clark,Alberto Soto,Christopher Lowe,Mario Espinoza*

Main category: cs.RO
