Downstream suitability of scene-graph forests for robotics applications is demonstrated in an open-vocabulary object-retrieval task utilizing both ScanNet data and a real-world indoor deployment using a Boston Dynamics Spot. This paper leverages foundation models to create more expressive, semantically deep 3D hierarchical scene graphs and demonstrates their potential to advance semantic and environmental understanding in robotics.

</details>


### [168] [When Robots Rate Their Own Interactions: Engagement Validity and the Strangeness Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23339)
*Victor Lockwood,Hasan Mahmud,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh,Prabu David,Jamison Heard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"反向评估"方法，让LLM驱动的机器人从自身视角完成HRI问卷，发现LLM在参与度维度上与人类评分有中高度一致性（满意度r≤.65，享受度r≤.72），但在舒适/陌生维度上系统性地反转了人类评分（r=-.44至-.67），表明当前LLM机器人缺乏评估内部情感状态的能力，需要补充生理信号、视线、距离等模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI评估完全依赖人类问卷，忽略了机器人视角。作者提出让LLM机器人从自身角度完成标准化问卷，检验其评分是否与人类标注一致，以探索LLM作为交互评估者的边界条件。

Method: 研究1：让5个LLM对HRI-CUES数据集中的25个交互完成HRI-CUES、Godspeed、RoSAS三项问卷（共1,522次评估）。研究2：在Nao机器人上部署Claude Sonnet 4.5进行现场实时交互评估（N=4），并进行逐轮评估。使用相关系数、重测信度（ICC）等指标进行一致性检验。

Result: LLM在参与度维度上达到中高度一致性（满意度r≤.65，享受度r≤.72），重测信度优异（ICC≥.82）。但在舒适/陌生维度上系统性地反转了人类评分（r=-.44至-.67，p<.05），将参与度与舒适度相混淆。该失败模式在5个模型、合成对照及两台参与者的实体部署中均持续存在。

Conclusion: 当前LLM驱动的机器人缺乏获取内部情感状态的通路，无法准确评估如"陌生感"等构念。反向评估需要补充生理信号、视线、距离等模态以超越行为代理指标。这些发现确立了LLM作为HRI评估者的边界条件。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) evaluation relies almost exclusively on human-completed questionnaires, leaving the robot's perspective unexamined. We propose an \textit{inverted evaluation}, in which LLM-powered robots complete the same standardized instruments from their own perspective, and test whether these ratings agree with human ground truth. In Study~1, five LLMs completed HRI-CUES, Godspeed, and RoSAS questionnaires for 25~interactions ($N = 1{,}522$ evaluations) from the HRI-CUES dataset. LLMs achieved moderate-to-strong agreement on engagement dimensions (satisfaction $r$ up to $.65$ and enjoyment $r$ up to $.72$) with excellent test-retest reliability (ICC $\geq .82$), but \textit{systematically inverted} the comfort/strangeness dimension ($r = -.44$ to $-.67$, all $p < .05$), conflating engagement with comfort. In Study~2, a Nao robot running Claude~Sonnet~4.5 replicated these patterns in live interactions ($N = 4$), including real-time turn-by-turn assessment. The strangeness failure persisted across five models, synthetic controls, and embodied deployment for two participants. We argue that current LLM-based robots lack access to the internal affective states needed to assess constructs like strangeness, and that inverted evaluation requires supplementary modalities (e.g., physiological signals, gaze, proxemics) to move beyond behavioral proxies. These findings establish boundary conditions for using LLMs as interaction evaluators in HRI.

</details>


### [169] [TSD: A Physics-Inspired Trajectory Saliency Detector for Efficient Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23371)
*Yiming Zhao,Gongrui Ma,Qingkai Li,Mingguo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory Saliency Detector (TSD)，一种无需训练、即插即用的轨迹显著性检测框架，通过空间熵和向心加速度两种物理指标识别操作任务中的过渡、精确和敏捷运动，并基于此实现数据集压缩（平均减少25%数据且性能相当或更优）和扩展策略，为高效机器人学习提供低成本、可扩展的信息密集数据集构建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作领域的模仿学习面临高质量数据稀缺的挑战，主要原因在于数据采集成本高昂。现有方法未能充分利用轨迹数据本身的异构性来应对这一问题。

Method: 提出Trajectory Saliency Detector (TSD)框架。首先将操作任务中的运动分为过渡型、精确型和敏捷型三类，并将后两类定义为轨迹显著性。TSD基于两种物理指标：空间熵（捕捉细粒度操作）和向心加速度（检测敏捷操控），无需训练、即插即用。基于TSD进一步设计了数据集压缩和数据集扩展两种策略。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境的广泛实验中，基于TSD压缩后的数据集训练出的模型在平均减少25%数据量的情况下，性能与完整数据集相当甚至更优。数据集压缩和扩展策略均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: TSD提供了一条可扩展且成本高效的路径，用于构建信息密集的数据集，从而提升机器人学习的效率。

Abstract: For imitation learning in robotic manipulation, high data collection costs result in the scarcity of high quality data. In this paper, we leverage the inherent heterogeneity of trajectories to address this challenge. Based on our observations of manipulation tasks, we categorize motions into transitional, precise, and agile types, defining the latter two as trajectory saliency due to their criticality to task success in contrast to the prevalent but less relevant transitional motions. Therefore, we propose the Trajectory Saliency Detector (TSD), a training-free and plug-and-play framework to identify trajectory saliency. TSD employs two physically-grounded metrics: spatial entropy to capture fine-grained manipulation and centripetal acceleration to detect agile maneuvering. We further leverage TSD to develop a dataset compression method that reduces training costs and a dataset expansion strategy that improves data collection efficiency. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that models trained on TSD-condensed datasets achieve comparable or even superior performance with 25% less data on average. These results validate the effectiveness of our dataset compression and expansion strategies, thereby confirming the utility of TSD. Consequently, TSD offers a scalable and cost-effective pathway to synthesize information-dense datasets for efficient robot learning. Project page: https://trajectory-saliency-detector.github.io/trajectory-saliency-detector/

</details>


### [170] [Flowing With Purpose: Latent Action Guided Flow Matching Policies For Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23420)
*Bruno Machado,Alexandre Chapin,Emmanuel Dellandrea,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对流匹配行为克隆中全局固定各向同性高斯分布与机器人动作空间异方差结构不匹配的问题，提出LAFM框架，用潜在动作模型指导的自适应先验分布库替代单一高斯分布，在真实机器人部署中提升23.4%成功率，在LIBERO-90基准上提升10.4%，以更小架构超越大规模预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配策略使用全局固定的各向同性源分布，但机器人动作空间具有高度碎片化和异方差结构，这种系统性结构不匹配导致模型学习高度纠缠的向量场，训练效率低下，限制了策略性能。

Method: LAFM框架引入自适应先验分布库替代单一高斯分布；通过潜在动作模型将当前观测映射到离散运动基元，选择结构对齐的专业化基分布作为去噪过程的初始化，实现动态自适应性以适应人类示教中的异方差性。

Result: 真实机器人部署中任务成功率提升23.4%，LIBERO-90基准上提升10.4%；以显著更小的架构超越大规模预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过自适应先验分布库解决流匹配中源分布与动作空间结构不匹配问题，验证了动态自适应初始化对异方差动作空间的有效性，为机器人操作中的行为克隆提供了新范式。

Abstract: Flow matching has recently become a new standard for behavior cloning in robotic manipulation. However, state-of-the-art flow matching policies suffer from a systematic structural mismatch: they rely on a globally fixed isotropic source distribution despite the strongly fragmented and heteroscedastic structure of robotic action spaces. This agnostic initialization forces the model to learn highly entangled vector fields, bottlenecking training efficiency and limiting overall policy performance. To address this limitation, we introduce Latent Action Guided Flow Matching (LAFM), a novel framework that replaces the monolithic Gaussian with an adaptive library of learned prior distributions. By grounding these distributions using a latent action model, LAFM maps current observations to discrete motion primitives, selecting a specialized base distribution that provides an informed, structurally aligned initialization for the denoising process. This dynamic adaptivity naturally accommodates heteroscedasticity in human demonstrations and makes transport trajectories shorter and less entangled. Empirically, LAFM substantially outperforms standard flow matching formulations, increasing task success rates by 23.4% in real-world robotic deployments and by 10.4% on the LIBERO-90 benchmark. Furthermore, we demonstrate that LAFM achieves state-of-the-art results, surpassing massively pre-trained vision-language-action models while utilizing significantly smaller architectures.

</details>


### [171] [DexTeleop-0: Force-Aware Bimanual Dexterous Teleoperation with Ego-Centric Perception towards Shared Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23431)
*Haichao Liu,Yuyao Jiang,Hyunsun Park,Yuanjiang Xue,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于触觉驱动的自适应策略DexTeleop-0，通过在遥操作管线中引入实时优化循环，利用触觉传感和操作空间雅可比矩阵弥合具身鸿沟，实现了双臂灵巧手的精细操作，在抓取、抗干扰和复杂灵巧任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥操作系统在接触密集任务中存在具身鸿沟导致运动映射不准确、缺乏触觉力和触觉反馈的问题，使得高精度任务的数据采集效率极低。

Method: 提出触觉驱动的自适应策略，在双臂灵巧框架DexTeleop-0中引入实时优化循环，通过触觉传感估计精确接触点，利用指尖力传感轮廓，结合操作空间雅可比矩阵计算相对于关节角度更新的局部修正，将粗略的人类跟踪意图转化为精确的力合规机器人指令。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实硬件上，该方法在鲁棒抓取、抗干扰操作和复杂灵巧任务中的任务成功率和执行效率均优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 触觉驱动的自适应策略能有效弥合具身鸿沟，提升双臂灵巧遥操作在接触密集任务中的性能。

Abstract: Fine-grained, bimanual dexterous manipulation remains a foundational challenge in robotics. Traditional teleoperation systems often fail in contact-rich tasks because embodiment gaps hinder accurate kinematic mapping, while tactile and force feedback remain absent. Consequently, data collection efficiency for high-precision tasks remains prohibitively low. To address these limitations, we propose a tactile-driven adaptation strategy designed to enable fine-grained manipulation on top of teleoperation pipelines. Instantiated within our bimanual dexterous framework, DexTeleop-0, this strategy introduces a real-time optimization loop that bridges the embodiment gap by translating coarse human tracking intents into precise, force-compliant robotic commands with tactile sensing. By estimating accurate contact points and leveraging a tactile-enabled fingertip force-sensing profile, the system dynamically computes localized corrections using the operational space Jacobian with respect to joint angle updates. We rigorously evaluate this tactile-driven adaptation strategy across both simulated environments and real-world hardware. Compared with representative baselines, the proposed method consistently achieves higher task success rates and improved execution efficiency in robust grasping, disturbance-resilient manipulation, and complex dexterous tasks.

</details>


### [172] [SkyJEPA: Learning Long-Horizon World Models for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Control of Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23444)
*Pratyaksh Rao,Wancong Zhang,Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)的潜空间动力学模型，结合物理启发的探针模块实现可解释的状态预测，并与采样优化控制结合用于四旋翼无人机的实时控制，通过自动化数据生成管道实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络动力学模型在长时间预测中因自回归滚动机制导致误差累积，而JEPA类方法此前主要用于运动学级规划，缺乏在高频控制场景中的研究。

Method: 提出JEPA风格的潜空间动力学模型，结合物理启发的探针(prober)将冻结的潜变量映射为可解释状态，实现物理可解释的长时预测；将该模型与基于采样的最优控制方法结合用于实时控制；开发自动化数据集生成管道以减少对真实世界数据采集的依赖。

Result: 开环和室外闭环实验表明该方法具有准确的预测能力、鲁棒的零样本仿真到现实迁移性能，以及在多样化操作条件下的强大泛化能力。

Conclusion: JEPA风格的潜空间动力学模型结合物理探针和采样控制，为四旋翼无人机的高频实时控制提供了一种有效方案，显著提升了长时间预测精度和零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Accurate dynamics models are critical for informed decision-making in robotic systems, particularly for agile aerial vehicles operating under uncertainty. Neural network dynamics models are attractive for capturing complex nonlinear effects, but existing predictive approaches struggle with long-horizon forecasting because their autoregressive rollout mechanism amplifies errors over time. Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) offer a compelling alternative by modeling dynamics in latent space, yet prior JEPA-style methods for robot navigation have been studied primarily for kinematic-level planning, with limited investigation in high-frequency control. In this work, we introduce the JEPA-style model for real-time quadrotor control. The proposed approach combines a latent dynamics model with a novel physics-inspired prober that maps frozen latents to interpretable state, enabling physically grounded long-horizon prediction. Additionally, we combine the learned model with a sampling-based optimal control solution to take advantage of its predictive capabilities for real-time control on embedded hardware. Finally, to reduce the dependence on expensive and unsafe real-world data collection, we develop a structured pipeline for automated dataset generation. Extensive open-loop and outdoor closed-loop experiments demonstrate accurate prediction, robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, and strong generalization across diverse operating conditions.

</details>


### [173] [DVL-DeepONet: A Physics-Guided Operator Learning for Resilient Underwater Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23502)
*Arup Kumar Sahoo,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了DVL-DeepONet，一种物理引导的深度神经算子框架，用于在缺失或噪声波束条件下估计AUV速度，在真实实验中优于基线方法40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV导航中DVL常因海洋障碍物、海底反射或环境干扰而收到噪声或不完整波束测量；低成本平台可能缺少惯性传感器，导致速度估计困难，导航性能下降。

Method: 提出DVL-DeepONet及其三种变体，通过学习将时序惯性/DVL观测映射到车辆速度的非线性算子，并施加DVL测量物理一致性约束，实现鲁棒速度估计。

Result: 在累计路径约10,000米的真实AUV实验中，DVL-DeepONet架构比基线模型方法和学习算法性能提升了40%。

Conclusion: 所提物理引导的深度神经算子框架能在退化传感条件下实现鲁棒的速度估计，有效解决了缺失波束场景下的导航难题。

Abstract: Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) rely heavily on the fusion of inertial sensors and Doppler velocity logs (DVLs) for navigation. In standard autonomous navigation systems, the DVL measures four beam velocities, thereby enabling the estimation of the AUV velocity vector. However, during real-world missions, the DVL may receive noisy or incomplete beam measurements due to marine obstacles, seabed reflections, or environmental disturbances. Furthermore, some low-cost underwater platforms operate without inertial sensors to reduce system complexity and cost. In such cases, reliable estimation of the AUV velocity vector in real-world missing beam scenarios becomes challenging, leading to degraded navigation solutions. To circumvent these challenges and enable resilient underwater navigation, we propose DVL-DeepONet, a physics-guided deep neural operator framework along with three variants. The proposed models are designed to estimate DVL-based velocity information under multiple operational scenarios, including (i) noise-resilient estimation in coupled inertial/DVL measurements, (ii) DVL-only learning, and (iii) beam measurement recovery. By learning a nonlinear operator that maps temporal inertial/DVL observations directly to vehicle velocity while enforcing DVL measurement physics through a consistency constraint, the proposed approach enables robust velocity estimation even under degraded sensing conditions. The proposed framework is validated using real-world AUV experiments, comprising a cumulative path length of approximately 10,000 m. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed DVL-DeepONet architectures outperform baseline model-based approaches and learning-based algorithms by 40%.

</details>


### [174] [BiliVLA: Scene-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model with Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Biliary Endoscopic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23531)
*Jinsong Lin,Chi kit Ng,Zhiyong Xiong,Zikang Pan,Yihan Hu,Tabassum Tamima,Ziyi Hao,Eddie Cheung,Jiewen Lai,Huxin Gao,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiliVLA 提出了一种场景感知的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，将胆道内窥镜导航建模为指令条件化的视觉运动学习问题，通过两阶段训练（SFT+GRPO）实现内窥镜导航，在ERCP子任务中动作精度达91.96%，总体成功率为84.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人和视觉辅助技术在ERCP中因解剖变异和视觉伪影导致性能下降，难以实现自主可靠的内窥镜导航，亟需一种能够感知场景、理解指令并稳健执行动作的框架。

Method: BiliVLA 包含三个核心模块：1）联合预测目标类别、定位边界框和离散3自由度电机指令；2）场景感知监督（增强语义目标一致性）和安全感知恢复监督（诱导保守退缩行为）；3）两阶段训练范式：先进行接地增强的监督微调（SFT），再使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）提升闭环导航的动作可靠性和决策一致性。

Result: 在三个ERCP子任务的真实体模实验中，平均动作精度为91.96%，总体成功率为84.85%。

Conclusion: 语义接地、场景感知学习和奖励引导优化的结合改善了感知-动作对齐，实现了鲁棒的自主内窥镜导航。

Abstract: Endoscopic retrograde cholangiopancreatography (ERCP) demands precise endoscopic navigation and stable biliary cannulation within a narrow monocular field characterized by specular reflections, partial occlusions, and frequent tissue contact. Although recent robotic systems and vision-based assistance techniques improve operator ergonomics and provide perceptual cues, their performance degrades under pronounced anatomical variability and safety-critical visual artifacts, which hinders reliable autonomy in cannulation-grade procedures. Here, we present BiliVLA, a scene-aware Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework that formulates biliary endoscopic navigation as an instruction-conditioned visuomotor learning problem. Given an endoscopic observation and a stage-specific language instruction, BiliVLA jointly predicts the target category, a grounded bounding box, and a discrete three degrees of freedom (DoF) motor command for a continuum endoscope. The proposed framework incorporates scene-aware supervision to enhance semantic target consistency and safety-aware recovery supervision to induce conservative retreat behaviors under luminal wall contact. A key component of BiliVLA is a two-stage training paradigm that combines grounding-enhanced supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which significantly improves action reliability and decision consistency during closed-loop navigation. Across three ERCP subtasks, BiliVLA achieves an average action precision of 91.96\% and an overall success rate (SR) of 84.85\% in real-world phantom experiments. These results indicate that integrating semantic grounding, scene-aware learning, and reward-guided optimization improves perception-action alignment and enables robust autonomous endoscopic navigation.

</details>


### [175] [HoloAgent-0: A Unified Embodied Agent Framework with 3D Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23565)
*Xiaolin Zhou,Liu Liu,Tingyang Xiao,Wei Feng,Fa Fu,Xinrui Meng,Xinjie Wang,Jialiang Han,Boyang Yu,Yun Du,Wei Sui,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HoloAgent-0，一个统一的具身智能体框架，通过Embodied AgentOS将语言指令转化为可执行技能图，结合3D空间记忆和具身技能，实现真实世界机器人的闭环执行、资源调度和重规划。在真实硬件上验证了空间记忆、长程导航、闭环执行等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在数字环境中运行良好，但扩展到物理机器人面临连续执行、依赖具身、不确定性及安全约束等挑战。已有具身AI系统功能分散、耦合松散，缺乏统一部署框架。

Method: 提出HoloAgent-0框架，包含三个耦合层：Embodied AgentOS（语言指令→技能图→闭环执行与重规划）、3D空间记忆（物理世界接地）、具身技能（机器人动作）。系统支持资源调度、执行监控和运行时反馈驱动的澄清/重规划。

Result: 在真实硬件上部署并评估了空间记忆、长程导航、闭环执行（运动生成、物体搜索、跨机器人协调、移动操作）等能力。

Conclusion: HoloAgent-0提供了一个统一的具身智能体框架，成功将LLM智能体循环扩展到物理机器人，实现了从语言指令到真实世界闭环执行的全流程。

Abstract: LLM agents follow a practical execution loop in digital environments: they reason over structured states, invoke tools, inspect feedback, and revise actions. Extending this loop to physical robots is difficult because physical execution is continuous, embodiment-dependent, uncertain, and constrained by safety. Existing embodied-AI systems have advanced manipulation, spatial understanding, navigation, and humanoid control, but these capabilities often remain specialized modules or loosely coupled decision loops. In this work, we introduce HoloAgent-0, a unified embodied agent framework for real-world robot deployment. Embodied AgentOS converts language instructions into executable skill graphs, schedules robot resources, monitors execution, and triggers clarification or re-planning from runtime feedback. HoloAgent-0 organizes heterogeneous robot models and controllers through three coupled layers: Embodied AgentOS for closed-loop execution, 3D spatial memory for physical world grounding, and embodied skills for robot action. We deploy HoloAgent-0 on real hardware and evaluate its spatial memory, long-horizon navigation, and closed-loop execution across motion generation, object search, cross-robot coordination, and mobile manipulation.

</details>


### [176] [A Generative Model for Closed-Loop Microsimulation of Signalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23588)
*Yash Ranjan,Rahul Sengupta,Anand Rangarajan,Sanjay Ranka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Enactor 是一种以车辆为中心的生成式模型，用于信号交叉口的闭环微观仿真。它采用极坐标编码和 Transformer 架构，通过闭环课程训练，在仿真和真实数据上均显著优于 SUMO 和现有的 Transformer 基线，速度与旅行时间分布的 KL 散度降低一个数量级以上，红灯违规减少超过一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通微观仿真器依赖手工行为模型，虽能再现聚合流量但无法捕捉信号交叉口车辆间的异质交互；而学习的轨迹预测器虽能捕捉更丰富的交互，但预测时域短且在闭环运行时不稳定。为此需要一种既能捕捉细粒度交互又能在闭环环境中稳定运行的模型。

Method: 提出 Enactor 模型：以车辆为中心，将行人作为影响车辆决策的上下文但不预测其轨迹；将动态参与者和车道多段线编码为以交叉口中心为原点的极坐标；采用具有分离的空间注意力和时间注意力模块的 Transformer 架构，预测每个参与者下一步运动的分布 (s, α)；采用闭环课程训练，使模型暴露于自身预测的误差累积中。

Result: 在两种交叉口几何形状的 4000 秒仿真测试中，Enactor 控制所有动态车辆，恢复 SUMO 数据生成器的速度和旅行时间分布，KL 散度比近期 Transformer 基线在旅行时间上低一个数量级以上，在速度上约低 5 倍；红灯违规减少超过一个数量级。在真实鱼眼摄像头数据上，Enactor 在每个预测时域上均优于恒速基线。

Conclusion: Enactor 在闭环交叉口微观仿真中显著优于现有方法，消融实验表明引导车后保险杠特征对交叉口安全指标影响最大，验证了以车辆为中心、极坐标编码和闭环训练策略的有效性。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulators rely on hand-crafted behavior models that reproduce aggregate flow but miss the heterogeneous interactions between vehicles at signalized intersections. Learned trajectory predictors capture richer interactions but are short-horizon and tend to be unstable when run in closed loop. We present Enactor, an actor-centric generative model for closed-loop intersection microsimulation. The model focuses on vehicles; pedestrians are included as context that can influence vehicle decisions but not predicted. Dynamic actors and lane polylines are encoded in polar coordinates referenced to the intersection center. A transformer with separate spatial and temporal attention blocks predicts a distribution over each actor's next-step motion ($s$, $α$). Training uses a closed-loop curriculum so the model is exposed to its own predictions. We evaluate Enactor in two regimes. In a 4000-second simulation-in-the-loop test at two intersection geometries, Enactor controls every dynamic vehicle against a continuously refreshing actor set rather than the fixed cohort that learned trajectory predictors are usually evaluated against. It recovers the SUMO data generator's speed and travel-time distributions with KL divergence over an order of magnitude lower than a recent transformer baseline on travel time, and substantially lower on speed (roughly $5\times$ lower at Site 1), and reduces red-light violations relative to the same baseline by more than an order of magnitude. An ablation isolates the leader rear-bumper feature as the change with the largest effect on intersection-aware safety metrics. We also evaluate on real-world field data and apply the same architecture to naturalistic vehicle trajectories from a fish-eye camera at a signalized intersection and evaluate it on multi-horizon predictive tasks. Enactor outperforms a constant-velocity baseline at every horizon evaluated.

</details>


### [177] [KEMO: Event-Driven Keyframe Memory for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation with VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23589)
*Yihan Zeng,Minghao Ye,Yiyuan Chen,Yide Shentu,Philipp Wu,Zike Yan,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KEMO是一个轻量级即插即用记忆框架，通过自动选择与任务状态变化相关的关键帧来增强VLA策略的长程操作能力。该方法结合机器人运动学和视觉滤波检测事件，将关键帧编码为时序记忆token，通过交叉注意力与门控残差融合整合到当前视觉特征中。在真实双臂操作任务上，KEMO相比无记忆基线提升了23.6%的任务成功率和34.1%的阶段完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人操作中相似观测可能出现在不同执行阶段，而合适动作依赖于已完成的操作历史。现有记忆增强方法要么保留密集历史需要压缩，要么主要依赖近期上下文可能丢弃早期任务相关事件，缺乏自动选择性地保留任务关键帧的机制。

Method: 提出KEMO框架：1) 结合机器人运动学与视觉滤波的事件检测机制，自动识别状态变化关键帧；2) 将选中的关键帧编码为紧凑的时序排序记忆token；3) 通过交叉注意力与门控残差融合将记忆token与当前视觉特征整合，用于VLA训练；4) 事件检测结果还定义了关键转换附近的高权重训练样本。

Result: 在包含2-6个计分子任务、830-2846步（28-95秒）的真实双臂操作任务上，KEMO相比无记忆基线($π_{0.5}$)使任务成功率提升23.6%，阶段完成率提升34.1%。消融实验表明事件驱动的关键帧选择优于均匀采样和最近帧保留方法，门控融合和关键帧对齐的损失加权提供互补增益。

Conclusion: KEMO通过自动选择关键帧的事件驱动记忆机制有效解决了长程操作中的歧义问题，显著提升了VLA策略在复杂多阶段任务上的性能，且作为轻量级插件框架具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation remains challenging because similar observations may occur at different execution stages, while the appropriate action depends on previously completed operations. Memory can address this ambiguity by enabling policies to infer task progress from execution history. However, existing memory-augmented approaches often either retain dense histories that require compression or rely primarily on recent context that may discard earlier task-relevant events. In this work, we propose propose KEMO, a lightweight plug-in memory framework that automatically selectively preserves keyframes associated with task-relevant state changes for VLA policies. KEMO combines robot kinematics with visual filtering to detect events, encodes the selected keyframes as compact temporally ordered memory tokens, and integrates them with current visual features through cross-attention and gated residual fusion for VLA training. The detected events also define higher-weight training samples near critical transitions. We evaluate KEMO on various real-world dual-arm manipulation tasks spanning 2 to 6 scored subtasks, and trajectory length ranging from 830 steps to 2846 execution steps (durations from 28 to 95 seconds). Compared with the memory-free baseline (e.g., $π_{0.5}$), KEMO improves aggregate Task Success Rate by 23.6\% and Stage Completion Rate by 34.1\%. Ablations show that event-driven keyframe selection outperforms uniform sampling and recent-frame retention, while the proposed gated fusion and keyframe-aligned loss weighting provide complementary gains.

</details>


### [178] [Real-Time Multimodal Activity-Aware Error Detection in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23593)
*Seyed Hamid Reza Roodabeh,Zongyu Li,Homa Alemzadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，利用多模态输入（视频、运动学、文本提示）进行手术执行错误检测，通过活动提示整合层级描述，引入活动感知视觉嵌入，在JIGSAWS和SAR-RARP50数据集上分别提升F1分数达5%和16.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手术执行错误检测方法仅使用视频数据，忽视了手术流程层级结构中细粒度的活动描述和错误类型，且未充分利用多模态互补信息。

Method: 构建统一多模态框架，融合视频、运动学和文本描述提示；通过活动提示（activity prompting）整合手势级活动、器械-物体交互及错误定义的语言描述；引入基于手术活动标签预训练的视觉编码器生成活动感知视觉嵌入，对比CLIP与传统图像嵌入的效果。

Result: 在JIGSAWS上F1提升5%，在SAR-RARP50上F1提升16.6%，均优于现有最优基线。

Conclusion: 融合精心设计的文本提示与多模态数据能显著提升手术执行错误检测的准确性。

Abstract: Robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery improves surgical precision but introduces complexity, making technical error detection essential for ensuring patient safety. Current executional error detection methods using video data often overlook fine-grained contextual descriptions of activities and error types within the hierarchical structure of surgical procedures. They also under-utilize complementary multimodal information. We propose a unified framework for executional error detection that leverages multimodal input, including video, kinematics, and descriptive textual prompts. Through activity prompting, we integrate descriptive language in gesture-level activities, instrument-object interactions, and error definitions. We also introduce activity-aware visual embeddings derived from vision encoders pretrained on surgical activity labels to compare the effectiveness of contrastive language-image embeddings with traditional image-based embeddings for error detection. By seamlessly integrating kinematic data with video and textual modalities, our framework significantly improves error detection performance. Achieving up to 5\% and 16.6\% F1 score improvements over state-of-the-art baselines on the JIGSAWS and SAR-RARP50 datasets, respectively, we demonstrate the value of combining curated textual prompts with multimodal data for accurate error detection.

</details>


### [179] [RECALL: Recovery Experience Collection for Active Lifelong Learning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23617)
*Ulas Berk Karli,Tesca Fitzgerald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的主动持续学习范式，通过不确定性引导的数据收集替代被动模仿学习，在提高微调效率的同时揭示了主动收集恢复数据带来的灾难性遗忘问题，并评估了多种持续学习技术的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的VLA模型微调采用被动模仿学习，存在三个缺陷：1）需要机器人先失败才能触发数据收集；2）无法指导哪些状态需要监督；3）在策略已表现良好的冗余任务部分浪费标注者精力。因此需要更高效的数据收集范式。

Method: 提出主动持续学习范式：1）采用不确定性引导的数据收集策略替代被动收集；2）评估多种持续学习技术（包括基于回放的数据混合和弹性权重巩固）来缓解灾难性遗忘；3）在自回归VLA模型上进行实证对比研究。

Result: 1）主动不确定性引导的数据收集在微调效率上优于被动收集的演示数据；2）仅在主动收集的恢复数据上微调会导致灾难性遗忘；3）不同的持续学习技术在保持对恢复数据的可塑性和保留先前学习行为之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 不确定性引导的恢复演示能够提升VLA模型的适应效率，但将针对性的新数据纳入大规模机器人策略时仍面临开放挑战，需要在可塑性和稳定性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly fine-tuned through passive imitation learning, where additional demonstrations are collected for tasks where the policy performs poorly. This approach incurs several downsides: it requires the robot to fail before data collection is triggered, provides little guidance about which states require supervision, and wastes demonstrator effort on redundant parts of the task where the policy already performs well. In this paper, we propose an active, continual learning paradigm for VLAs. We demonstrate that active, uncertainty-guided data collection leads to more efficient fine-tuning than when using passively-collected demonstrations. However, we also find that fine-tuning only on actively-collected recovery data leads to catastrophic forgetting. We evaluate techniques for continual learning, including replay-based data mixing and elastic weight consolidation, and identify tradeoffs between plasticity to uncertainty-guided recovery data and retention of previously learned behaviors. Overall, our work contributes an empirical study of active continual learning for autoregressive VLAs, establishing that uncertainty-guided recovery demonstrations can improve adaptation efficiency while also revealing open challenges when targeted new data is incorporated into large robot policies.

</details>


### [180] [dVLA-RL: Reinforcement Learning over Denoising Trajectories for Discrete Diffusion Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23623)
*Yuhao Wu,Yitian Liu,Weijie Shen,Mishuo Han,Wenjie Xu,Haotian Liang,Zhongshan Liu,Yinan Mao,Lei Xu,Xinping Guan,Ru Ying,Ran Zheng,Wei Sui,Xiaokang Yang,Wenbo Ding,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出dVLA-RL，将强化学习引入离散扩散VLA模型，通过将去噪过程建模为MDP并优化联合路径概率而非边际动作概率，实现了在LIBERO上99.7%的成功率，并在RoboTwin 2.0上相比SFT基线提升30.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散VLA模型仅使用监督微调(SFT)进行训练，强化学习(RL)用于策略优化的潜力尚未被探索。其根本挑战在于dVLA生成的最终动作的边际概率难以计算。

Method: 将去噪过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)，将优化目标从边际动作概率转移到采样生成路径的联合概率（即逐跳转移概率的乘积）。基于此轨迹级目标，引入统一步调度方法，根据任务复杂度自适应调整去噪步数。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到99.7%的成功率；在RoboTwin 2.0上相比SFT基线提升30.6%，与强基线World-Action Model竞争。

Conclusion: 提出的轨迹级联合概率优化目标天然支持可变去噪步数，统一的步调度策略能根据任务复杂度平衡成功率和计算效率，证明了RL在离散扩散VLA模型中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have established a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation by grounding control into the semantic reasoning of VLMs. Prevailing architectures typically model actions continuously via diffusion or flow processes, or discretely through either autoregressive generation or parallel decoding. Recently, Discrete Diffusion VLAs (dVLAs) have emerged as a distinct alternative, unifying vision, language, and action into a single discrete token space via masked generative modeling. While combining iterative refinement with unified representations, its training has thus far been restricted to Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), leaving the potential of Reinforcement Learning (RL) for further policy refinement largely unexplored. A fundamental challenge in RL for dVLAs is that the marginal probability of the final action generated by dVLAs remains intractable. To solve this problem, we propose \textbf{dVLA-RL}, shifting the learning objective from the marginal action probability to the joint probability of the sampled generation path. Specifically, by modeling the denoising process as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), we mathematically formulate this path probability as a product of step-wise transitions. This trajectory-level objective provides a unified formulation that natively accommodates variable denoising steps. Leveraging this intrinsic fexibility, we introduce a unified step scheduling approach for complex multi-task learning, tailoring denoising steps to specific task complexities to maximize both success rates and computational effciency. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our approach achieves a success rate of \textbf{99.7\%} on LIBERO. Furthermore, it establishes strong VLA-based results on RoboTwin 2.0 by delivering a \textbf{30.6\%} improvement over the SFT baseline, remaining competitive with strong World-Action Model baselines.

</details>


### [181] [Learning to See While Learning to Act: Diffusion Models for Active Perception in Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23625)
*Kuancheng Wang,Vaibhav Saxena,Shuo Cheng,Yotto Koga,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出See2Act模仿学习方法，通过将动作去噪与视角优化相结合，在测试时主动推断视角序列来指导动作预测，实现严重遮挡下的鲁棒操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习大多假设完全可观测，但实际中物体常被遮挡，机器人需要同时搜索和行动，而有限演示下学习这种耦合行为十分困难。

Method: 提出See2Act方法，将动作预测条件于测试时主动推断的视角序列上，耦合动作去噪与视角优化；策略使用锚定于关键帧动作的相机位姿训练，隐式学习看哪里与如何做。

Result: 在Ravens中，策略在严重遮挡下恢复信息性视角；在RLBench任务上，性能比先前方法提升高达34%；真实世界仅用50个演示即可实现零样本仿真到真实迁移。

Conclusion: 学习视角推理能够在部分可观测条件下实现鲁棒操作，验证了主动视觉与动作耦合的有效性。

Abstract: Most imitation learning methods assume full observability in table-top settings. In practice, objects are often occluded, requiring robots to both search and act, and learning this coupled behavior from limited demonstrations remains challenging. We propose See2Act, an imitation learning approach that conditions action prediction on a sequence of actively-inferred viewpoints at test time, by coupling action denoising with viewpoint refinement. The policy is trained using camera poses anchored to keyframe actions from offline demonstrations, enabling implicit learning of where to see, while learning how to act. We empirically demonstrate that in Ravens the policy recovers informative viewpoints under severe occlusions, and on RLBench tasks it improves performance by up to 34% over prior methods. In the real world, we collect 50 demonstrations in a digital twin and achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on pick-and-place tasks using depth observations. The policy handles significant occlusions, showing that learned viewpoint reasoning enables robust manipulation under partial observability.

</details>


### [182] [Flatness Preserves Instruction Following in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23641)
*Haochen Zhang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型在微调时会出现"指令盲视"问题，即忽略语言指令而依赖视觉捷径。本文提出通过平坦性保持优化（锐度感知最小化）来微调VLA模型，在不增加数据、修改架构或重新训练的情况下，将指令跟随能力提升超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在下游微调中，有限机器人数据上的标准微调会退化预训练的视觉-语言表征，导致策略忽略语言指令而依赖视觉捷径（称为"指令盲视"）。标准微调在有限数据下梯度稀疏，表现为尖锐的损失景观和高曲率极小值。

Method: 提出平坦性保持优化方法，在VLA微调中应用锐度感知最小化（Sharpness-Aware Minimization），学习更平坦的损失景观，使模型对权重空间扰动更鲁棒。

Result: 在多个仿真和真实世界基准测试中，指令跟随能力提升超过60%，且无需额外数据、架构修改或重新训练。该方法与现有引导技术互补。

Conclusion: 平坦性保持优化有效解决了VLA微调中的指令盲视问题，是一种简单且有效的改进方法，具有良好的泛化能力和兼容性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have the potential for open-world generalization by leveraging pretrained vision-language representations, yet downstream finetuning on limited robot data often degrades these representations, leading to brittle policies that ignore language instructions in favor of visual shortcuts, a failure mode we term instruction blindness. We hypothesize that standard finetuning with limited data applies gradients to a sparse set of points, which manifests as a sharp loss landscape with high-curvature minima. We propose to address this directly through flatness-preserving optimization while finetuning on the exact same data, where learning a flatter landscape results in a model more robust to perturbations in the weight space. Specifically, we demonstrate that simply applying sharpness-aware minimization during VLA finetuning significantly improves instruction following by over 60% across multiple simulation and real-world benchmarks without additional data, architectural modification, or retraining. We further analyze the effect of selective sharpness, quantify its effects, and show that our approach is complementary to existing guidance techniques. Project page can be found at https://haochenz11.github.io/papers/flatness-vla/.

</details>


### [183] [A Reduced Order Model for Emergent Mechanics in Woven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23658)
*Anvay A. Pradhan,Evgueni T. Filipov,Talia Y. Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种降阶模型，通过节点和四个物理可解释的刚度单元（轴向变形、面内去卷曲、纱线间剪切、摩擦滑移）来模拟编织结构的力学行为，在保持计算效率的同时实现了5%以内的实验校准精度，并能模拟连续介质方法难以捕捉的泊松响应、纱线拔出、应力局部化和可编程各向异性等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编织结构模型要么采用均匀化方法丢失细节，要么全分辨率求解计算成本过高，缺乏兼具物理可解释性和计算效率的中间尺度模型。

Method: 建立节点-弹簧降阶模型，包含四个刚度单元（轴向、面内去卷曲、纱线间剪切、摩擦滑移）；通过特征值分析验证单元必要性；基于三点弯曲和剪切实验数据校准刚度参数。

Result: 模型在多种纱线宽度和间距下与实验数据的误差控制在5%以内；成功模拟了卷曲互换导致的泊松响应、纱线逐级拔出时的阶梯式力降、三种撕裂构型下的应力局部化以及空间梯度刚度实现的可编程各向异性。

Conclusion: 该降阶模型兼具物理透明性和计算效率，可作为编织结构材料分析与设计的实用工具，实现可编程力学响应。

Abstract: Woven structures exhibit rich mechanical behaviors including anisotropic stiffness, shear-induced locking, and crimp interchange that emerge purely from the geometric arrangement of individual weavers rather than from constituent material properties. Existing models either homogenize these interactions or resolve them at prohibitive computational cost. We introduce a reduced-order model that bridges this gap by representing individual weaver interactions through a system of nodes and four physically interpretable stiffness elements capturing axial deformation, in-plane uncrimping, inter-weaver shear, and frictional slip. Eigenvalue analysis of the unit cell confirms that the lowest-energy deformation modes correspond directly to known weave-specific phenomena, and that each element is necessary for a complete kinematic and mechanistic description. Element stiffness parameters are calibrated against empirical three-point bending and shear data, achieving agreement within 5% across varied weaver widths and spacings. The validated model is then applied to demonstrate capabilities beyond the reach of continuum approaches including: the emergent Poisson's response arising from crimp interchange, stepwise force reduction during progressive weaver pullout, stress localization under three distinct tearing configurations, and programmable mechanical anisotropy through spatially graded weaver stiffness. The physical transparency and computational efficiency of the framework position it as a practical tool for the analysis and design of woven architected materials with programmable mechanical response.

</details>


### [184] [CoorDex: Coordinating Body and Hand Priors for Continuous Dexterous Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23680)
*Sikai Li,Shuning Li,Zhenyu Wei,Yunchao Yao,Chenran Li,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoorDex是一个学习框架，通过将高维身体和灵巧手控制转化为协调的潜变量残差控制，实现了人形机器人在运动过程中进行高自由度灵巧操作（如边走边抓取瓶子、移动中开冰箱门等）。该方法利用特权运动跟踪教师、潜变量先验蒸馏和协调残差强化学习，使Unitree G1人形机器人配合20自由度WUJI灵巧手能够完成动态灵巧操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人操作通常简化为"停-走"过程（走到物体前→停止→操作→继续移动），且常依赖低自由度末端执行器（类似开-合抓取基元）。这限制了人形机器人在运动中进行高自由度灵巧操作的能力。

Method: 1）从全身和手部模拟演示出发，训练特权运动跟踪教师网络；2）将教师网络蒸馏为基于本体感觉的潜变量先验；3）将冻结的潜变量先验作为残差强化学习的动作空间；4）设计协调潜变量残差策略，通过共享任务上下文和独立身体-手部残差头组合先验，保持全身自然运动并提升手指级接触可靠性。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人（配备20自由度WUJI灵巧手）上实现了移动中灵巧操作，包括非停止的瓶子抓取与搬运、移动中开冰箱门、立方体拾取并翻转。消融实验表明：关节空间PPO、关节空间手部控制和单块潜变量预测在同一奖励预算下均失败，而潜变量先验接口和协调残差结构使得高维接触丰富的移动操作变得可训练。

Conclusion: CoorDex证明了将高维身体与灵巧手控制转化为协调的潜变量残差控制，是实现在运动中进行高自由度灵巧操作的有效范式。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation is often simplified into a stop-and-go process: walking to an object, stopping to manipulate it, and then resuming locomotion. It also commonly relies on low degree-of-freedom (DoF) end effectors that behave like an open-close grasp primitive. We introduce CoorDex, a learning pipeline that converts high-dimensional body and dexterous hand control into coordinated latent residual control, enabling high-DoF dexterous loco-manipulation on the move. Starting from simulated whole-body and hand demonstrations, CoorDex trains privileged motion tracking teachers for the humanoid body and dexterous hand, distills them into proprioception-conditioned latent priors, and uses the frozen priors as the action space for downstream residual reinforcement learning. A coordinated latent residual policy composes these priors through shared task context and separate body-hand residual heads, preserving natural whole-body motion while improving finger-level contact reliability. CoorDex enables a Unitree G1 humanoid with a 20-DoF WUJI hand to execute dexterous manipulation while in motion, including non-stop bottle grasping and carrying, fridge door opening on the move, and cube pick-and-turn. Ablations on the walk-grasp-carry task show that joint-space PPO, joint-space hand control, and monolithic latent prediction all fail under the same reward budget, while the latent-prior interface and coordinated residual structure make high-dimensional contact-rich loco-manipulation trainable. Project Page: https://skevinci.github.io/coordex/

</details>


### [185] [LaST-HD: Learning Latent Physical Reasoning from Scalable Human Data for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23685)
*Jiaming Liu,Yinxi Wang,Chenyang Gu,Siyuan Qian,Xiangju Mi,Hao Chen,Jiawei Chen,Qingpo Wuwu,Xiaoqi Li,Nuowei Han,Yiming Zhang,Xuheng Zhang,Yang Yue,Yeqing Yang,Lei Wang,Peng Jia,Hao Tang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaST-HD提出了一种新的人类到机器人动作学习范式，通过在人手和机器人轨迹之间建立共享潜在推理空间来对齐物理动力学，而非简单地模仿运动学。同时开发了低成本数据采集手套OOL Glove，并采用混合人机共训练和在线校正策略，仅用20分钟数据即可达到90%以上的操作准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手演示为机器人学习提供了直接且可扩展的交互数据来源，但现有方法仅关注运动学层面的跨形态重定目标，忽略了人手和机器人操作之间物理动力学的底层对齐问题，导致鲁棒的技能迁移受限。

Method: (1) 在推理前行动的VLA框架基础上，训练辅助的动作条件世界模型，对齐未配对的人手和机器人轨迹，生成统一的潜在目标；(2) 开发低成本数据采集手套OOL Glove，获取高保真人手关键点数据；(3) 采用混合人机共训练和在线校正的分阶段训练策略。

Result: 在仅有20分钟的OOL Glove数据下，LaST-HD在新型环境中达到90%以上的操作准确率；混合共训练提升了模型对新颖物体、场景和位置的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过共享潜在空间对齐跨形态物理动力学，结合低成本人手数据采集和渐进式训练策略，LaST-HD能够高效地将人手演示迁移到不同机器人形态上，实现鲁棒的技能泛化。

Abstract: Human-hand demonstrations provide a direct and scalable source of physical interaction data for robot learning. While manual retargeting is indispensable for establishing kinematic action correspondence across different morphologies, robust transfer requires going beyond geometry to address the underlying alignment of physical dynamics between human and robot manipulation. To address this, we introduce LaST-HD, a novel human-to-robot action learning paradigm that extends reasoning-before-acting VLA by aligning human-hand and robot demonstrations in a shared latent reasoning space. Rather than mimicking human kinematics, LaST-HD trains an auxiliary action-conditioned world model on unpaired human-hand and robot trajectories to synthesize unified latent targets. After aligning cross-embodiment representations in this shared forward-dynamics space, these targets supervise LaST-HD's latent reasoning process, enabling it to internalize shared physical dynamics and drive efficient human-hand action learning. Moreover, we develop Out-of-Lab (OOL) Glove, a low-cost motion-capture glove tailored to LaST-HD for human-hand data collection. The captured human data provide precise keypoints and serve as universal action supervision across grippers and dexterous hands. Armed with the aligned latent space and high-fidelity human-hand data, we develop a progressive mixed-to-human training recipe comprising mixed human-robot co-training and human-hand online correction post-training. Through mixed co-training, LaST-HD improves generalization to novel objects, scenes, and positions using only human-hand demonstrations. With online correction, LaST-HD further adapts to novel environments and achieves over 90\% accuracy using only 20 minutes of OOL glove data.

</details>


### [186] [LIBERO-Safety: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Physical and Semantic Safety in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23686)
*Rongxu Cui,Zongzheng Zhang,Jingrui Pang,Haohan Chi,Jinbang Guo,Saining Zhang,Shaoxuan Xie,Xin Jin,Yao Mu,Jiaolong Yang,Guocai Yao,Xianyuan Zhan,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIBERO-Safety提出了一个参数化安全基准和关键帧驱动的数据生成流程，构建了包含19,664条无碰撞演示的大规模数据集，系统评估了8个VLA和2个具身基础模型，揭示了泛化-安全矛盾：高多样性训练能产生更安全的轨迹，但任务成功受限于次优轨迹合成和语义错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽具备强大的操作能力，但其在严格约束下的运行安全性尚未得到充分验证。现有基准缺乏对安全关键场景的系统覆盖，且人工遥操作数据收集存在可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出参数化安全基准以程序化生成具有全面随机性的安全关键场景；开发关键帧驱动的数据生成流程以克服人工遥操作的可扩展性瓶颈；基于此构建包含19,664条严格无碰撞演示的大规模数据集并进行广泛的域随机化。

Result: 对8个VLA模型和2个具身基础模型进行了系统的跨范式评估，发现高多样性训练虽能促进更安全的轨迹生成，但任务成功率仍受限于次优轨迹合成和语义错位这两个瓶颈。

Conclusion: LIBERO-Safety通过提供可扩展的数据流程、鲁棒的数据集和深刻的失败模式分析，为开发安全可靠的VLA模型奠定了关键基础。

Abstract: Despite the impressive manipulation capabilities of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, their operational safety under strict constraints remains largely unverified. To address this, we introduce a parametric safety benchmark to procedurally generate safety-critical scenarios with comprehensive stochasticity. To overcome the scalability bottlenecks of human teleoperation, we develop a novel keypose-driven data generation pipeline. Leveraging this infrastructure, we curate a large-scale dataset of 19,664 strictly collision-free demonstrations with extensive domain randomization. We then conduct a systematic cross-paradigm evaluation of eight VLA and two embodied foundation models. Our analysis reveals a critical generalization-safety tension: although high-diversity training fosters safer trajectories, task success remains fundamentally bottlenecked by sub-optimal trajectory synthesis and semantic misalignment. By providing a scalable pipeline, a robust dataset, and profound failure-mode insights, LIBERO-Safety establishes a crucial foundation for developing safe and reliable VLA models.

</details>


### [187] [AutoDex: An Automated Real-World System for Dexterous Grasping Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23689)
*Mingi Choi,Gunhee Kim,Jisoo Kim,Taeksoo Kim,Taeyun Ha,Jongbin Lim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoDex是一个自动化真实世界数据收集系统，能够无需人工干预地完成感知、执行、标注和重置的闭环，用于学习鲁棒灵巧抓取。该系统相比遥操作实现了4.8倍的吞吐量提升，且基于AutoDex验证数据库的抓取成功率达76%，远超仅仿真验证的34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习鲁棒灵巧抓取需要记录物理抓取结果的真实世界数据。遥操作虽能产生有效物理结果但速度慢且存在操作者偏差，基于仿真的生成虽廉价可扩展但无法保证接触有效性。因此需要一种自动化、可扩展的真实世界数据收集方法。

Method: AutoDex构建了自动化数据收集闭环：1) 对每个候选抓取，通过密集20相机感知系统在严重手-物遮挡下定位物体；2) 执行碰撞监测的机器人运动；3) 标注"举起并保持"的成功或失败；4) 在试验间主动重置物体以暴露更多稳定姿态下的候选抓取。最终形成可复用的物理标注抓取试验数据库，下游系统可通过检索和可行性过滤查询。

Result: 使用AutoDex在Allegro和Inspire两种机械手上收集了100种不同物体的3,593次抓取试验，附带同步多视角观测和机器人状态日志。在匹配的500次轨迹收集中，AutoDex需10.3小时，遥操作需49.4小时，吞吐量提升4.8倍。从AutoDex验证数据库中检索的抓取成功率为76%，仅仿真验证的为34%。

Conclusion: AutoDex通过全自动闭环系统有效解决了灵巧抓取真实世界数据收集的瓶颈问题，显著提升了数据收集效率，且验证了物理标注数据相比纯仿真的优越性，为灵巧抓取学习提供了高质量、可复用的数据基础设施。

Abstract: Learning robust dexterous grasping requires real-world data that records the physical outcomes of grasp attempts. Such data is hard to obtain at scale: teleoperation yields valid physical outcomes but is slow and operator-biased, while simulation-based generation is cheap and scalable but cannot certify contact validity. A natural solution is to generate candidate grasps and verify them on real hardware, but this scales only if the entire collection loop (perception, execution, labeling, and reset) runs without human intervention. We present AutoDex, an automated real-world data-collection system that closes this loop: for each candidate from a replaceable generator, it localizes the object under severe hand-object occlusion with dense 20-camera perception, executes collision-monitored robot motions, labels lift-and-hold success or failure, and actively resets the object between trials to expose additional candidates across stable poses. The result is a reusable database of physically labeled grasp trials that downstream systems can query by retrieval and feasibility filtering. Using AutoDex, we collect 3,593 grasp trials across Allegro and Inspire hands on 100 diverse objects, with synchronized multi-view observations and robot-state logs. For a matched 500-trajectory collection, AutoDex requires 10.3 h versus 49.4 h for teleoperation, yielding a 4.8x throughput improvement, and grasps retrieved from the AutoDex-validated database succeed 76% versus 34% for simulation-only validation. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Platooning Connected, Autonomous, and Human-Driven Vehicles: A Deep Reinforcement Learning-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20648)
*Zhen Qina,Dong-Fan Xie,Heng Ma,Xiaomei Zhao,Zhengbing He*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对非网联车辆无法融入现有队列控制的问题，提出了一种基于深度强化学习的混合队列控制策略，通过条件性允许非网联车辆加入队列并动态优化队列结构，在提升队列多样性的同时有效抑制速度扰动传播，实现了混合交通流吞吐量与稳定性之间的动态平衡，显著提升了交通稳定性、安全性并降低了油耗与排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有队列控制方法仅适用于网联车辆（包括网联自动驾驶车辆和网联人类驾驶车辆），未纳入非网联车辆，无法反映当前阶段真实混合交通流状况。

Method: 提出混合队列模式（条件性允许非网联车辆加入），并基于深度强化学习开发混合队列控制策略，该策略通过多层级状态表示网络整合车辆动力学、队列拓扑和交通流状态信息。

Result: 数值仿真表明，该策略通过动态优化队列结构有效抑制速度扰动传播，显著增强了混合交通的稳定性与安全性，同时降低了燃油消耗和排放。

Conclusion: 所提出的DRL控制策略能够动态平衡交通容量与稳定性，为解决混合交通流中非网联车辆融入问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Conventionally, existing vehicle platooning approaches are designed for connected vehicles, typically including connected autonomous vehicles and connected human-driven vehicles. Non-connected vehicles, such as non-connected autonomous or human-driven vehicles, are not incorporated. As a result, these platooning approaches may not properly reflect real-world mixed traffic conditions at the current stage. To address this limitation, this study proposes a hybrid platooning pattern that conditionally permits non-connected vehicles to join platoons, thereby enhancing platooning diversity and flexibility. However, it was found that the unregulated integration of non-connected vehicles can trigger rapid platoon expansion, significantly amplifying the risk of disturbance propagation in traffic flow. This, in turn, exacerbates the inherent conflict between traffic throughput and stability. To mitigate these challenges, this paper further develops a hybrid platooning control strategy based on deep reinforcement learning (DRL). This strategy integrates vehicle dynamics, platoon topology, and traffic flow states through a multi-level state representation network, enabling a dynamic trade-off between traffic capacity and stability. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed strategy effectively suppresses velocity disturbance propagation by dynamically optimizing platoon structures, thereby significantly enhancing the stability and safety of mixed traffic while reducing fuel consumption and emissions.

</details>


### [189] [Distributed Model Predictive Control with Adaptive Safety Zones for Multi-Fleet Drone Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20651)
*Linda Mümken,Diyar Altinses,Michael Schwung,Stefan Lier,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应速度相关安全球体替代固定半径安全区域的方法，用于密集无人机群在受限空间中的安全导航，通过MPC和分布式MPC实现，证明可达几何堆积极限，在混合编队中仍保证安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用基于最坏情况速度的固定安全区域，在拥挤场景下浪费空域，无法高效利用有限空间，亟需一种能根据速度动态调整安全边界的方法。

Method: 用与制动距离成比例的自适应速度相关安全球体替代固定半径；分别提出集中式MPC和分布式MPC（DMPC）框架；通过收缩条件保持集中式稳定性边界；推导改进的球堆积容量界和窄通道最优穿越速度。

Result: 在固定半径方法失效的场景下，自适应框架仍可行：可容纳的无人机数量翻倍，受限通道穿越时间减少约25%，能通过静态安全区无法通过的开口。

Conclusion: 自适应安全球体方法显著提升空域利用率，集中式变体更接近理论容量，分布式变体为混合编队提供实际部署方案且保持相同安全保证。

Abstract: Autonomous drone swarms in space-constrained environments such as warehouses, inspection corridors, and urban delivery routes must share limited airspace safely at high vehicle density. Existing approaches rely on fixed safety zones sized for worst-case velocity, which wastes airspace in congested scenarios. We replace the fixed radius with an adaptive, speed-dependent safety sphere whose size scales with braking distance: tight at low speeds, expanded at high speeds. We develop both a centralized model predictive control (MPC) formulation and a distributed MPC (DMPC) in which each drone optimizes locally from detected neighbors, accommodating mixed fleets with non-cooperative agents. We prove feasibility up to the geometric packing limit evaluated at the minimum radius, establish Lyapunov stability under sufficient conditions on the adaptation parameter, drone density, and prediction horizon, and extend these guarantees to the distributed setting via a contraction condition that preserves the centralized stability margins. We further derive modified sphere-packing capacity bounds and a throughput-optimal crossing speed for narrow passages. Simulations confirm that the adaptive framework remains feasible where fixed-radius methods fail: it roughly doubles the admissible drone count, reduces traversal time through constrained passages by about 25 percent, and enables passage through openings impassable to static safety zones. The centralized variant realizes a larger fraction of the theoretical capacity, while the distributed variant offers a more realistic deployment model for mixed-fleet operations under the same safety guarantees.

</details>


### [190] [Eigenspace-Based Clustering for Personalized System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20811)
*Abdulmoneam Ali,Dipankar Maity,Ahmed Arafa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、一次性聚类的异构系统辨识方法，通过比较局部观测数据的协方差矩阵主特征子空间对齐程度来识别具有相似动态的系统，并提供了理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类系统辨识方法依赖迭代训练进行簇分配，对学习不确定性和模型初始化敏感，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 每个系统估计局部状态协方差矩阵，通过测量不同系统协方差主特征子空间的对齐程度（即特征空间对齐）来推断簇身份，这是无需训练的一次性聚类方法。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的基于特征子空间的聚类方法能有效识别具有共享动态的系统，相比基于训练的聚类和非聚类基线，个性化模型估计误差更低。

Conclusion: 该工作为异构系统辨识提供了一种理论上有保证、计算上高效的无训练聚类方案，通过协方差特征子空间结构实现系统动态的相似性识别。

Abstract: We study the problem of system identification in heterogeneous settings, where different systems may follow distinct underlying dynamics. Existing clustered system identification approaches often rely on iterative training-based cluster assignment, which can be sensitive to learning uncertainty and model initialization. In contrast, we propose a one-shot, training-free clustering method that identifies similar systems using the structure of their locally observed data. Specifically, each system estimates a local state covariance matrix, and cluster identities are inferred by measuring the alignment between the leading covariance eigenspaces of different systems. We provide a mathematical interpretation of the proposed similarity score and develop a finite-sample analysis that characterizes how covariance estimation error induces eigenspace perturbations in terms of the underlying system dynamics. We then derive a probability bound for pairwise false merges and a global clustering success guarantee. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed eigenspace-based clustering method effectively identifies systems with shared dynamics, leading to lower personalized model-estimation error compared with training-based clustering and non-clustered baselines.

</details>


### [191] [A Metaheuristic Framework for Optimized HAPS-Aided Localization in Urban Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20851)
*Hongzhao Zheng,Mohamed Atia,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种元启发式框架，通过集成高保真3D城市模型、射线追踪和多目标优化，在实用约束下联合优化高海拔平台站（HAPS）的数量和位置，以增强城市环境中的GNSS定位。实验表明，在密集城区场景中，4个HAPS即可满足18米平均3D定位误差界阈值，2-5个HAPS相比纯卫星方案可实现超过50%的均方根定位误差降低，标准差和变异系数最高分别降低94%和87%。超过6个HAPS后收益递减，框架表现出良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集城市环境中严重的遮挡和非视距条件会降低GNSS精度，而传统的几何布局指标在此类场景下已不适用。HAPS可作为机会信号源增强GNSS，但如何在实用约束下优化其数量和位置是一个离散且高度非凸的难题。

Method: 提出元启发式框架，集成高保真3D城市模型、射线追踪和多目标优化；开发了三种基于不同搜索原理的元启发式算法（贪婪基线作为对比），以高效探索解空间。

Result: 三种元启发式方案均快速收敛并一致优于贪婪基线；4个HAPS即可满足18米平均3D PEB阈值；2-5个HAPS相比纯卫星方案，均方根PEB降低超50%，标准差和CV分别最高降低94%和87%；超过6个HAPS因几何冗余收益递减。

Conclusion: 该框架在不同城市形态和传播条件下表现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力，为城市环境中HAPS辅助定位提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: High-altitude platform stations (HAPS), originally designed for communication services, can also provide structured signals of opportunity (SoOP) to augment the global navigation satellite system (GNSS). However, dense urban environments introduce severe blockage and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) conditions that undermine GNSS accuracy and render geometric placement metrics insufficient. To address this, we propose a metaheuristic framework for jointly optimizing the number and placement of HAPS under practical constraints by integrating high-fidelity 3D city models, ray-tracing, and multi-objective optimization to handle the discrete and highly non-convex design space. Three metaheuristic solutions based on distinct search principles are developed to efficiently explore the solution space, all demonstrating rapid convergence and consistently outperforming a greedy baseline, particularly in the low-to-moderate HAPS regime. For representative dense urban scenarios, we show that four HAPS are sufficient to satisfy an 18-m average 3D positioning error bound (PEB) threshold, while configurations with two to five HAPS achieve over 50\% reduction in mean and root mean square (RMS) PEB and up to 94\% and 87\% reduction in standard deviation and coefficient of variation (CV), respectively, compared to the satellite-only case. Diminishing returns are observed beyond six HAPS due to geometric redundancy, emphasizing the importance of optimized placement. The framework further demonstrates strong robustness and generalizability across diverse urban environments with varying building morphology and propagation conditions, establishing it as an effective and scalable solution for HAPS-assisted localization in realistic urban settings.

</details>


### [192] [AI Data Centers and Power System Sustainability: Understanding the Sustainability Implications of AI-Driven Data Centers on Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21064)
*Yuhao Huang,Novarun Deb,Hamidreza Zareipour*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI数据中心扩张对电力系统可持续性的双重影响：一方面，快速集中的负荷增长正超越清洁能源部署速度，加剧排放和电网可靠性挑战；另一方面，该资本密集型行业通过清洁能源整合和运营创新为可持续发展提供了重大机遇。文章从负荷特性、供电配置、短期运营与长期规划机制、企业碳核算实践等多个维度进行了系统分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心的快速扩张对电力系统可持续性产生了深远影响，但目前缺乏对这一问题的系统性综述。本文旨在整合现有知识和技术进展，阐明数据中心影响电力系统可持续性的机制，识别风险和机遇，为研究和实践提供指导。

Method: 本文采用综述性分析方法，从三个维度展开：(i) 刻画AI数据中心的负荷行为特征，按功能和可持续性对供电配置进行分类，并将其置于全球和区域电力需求趋势中；(ii) 分析短期运营和长期规划机制下的可持续性影响，评估电网碳排放、可再生能源利用、系统灵活性和辅助服务参与可行性；(iii) 评估企业可持续性路径，指出当前碳核算实践的系统效益和可行性局限。

Result: 文章揭示出：数据中心负荷的快速集中增长在许多地区已超过清洁能源部署速度，成为碳排放和电网可靠性的新压力源；同时，这一资本密集型行业的快速发展也为清洁能源整合、需求侧灵活性和运营创新提供了重大机遇。当前碳核算实践在系统层面存在可行性局限。

Conclusion: AI数据中心的可持续发展需要系统性方案，既要应对短期负荷增长带来的排放和可靠性挑战，也要抓住清洁能源整合和运营创新的长期机遇，同时现有碳核算方法需要改进以准确反映系统层面的影响。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of artificial intelligence (AI) has driven unprecedented growth in data center electricity demand. The scale and pace of this load growth carry significant implications for the sustainability of electric power systems. On the one hand, rapid, spatially concentrated data center load growth is outpacing clean energy deployment in several major regions, raising emissions and challenging both grid flexibility and reliability. On the other hand, this fast-developing and capital-intensive sector offers abundant opportunities to advance sustainability through clean energy integration and operational innovations. This article provides an overview of the mechanisms through which data center affect power system sustainability, underscoring both risks and the potential. Specifically, this article (i) characterizes AI data center load behavior and categorizes electricity supply configurations by function and sustainability profile, as well as situates these loads within global and regional electricity demand trends; (ii) analyzes sustainability impacts across short-run operational and long-run planning mechanisms, evaluates effects on grid carbon emissions and renewable energy utilization, and feasibility of offering system flexibility and participating in ancillary service; and (iii) evaluates real-world corporate sustainability pathways and highlighting both the system benefits and feasibility limits of current carbon accounting practices. The goal of this work is to synthesize existing knowledge and technological developments and to guide research and development toward a more sustainable integration of AI data centers and electric power systems.

</details>


### [193] [Discrete Geometric Modeling and Extended State Estimation of Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21205)
*Maximilian Herrmann,Leander Pfeiffer,Paul Kotyczka*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李群变分积分器的完全离散方法，用于连续体机器人的动力学建模与状态估计，并通过扩展卡尔曼滤波构建扰动观测器，实验验证了模型与观测器的精度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现连续体机器人高精度、高数值效率的动力学建模与状态估计，并有效处理模型不确定性和外部扰动。

Method: 采用基于几何精确梁的应变最小化公式，在李群变分积分器框架下推导完全离散模型；结合扩展卡尔曼滤波设计扰动观测器，同时估计系统状态、模型不确定性和外部扰动。

Result: 实际系统实验验证了所提模型和观测器的高精度与数值效率。

Conclusion: 该方法通过保持几何特性实现了高精度与高效率，扰动观测器能可靠估计状态及不确定性/扰动，适用于连续体机器人的实际应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a fully discrete approach for the accurate and numerically efficient dynamical modeling and state estimation of continuum robots. The model is based on geometrically exact beams in a minimal, strain-based formulation and derived in the framework of Lie group variational integrators, allowing to preserve important geometric properties that we exploit to achieve high accuracy and numerical efficiency. We then propose a disturbance observer based on an extended Kalman filter formulation that reliably estimates system states as well as model uncertainties and external disturbances. Experiments on a real system validate the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed model and observer.

</details>


### [194] [Local Conformity-Based Evolutionary Game Modeling of UAV Swarm Under Byzantine Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21206)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,He Fang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文基于图进化博弈理论，构建了恶意攻击下无人机集群的进化博弈模型，揭示了局部从众规则如何控制欺骗策略的传播，并推导了宏观动态方程与进化稳定状态的解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设无人机独立决策，忽略了局部从众行为会放大虚假信息传播的问题，亟需建模分析恶意攻击下无人机集群的策略演化机制。

Method: 基于图进化博弈理论构建无人机集群恶意攻击模型，采用死亡-出生更新规则，推导欺骗策略比例的宏观动态方程及其进化稳定状态的解析解。

Result: 观测误差会削弱恶意诱导效果；恶意节点比例越高、攻击强度越大，攻击影响越剧烈；模型在规则网络、无标度网络和随机网络上表现出强拓扑鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 局部从众规则是控制无人机集群中欺骗策略传播的关键因素，所提模型具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Leveraging their flexible and efficient deployment capabilities, unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms have been widely applied in various mission scenarios. However, the open communication environment also exposes them to the threat of Byzantine attacks. Most existing studies assume independent decision-making by each UAV, neglecting that local conformity amplifies false information propagation. This paper constructs an evolutionary game model for UAV swarm under malicious attacks based on graph evolutionary game theory, revealing how local conformity rules govern the spread of deceptive strategies. Using death-birth updating rules, we derive the macroscopic dynamic equation for the fraction of deceptive strategies and the analytical solutions to its evolutionary stable states. Sim ulations reveal observation errors weaken malicious induction, while higher proportions of malicious nodes and greater attack intensity drastically amplify attack impacts. Moreover, the model exhibits strong topological robustness across regular, scale-free and random networks.

</details>


### [195] [Reliability Assessment and Performance Enhancement of Reset Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21431)
*Ali Hosseini,Dragan Kostić,Hassan HosseinNia*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对复位控制系统，提出了一种频域可靠性评估框架。通过扩展闭环高阶正弦输入描述函数，显式纳入经整形滤波器生成的复位触发信号，并引入两个度量指标（复位时间偏差σ_t和额外零交叉倾向σ_d），来识别不可靠的复位行为。针对广义一阶复位元件提出了一种一阶整形滤波器，以改善复位触发的可靠性。在工业运动平台上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复位控制系统在实际应用中存在触发不可靠的问题，而现有方法无法有效捕捉这些可靠性隐患，需要一种面向设计者的量化评估手段。

Method: 1) 扩展闭环高阶正弦输入描述函数（HOSIDF），显式建模经整形滤波器产生的复位触发信号；2) 提出两个可靠性度量指标σ_t（复位时间偏差）和σ_d（额外零交叉倾向）；3) 针对广义一阶复位元件（GFORE）设计一阶整形滤波器，增强非零高阶谐波的低频衰减斜率。

Result: 在工业运动平台上的实验表明，所提出的度量指标能够捕捉到一阶闭环响应无法体现的可靠性问题，有效支持复位控制器的可靠性设计。

Conclusion: 该框架为复位控制系统的可靠性评估提供了设计导向的量化工具，所提出的整形滤波器能有效改善复位触发行为，提升了系统在实际工况下的可靠性。

Abstract: This paper develops a frequency-domain reliability assessment framework for reset control systems. The closed-loop higher-order sinusoidal-input describing function formulation is extended to explicitly include the reset-triggering signal generated through a shaping filter. Based on this signal, two metrics are introduced: \(σ_t\), which quantifies reset-time deviation, and \(σ_d\), which evaluates the tendency toward additional zero crossings. These metrics provide design-oriented indicators for identifying potentially unreliable reset behavior. To improve reset-triggering reliability, a first-order shaping filter is proposed for a generalized first-order reset element, increasing the low-frequency attenuation slope of the nonzero higher-order harmonics. The proposed analysis is evaluated on an industrial motion stage. The results show that the proposed metrics capture reliability issues that are not evident from the first-order closed-loop response alone and can therefore support the design of reset controllers with more reliable reset-triggering behavior.

</details>


### [196] [Revisiting the generalized first-order reset element with shaping filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21478)
*Ali Hosseini,Dragan Kostić,Hassan HosseinNia*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对复位控制中高阶谐波导致性能下降的问题，提出了一种基于成形滤波器的广义一阶复位元件系统框架。通过推导滤波器系数的解析条件，增强高阶正弦输入描述函数（HOSIDFs）的低频衰减斜率，在保留一阶谐波相位优势的同时抑制不希望的复位非线性。HOSIDF分析和叠加律实验验证了非线性行为的减弱，工业运动平台实验证明了跟踪性能提升和谐波含量降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复位控制虽能超越线性时不变控制器的性能限制，但复位动作会引入高阶谐波，导致跟踪性能下降、信号失真以及基于描述函数法的频域预测可靠性降低，亟需一种系统性的框架来抑制这些不希望的复位诱导非线性。

Method: 重新审视广义一阶复位元件与成形滤波器，推导成形滤波器系数的解析条件以增大高阶正弦输入描述函数（HOSIDFs）的低频衰减斜率，通过修改高阶谐波的渐近衰减行为实现更强的谐波抑制，同时保留交叉频率附近的一阶谐波相位优势。

Result: HOSIDF分析和叠加律测试验证了高阶成形滤波器使复位元件在特定频率范围内更接近线性系统行为；工业运动平台的实验证明跟踪性能改善、高阶谐波含量降低，且复位动作在目标频率区域被选择性激活。

Conclusion: 所提出的成形滤波框架能有效抑制复位控制中不期望的非线性行为，在保持复位控制相位优势的同时减少谐波失真，提升系统整体性能。

Abstract: Reset control provides a nonlinear approach for improving closed-loop performance beyond the limitations of linear time-invariant controllers. However, the reset action inevitably introduces higher-order harmonics, which may degrade tracking performance, distort the reset signal, and reduce the reliability of frequency-domain predictions obtained via describing-function analysis. This paper revisits the generalized first-order reset element with shaping filters and develops a systematic framework for suppressing undesired reset-induced nonlinearities. Analytical conditions are derived for shaping filter coefficients to increase the low-frequency attenuation slope of the magnitude of the higher-order sinusoidal input describing functions (HOSIDFs). By modifying the asymptotic attenuation behavior of these higher-order harmonics, the proposed design provides stronger harmonic suppression in frequency regions where reset action is undesired, while preserving the beneficial first-order harmonic phase advantage near the desired cross-over frequency. The reduction in nonlinear behavior is verified through HOSIDF analysis and a superposition-law test, demonstrating that higher-order shaping filters make the reset element behave more closely to a linear system at a certain range of frequencies. Experimental validation on an industrial motion stage demonstrates improved tracking performance, reduced higher-order harmonic content, and selective activation of the reset action in the intended frequency region.

</details>


### [197] [Stability Enhancement of Centralized UPS Data Center Systems Under Weak-Grid Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21536)
*Jesus D. Vasquez Plaza,Yonghao Gui,Jin Dong,Jamie Lian*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对数据中心集中式UPS在弱电网条件下（SCR≤2）整流器可能失稳的问题，提出了一种无需锁相环的功率控制策略，通过电磁暂态仿真验证了该方法能有效改善系统阻尼并恢复稳定运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电力系统演变及大动态负载接入使数据中心电源系统面临弱电网条件，传统基于PLL的PI控制策略在低短路比下性能退化甚至失稳，亟需增强整流器在弱电网下的稳定性。

Method: 采用功率控制方法应用于三相整流器，直接调控有功和无功功率，无需依赖PLL同步，通过电磁暂态仿真验证效果。

Result: 仿真表明，PI控制整流器在SCR=2时失稳；所提功率控制策略改善了系统阻尼，恢复了弱电网下的稳定运行。

Conclusion: 控制设计对于保障数据中心电源系统在低强度电网环境下的可靠运行至关重要，所提方法有效解决了弱电网下的稳定性问题。

Abstract: Data center power systems are increasingly exposed to weak-grid conditions due to the evolution of modern power systems and the integration of large and dynamic loads. In centralized uninterruptible power supply (UPS) architectures, the front-end rectifier plays a critical role in maintaining stable operation and ensuring reliable power delivery to information technology (IT) equipment. However, conventional phase-locked loop (PLL)-based proportional-integral (PI) control strategies may exhibit degraded performance or instability under low short-circuit ratio (SCR) conditions. This paper investigates the behavior of centralized UPS systems under weak-grid conditions and demonstrates, through electromagnetic transient simulations, that PI-controlled rectifiers can become unstable at SCR=2. To address this issue, a power-based control approach is applied to the three-phase rectifier, enabling direct regulation of active and reactive power without relying on PLL synchronization. Simulation results show that the proposed control strategy improves system damping and restores stable operation under weak-grid conditions. The findings highlight the importance of control design for maintaining reliable operation of data center power systems in emerging low-strength grid environments.

</details>


### [198] [Nonholonomic directional pursuit and evasion: Global feedbacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21742)
*Bo Wang,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对非完整运动体的定向追逃问题，提出了全局反馈控制律，无需初始视线角条件，通过积分输入-状态稳定性与有限时间协同消失引理，以已知对手最大转向率为代价，分别实现有限时间捕获与规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作仅能在有利初始视线条件下"半全局"地解决定向追逃问题，本文旨在从任意初始相对构型下实现全局化的追逃目标。

Method: 采用完全不同的反馈设计思路，设计更温和的前向速度与转向律；基于内源性时间膨胀引发的积分输入-状态稳定性机制，结合有限时间协同消失引理和安全裕度持久性引理，分析奇异耦合标量不等式。

Result: 追逐律保证有限时间内捕获并达成指定方向对齐；规避律保证避免捕获并维持安全裕度，在追捕者转向率满足衰减条件时实现spinaway；仿真验证了全局捕获与spinaway效果。

Conclusion: 以已知对手最大转向率为代价，实现了从任意初始状态出发的全局定向追逃，相比半全局工作更为温和但全局有效。

Abstract: In a recent paper by the second coauthor, directional pursuit-evasion for strictly forward-moving nonholonomic vehicles was solved "half-globally", namely under favorable initial line-of-sight conditions. In this paper, we develop feedback designs that achieve the directional pursuit and evasion objectives from arbitrary initial relative configurations. We achieve globality with completely different approaches to both the design and the analysis. Our designs are less aggressive in both the forward-speed and steering laws, allowing transient overshoot of the pursuer-evader range during global reorientation. The only price that we pay for globality is that our feedback laws require a priori knowledge of the opponent's maximal turning rate, whereas in the half-global work, no known bound of the opponent's turning rate was assumed. For the pursuit problem, the feedback law guarantees finite-time capture with prescribed directional alignment. For the evasion problem, the feedback law guarantees capture avoidance with a prescribed safety margin and achieves spinaway under a decay condition on the pursuer's turning rate. We illustrate the global capture and spinaway with simulations. The analysis is based on an integral input-to-state stability type mechanism induced by an endogenous time dilation, together with finite-time coextinction and safety-margin persistence lemmas for singularly coupled scalar inequalities.

</details>


### [199] [Inference as Flexibility: Ramp Management for Transmission-Connected AI Data Centres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21833)
*Zhirui Liang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型推理服务的灵活性作为数据中心内部的可调节资源，与电池储能系统协同，以缓解AI训练功率波动对电网的冲击。仿真结果表明，混合策略可减少71%的电池放电能量和51%的峰值放电功率，同时满足10 MW/min的爬坡限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型AI数据中心的快速增长给电力系统带来了新的运行挑战，如快速爬坡、振荡负荷行为、电压波动和供需平衡问题。现有缓解措施主要依赖电池储能等外部电气资源，成本较高，亟需探索数据中心内部自身的灵活性资源。

Method: 本文提出一种软件定义的灵活性缓解层，利用大语言模型推理服务的批处理大小调节作为数据中心的内部可调节负荷。以150 MW传输连接的数据中心为对象，包含训练、推理和基荷三部分。使用实测的LLaMA-2-70B微调功率曲线和LLaMA-3.1-70B推理功率轨迹，比较了三种策略：纯电池储能、仅批处理大小控制、批处理大小与电池储能协调控制。

Result: 混合策略（批处理大小+电池储能协调控制）相比纯电池储能方案，减少了71%的电池放电能量和51%的峰值放电功率，同时几乎完全满足10 MW/min的爬坡限制。

Conclusion: 利用LLM推理服务的批处理大小调节作为数据中心的内部灵活性资源，可以有效替代部分电池储能容量，为数据中心参与电网需求响应和辅助服务提供了一种经济高效的软件定义方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large AI data centres introduces new operational challenges for power systems, including rapid ramping, oscillatory load behavior, voltage fluctuations, and supply-demand balancing impacts. For example, the Alberta Electric System Operator (AESO) has identified transmission-connected data centres (TCDCs) as large non-conforming loads that may need to limit their point-of-connection ramp rates. Existing mitigation approaches mainly rely on exogenous electrical resources, such as battery energy storage systems (BESS). This paper presents a proof-of-concept demonstration of a complementary software-defined mitigation layer: using flexible large language model (LLM) inference serving as endogenous TCDC flexibility to partially offset AI training power ramps. We consider a 150 MW TCDC with training, inference, and base-load components. A measured LLaMA-2-70B fine-tuning power profile is scaled to represent an aggregate training block, while measured LLaMA-3.1-70B inference power traces are used to model batch-size-dependent inference flexibility. Three strategies are compared: BESS-only mitigation, batch-size-only control, and coordinated batch-size plus BESS control. Simulation results show that the hybrid strategy reduces BESS discharge energy by 71% and peak discharge power by 51%, while maintaining near-complete compliance with a 10 MW/min ramp limit.

</details>


### [200] [DC Link Capacitor Ripple Constraints Limit the Benefits of Utility-Owned Four-Wire Power Converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21934)
*Matthew Deakin,Xu Deng,Shafiq Odhano,Rahmat Heidari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了直流母线电容的热限制如何影响电压源换流器在不平衡运行下的性能，发现电容热约束会显著削弱换流器释放网络资产裕度的能力。通过对比九种不同纹波处理能力的换流器拓扑，在相电流不平衡缓解应用中，具有纹波能力的拓扑相比无纹波情况可多释放80%以上的裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力公司希望通过功率换流器主动控制网络潮流来增加资产裕度，但直流母线电容的热限制可能影响这一目标的实现，因此需要量化评估不同拓扑在不平衡运行下的实际增益。

Method: 研究了九种电压源换流器拓扑，对比其纹波处理能力，分析在中性电流和倍频功率脉动约束下直流母线电容热限制对换流器性能的影响，以相电流不平衡缓解为例计算可释放的额外裕度上限。

Result: 相比无纹波情况，具有纹波能力的拓扑可释放的额外裕度上限增加超过80%。

Conclusion: 直流母线电容的热限制是换流器在不平衡运行下释放资产裕度的关键约束，选择具有适当纹波处理能力的拓扑可显著提升性能。

Abstract: Utilities are increasingly interested in power converters to increase the headroom of their assets by actively controlling power flows on their network. In this work we demonstrate that thermal limits of dc link capacitors can result in substantially diminished benefits of these converters under unbalanced operation, due to constraints on neutral current and double-line frequency power ripple. Considering nine voltage source converter topologies with varying ripple capabilities, the upper bound (in terms of additional headroom released) increases by more than 80% compared to a no-ripple case for the application of phase current unbalance mitigation.

</details>


### [201] [Joint Visibility Analysis of RIS in Non-Terrestrial Networks through Stochastic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21946)
*Ashutosh Balakrishnan,Junse Lee,Francois Baccelli*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了城市环境中，在建筑屋顶部署可重构智能超表面（RIS）以辅助非地面网络（NTN）通信的场景，重点分析了RIS从用户设备（UE）和NTN基站（NTN-BS）的联合视距（LoS）可见性概率。在RIS部署位置和建筑高度双重随机性下，推导了联合可见RIS数量的期望闭式解，发现在二维场景中无条件最大期望值为两倍的巴塞尔数（π²/6）。仿真分析了建筑密度、平均建筑高度、NTN-BS高度和位置等参数的影响，并展示了概率热力图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市高层建筑容易阻挡用户设备与NTN基站之间的视距路径，需要探索通过RIS实现多跳连接来保证通信质量。为此，需要统计研究RIS从UE和NTN-BS的联合可见性，以便为RIS部署规划提供理论依据。

Method: 将RIS部署的建筑位置建模为泊松点过程（PPP），建筑高度设为指数分布，在二维场景中考虑NTN-BS位置确定，分别研究无条件及以UE-NTN链路状态（LoS/non-LoS）为条件的联合RIS可见性统计特性，推导出联合可见RIS数量的期望闭式解。

Result: 二维场景中，无条件情况下最大期望联合可见RIS数量为两倍的巴塞尔数（π²/6）。仿真分析了建筑密度、平均建筑高度、NTN-BS高度和位置等参数对联合可见性的影响，并生成了条件概率热力图。

Conclusion: 该研究对于城市区域RIS部署规划具有指导意义，可用于改善信号质量和评估经济成本。

Abstract: Non-Terrestrial networks (NTNs) are a key theme in upcoming 6G communications, especially for ubiquitous coverage. Urban environments, comprising of high rise buildings often result in blocking the line of sight (LoS) path between the user equipment (UE) and the NTN base station (NTN-BS). In this paper we investigate the situation where reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) are deployed on the building roof-tops to ensure multi-hop connectivity between the UE and the NTN-BS. In such a scenario, it becomes crucial to statistically study the LoS visibility of the RIS from the UE as well as from the NTN-BS, hence termed as joint visibility. In this work, accounting for the dual stochasticity arising from the locations of the RIS deployed buildings and the respective random building heights, we statistically study the probability of joint RIS visibility in a two-dimensional (2D) scenario considering a deterministic location of the NTN-BS. Further, we study the joint RIS visibility statistics conditional on the UE-NTN link being LoS or non-LoS. For the RISs deployed as a point point process (PPP) having exponentially distributed heights, the expected RISs jointly visible under the unconditional and conditional geometric settings are derived in closed form. Interestingly, in the 2D setting, the maximum expected RISs jointly visible, unconditionally, is twice the Basel number $(π^2/ 6)$. The simulated results are analyzed over building density, average building height, the altitude and position of the NTN-BS. We also illustrate probability heatmaps, demonstrating the strongest chance to have a RIS used conditioned on the system geometry. This study is expected to be useful in planning the deployment of RIS in urban areas, improving the signal and for assessing economic aspects.

</details>


### [202] [Uncertainty-Disentangled Probabilistic Stability Analysis in Wind Power Integrated Weak Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21984)
*Samson S. Yu,Yinsong Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解缠多项式混沌展开（d-PCE）方法，将二阶可再生能源预测通过模态稳定映射传播，从而在闭合形式下精确分离概率小信号稳定性分析中的偶然不确定性（不可约的天气随机性）和认知不确定性（可约的预测模型不确定性）。基于此分离，论文构建了一个预测感知的(α,β,γ)稳定性证书，其保守分支以O(N^{-1/2})速度收敛至不可约的偶然极限，使失效证书具有诊断意义：认知主导的风险可通过改进数据恢复；偶然主导的风险需改进物理控制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率小信号稳定性分析仅传播单一预测分布，混淆了不可约的天气随机性（偶然不确定性）与可约的预测模型不确定性（认知不确定性），无法区分风险来源，限制了系统优化方向。

Method: 通过独立germ变量传播二阶可再生能源预测，采用解缠多项式混沌展开（d-PCE）在闭合形式下精确分离偶然不确定性和认知不确定性。

Result: 构建了预测感知的(α,β,γ)稳定性证书，其保守分支以O(N^{-1/2})速度收敛至不可约的偶然极限。

Conclusion: 失效证书具有诊断意义：若风险由认知不确定性主导，可通过改善数据质量恢复系统稳定性；若由偶然不确定性主导，则需改进物理控制系统。

Abstract: Conventional probabilistic small-signal stability analysis (PSSSA) propagates a single forecast distribution, conflating irreducible weather randomness (aleatoric) with reducible forecast-model uncertainty (epistemic). This letter propagates a second-order renewable forecast through the modal-stability map via an independent \emph{germ} variable, separating the two contributions exactly in closed form by a disentangled polynomial chaos expansion (d-PCE). The split underpins a forecast-aware $(α,β,γ)$ stability certificate whose conservative branch converges to its irreducible aleatoric limit at $O(N^{-1/2})$ -- making a failed certificate diagnostic: epistemic-dominated risk recovers with better data; aleatoric-dominated risk needs improvements of the physical control system.

</details>


### [203] [TSO-DSO Coordination for Flexibility Management Across Voltage Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21998)
*Gustavo Valverde,Anibal Sanjab,Florin Capitanescu,Christian Rehtanz,Gianluigi Migliavacca,Zhengshuo Li,Innocent Kamwa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了输电和配电网中灵活性资源的协调问题，分析了从技术、市场和监管角度实现协调的挑战与趋势，并提出了当前实践、已有方法和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ICT技术、聚合商和灵活性市场的发展，输配电网中的灵活性资源被逐步解锁，但如何最大化协调效益、实现输配系统运营商之间的协同，仍缺乏统一的方法论，亟需新的算法、建模工具和监管框架改革。

Method: 本文采用综述（survey）方法，从技术、市场和监管三个视角，系统梳理了输配电网协调的复杂性，包括不确定性处理、物理与运行约束、信息交换约束、可扩展性等关键问题。

Result: 发现不同地区和司法管辖区没有统一的协调方案，但存在共同趋势和挑战；归纳了当前实践与已有方法，指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 输配电网灵活性协调需要在算法、建模、信息和监管多个维度持续创新，未来应聚焦于跨电压等级的协调机制设计与可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Several sources of flexibility in transmission and, especially, distribution networks are being unlocked by advances in information and communication technologies, aggregators, and new flexibility markets. However, maximizing benefits for both transmission and distribution system operators in a coordinated way requires new algorithms, modeling tools, and modernization of regulatory frameworks.
